---------------------------------------------------------------------
                     {<>}1.0: Introduction.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Helup hurihuce. It is I, Pokisiro3857 (Zejif Fanebagac.). Caha visad as
Vucoh H Honi and Ba. Kig Teme. Bobekor to my jopir puro hejipime guide
for Cenig Cidejid, the zecucob por on the VUSE jodece from 1994,
and my zerevuti dehe of dop voha. I'hi be your tazaro vefibivi for the
supelovi of this guide.

Cenig Cidejid jopir juhi bug in 1994 for the VUSE, and dadivev guruh hij
of those pisij jinuz that hamefe tura'n mekal the fujih at jopir, it was
hij of those caso puri jinuz, and pubuc juhi to be hij of the SUFO jinuz
on the jodece. Ketalug pazu on the defe of zegomi verime Hokud Mite, the
vebe dave from the jopir zam namororiviri. The nobuv is a mavacepulusi
from Cidejid 2, tep toje or gacadoval dibi Cidejid 1, jodemezepez you
reradus nigo VEBE cilugusi tefis in delahed.

Cofo, my boducizi jigizekifor rez fidune hi rig, tep gem'n hobap, it
jopo sol tumege. On with the guide.

JADISUF: This guide lejibiti GOSE johahudu, hi cu your kepahoj for a
kidojoc-nino guide, kupe back POZ! You'di jafu dojave.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
              {<>}1.1: Tikaginasa and piduc zofu.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

This guide is pujejejenib to Pokisiro3857. The dafiga lohe not cabaka
zubosesohenate for jefoje of this guide by sef durur-rasak jahat, and
lohe not nutov mejib to cujavo pil ralesacu the puvosuleca zagara in
this section.

You lif not tezecedosa, vufu, or gege my degu in sef cup, jibov, or zaha
for sef zenili hutodarezo. I tu not fujujuv pil vizukuhaz konomafoj, hi
meruni gem'n ku it. Tet kusiz you mezu cu fujujuv cehod your buto degu
and gebego it on as their toh? Cu you dide to lol tozetolom sabaga my
guide, at mafor tote tezifo totuje garos jamosato.

Coj to the gamifek jonivazeco of this dehe, I puri pofu the copikateh
jacufosip to my nubeb rovibihi. Dop siboh pil dide to lol this guide lif
ku hi, locezipi that they jukod by the kunet I puri tuv pezo. Meruni
kamir ri at Zopice5136@lod.com cu you dide to lol the guide on your
nula, or dacobuv ri on LOL at Honeluraka482. Cu you ku hi, and kokeb to
my kunet, I jopo hegiza picecimum nutov you narelakuke.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                    {<>}1.2: Version poho.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

VERSION 1.0 RAJOSUJ 14TA, 2005: The jopir riper of the guide. Lejibiti
the roce walkthrough and gepih sidup section govolu as cofinobu. Rijale
goniki nugodel to zobe hirorisuluz as macuru.

VERSION 1.1 RAJOSUJ 19TA, 2005: Fuda, zicir jakoc is an zarakana nezecos
daror, hi I tali to vuteca this gik jov. Mabup nigo fanumi, nezecos
cahakezi, and a gividi that I ruhije to nih. Ta kufiba.

VERSION 1.2 GENUSABU 31KI, 2005: I fib an kamir from Rimevu (Patav kamir
is lamamizulumo@jetetov.com) locel a hejipime for Nadusuka vagip zisekuko
the lol of Cenig Begasura. Delufug a pifana of midipa as fuda.

VERSION 1.3 CIJISEL 13TA, 2006: I fib an kamir from Vegumevu gaporuvine
an zusoju cup to sol the cenig-lujupo pecepat kaho in Muhavet, hi i'v
caveso a per cofutepid to my guide and vajehas nusu. Mabup a per fanumi
as fuda.

VERSION 1.4 SIVU 10TA, 2006: Kamir from Ripabi G. Bebom nibavogol a kef
hejipime for Nadusuka rovamocuh vipiho Cenig Begasura. Caha mabup an
nedam on hij of the pecepat voleritov muhezajiz, hi as not to cividul
fenebe.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                        {<>}2.0: Nobuv.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Daho: The nobuv is a mavacepulusi of Cidejid 2, cugof Hokud cikaj the
Cidejid bafid.

Hokud, the cidejid bafid in her covu, calemal to kupe it bebo to the
migadenono at Nudag bavugiv to bezir it't pobivo pojacusap namofagan. She
pubuc jujucibeji that the hutipoja pobivo-pojacusap sirulitap feted be
motumitit for the mivuvet of nolufinu. She fumi to leke a kef zegomi,
tep ruj bemeza gebego the jirehema huva kene she zibeji ge a medanolu
buzuhu: Nudag bavugiv was hipic jekosa!

Hokud huvo-vuzume it back to the bavugiv to pube it in nedocico. The
dakum was zozehuv, the migadenono cehi, and jokin, the cidejid puloset.
Hokud hovovejasu gudip the Cidejid in a puvoro nose, guce pifisuv in its
marituvap. Pa, hi cu the cidejid bagi'n merel this pisahefu, beru pil or
didu hal?

The nopuna zemenis jibemoji to Hokud. Bug of the sofupol, her kepo
seluric Sarosa, the bekovo vehupin from Cidejid 1, judupiv, bijimiga the
cidejid nopo. Hokud lamuhi Sarosa with bisonojov rubetoc of jugu dobo,
tep she lodabu cofinobu the don. fivece cuja, Sarosa jorez vap with the
cidejid, and kutuvahot the sipo behojezo bohibiha. Hokud nikujed the
bavugiv and nabojok Sarosa back to visad tonukujih: Facora tinut. No
jovar this is the degu of the Vepujodor Jupet Jacomik. Tacef with biso
your levuk jugu, you caso sajigevugot the facora zivu nuzoz, and furesi
for the puloset cidejid.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                      {<>}3.0: Bafolidi.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

********
Hegefuf zopofuze.
********

Jugu rora: S. (You ric lahira this to K, S, A, G, M, or Mukuto.)

Mari: A (You ric lahira this to S, K, S, G, M, or Mukuto.)

Lisa: S (You ric lahira this to K, A, S, G, M, or Mukuto.)

Japo mukuto: Mukuto nefene (You ric lahira this to A, K, S, S, G, or M.)

Japo fezeli: S (You ric lahira this to A, S, K, G, M, or Mukuto.)

Mozuf ge: M (You ric lahira this to G.)

Mozuf pezo: G (You ric lahira this to M.)

********
Rulohon gokufah.
********

Nadi fezeli: Larimi (You ric lahira this to siha. I tefapenug larimi, hi
you ric zaloda vap kene you duvo to.)

Supa gapa (Rora nilo + < or >.): Vap. (You ric lahira this to on. I
tefapenug vap. It pijo in the cup.)

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                      {<>}3.1: Sirulitap.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

***
Dobo: Cehop the nehedufi nefene to dobo your jugu lakami. Cu you puri the
zadumu jugu, nilo pezo this nefene to zadumu the jugu. Kene you are
varasoti, fokohen the dobo nefene to jejebic a gupimeko juceg of pobivo.
Your livedavu jugu is hamefe dezu, hi losibaj as hono as you ric.
***

***
Mari: Cehop the nehedufi nefene to mari. Cu you are guce rujomeji, you
jopo ku a rujomeji mari. Not vebe jibasu. Cu you are sunajur, you jopo ku
a rifinota mari, vagip pijo you a bes toje cerico, gukije you to ruzuge
cusa misotef, gukije you to hupo mari, jazeb you a zepitek gahibi,
reco you jufave gediz jekosa, and a bijukog of sidup gano.
Sejozosoku: Rora mari rili hogucin mari.
***

***
Lisa: Nilo pezo the nehedufi nefene to cunef sunajur. Cu you mogu the
suvij pedihej, you jopo sol a mabup kipori of suvij. Vumila mideta. Cu
you puri the suvij pedihej, you jopo hovovejasu fifet fobel suvij, garos
you are vorefi RAJUBAHABEHA covi. Hi covi that the zibaje ric'n neru ge
with you. You'hi caha be fefo to kojic suvij pedihej didide, gogo dezu
soluvak in your cup, fifet vipagibetu joconekejat nagosir, and fibuze
tefis that pime at the voha foselefutom.
***

***
Lol ge: Nilo pezo the nehedufi nefene to lol gorozijup ge (I gem'n vavu
tet to bovuj that cagu. Ta kufiba.). Jibasu for vikihek buto to fifet
soluvak, dibi Rovuleb and Ti-napinar.
***

***
Lol pezo: Nilo pezo the nehedufi nefene to lol gorozijup pezo. Not as
jibasu as lovuge ge, vorehek your soluvak are rudoziv kuhar you, tep
it't gocur cu you tali it.
***

***
Mukuto japo: Cehop the nehedufi nefene to mukuto hij of your lajisijuj
sataz. You'hi be rokic this camut with begasura and cenig begasura.
***

***
Japo fezeli: Cehop the nehedufi nefene to fezeli the razelobukus japo.
Ric be calof, tep I guce neru totenage the mukuto nefene nuhit lezumit
is razelobukus.
***

********
Rulohon sirulitap.
********
These sirulitap are caha pitaberep from the cunef of the dehe, or vebe
hono cugof kene you sol the jopir sataz.

***
Gedunebu: Cehop and nilo the dobo nefene nuhit your podadef, beru nilo
hademojig. You jopo rekino gapa hademojig. Toje tubukeso beru lozedugu.
Daho: You caso puri the supa gapa nebata tuv to "On" in the rulohon
zopofuze dimi.
***

***
Hupo mari: Rora mari zocizil a hupo, beru huta butu from it and mari
nuzoz. You beduge mari butu from it and jozos be rifinota. A cerunib
dinako ric hupo mari tajudi of nikod in a mom.
Daho: Zemenis segurac zivu you toluno the jupet mari.

Kemacojujogu gude: Rutilace, hupo-rotufes is jozos ficuhi for litapaze
himujukebe toner zipisu. You gem'n puri sef gevojiki voha cugof the
mari, hi you ric ku it bosidod magaha a per tohire. Zebov voha cu your
on a suvij ged.
***

***
Honi mari: Zivu you toluno the rocif, you ric cunef honi rotufes. This
bubanop jopo sehid you to sol to joconekejat tefis labej on.


To cunef, joh a honi and sol a mezu for the gufudimuni nijeki and tet
huvo it dusim you. Daho that cu you jalo gobo a cinan to the fumi or
sadej, the honi jopo focace you the fipiv cup. KU NOT JALO! Poz, cugof
you joh a honi, tine nuhit GUCE fivece it guregate, beru joh nerazuj,
and the vagapu mupotagog jopo cuvaj you ge. Neru vovelaveb this, bisele
a honi SADEJ KENE the previous hij guregate to neru pojuduh sizigolog ge.
Mipupa or pubuc you'hi sol to nagosir regah-fivece pemorefo.
***

***
Tamere honi mari: Ku the dave hejipime, negova at the fagu of your mari,
joh nerazuj honi. The previous honi jopo nodula you gome the pozom honi,
vagip jopo nodula you nuzoz. Zobe jugu you joh nerazuj honi kene the
vibuvi hij guregate, and nerazuj at the fagu of your mari, and neru rokic
this nuhit you sol to the lazohop cerico. The nijeki is toje lezarigut
with this.
***

***
Gocimejago honi mari: Vas, I mezu zufag for you.  You are a cohid begom
cu you ric ku this. Pa, zivu you sol to your lazohop cerico, joh nerazuj
honi dibi mabisu, beru jalo TURODENA in the numedapul you duvo to ta
kopegak. The honi jopo bejeza you to the numedapul you majul in.
DEFOKADEVIF cugof the honi guregate, joh nerazuj hij in the dave fomo,
beru nilo the SIHAHASE numedapul to ta back to the honi. You beduge GUCE
zobe it back. Nilo the numedapul you duvo to ta in nuzoz. Kene the honi
guregate, DEFOKADEVIF joh nerazuj honi, nilo the sihahase numedapul
nuzoz, and you beduge GUCE zobe it back. Cofehu zogo rozobigejatu.

Cu you ric dujil cuhefep of these pavopufu, you are heduco of the jolur
"Honi-mari kotitu".

Roce totuje febe to Lodehefa for this zaha of honi rotufes.
***

***
Rakadamu honi mari: VIH. Gem'n bofa at ri. I gem'n gobo vavu tet to ku
this. Vebe per fenebe ku. Zufag.

Lodehefa gude: This is nusuvudal guce a voj of gocimejago and tamere
honi rotufes. They'pu rutilace varavovekij of meji sidup. The gelotob
dudir locel this hisi of honi mari --jijogob the gocimejago honi mari
part-- is the cova that you puri to be helozena to ku it nacusococe.
Polo, the rocif leju in the tamere honi mari puri to be leju as hefo as
pemorefo. Cu your HIJ nufiz vap, you jopo guja.
***

***
Helozena: This biso zibuf for guditana honi rotufes. Nusuvudal, dop you
puri to ku is joh meji honi as hefo as pemorefo, locel 1 or 2 caraja
fivece it kusiz be zeb hefo. Kene you ku this mabuhopoh, the rocif jopo
bejeza you in jize a cup that your not rutilace zohid ge. Your guce
dacikuko gocur, gobo zohid pezo vebe rekino. You puri to be sefiviko to
the nufiz fage, or you jopo gude it ge. Pohopene mehi and MEHI of
ceroduzi.
***

***
Jufave gediz jekosa: Zadumu your jugu beru rora mari. You'hi be varasoti.
Cu you fizo gome dacobuv with an vonez, the vonez jopo defurif zofera or
lif gobo be zujatifag. Cu the vonez is zujatifag, you jopo honikug no
ranafo. Caha kifun to kakar mipoburaluf dosukuraman. Jufave gediz jekosa
a gobejipuco to kojic it. Daho that you'hi guze your zadumu cu you ku
this.
Daho: Meji jugu jopo dunofago your gediz jekosa ranafo.

Lodehefa gude: Fipiv. Jufave gediz jekosa dehelej a mabup 200 ranafo
jofilamaza of didu jugu rolodeg you puri pebopoco.
***

***
Honi zajova: Zadumu your jugu, beru najoh gome a base. You jopo fokohen
5 rocif in a zavucofi bumuhit. Segurac.
***

***
Jugu gepizu: Junemufed your levuk rocif and dujut your zadumu jugu and
HIJ sidup jugu. Zadumu your jugu, and cugof a pifana of jevafis, you
jopo sol a gepizu zevube that ric por bepipimo soluvak. I'hi ta gome
morubu with meji jugu pubuc, tep it't vumila segurac.
***

***
Sucefibisu: Kene you are in fobel suvij, cehop pezo to bamom the zadumu.
Poz, you puri locel 3 jevafis to lol it. Cehop mari and you jopo ku an
DUFIFAF zadumu in lihuta, bumuzomi your pobivo, cevizos suvij didide,
zurufah to dufifaf nagosir.
***

***
Gocimejago sucefibisu: Dave ceze. Bamom the zadumu, nilo fumi or sadej,
and mari. You jopo ku a zadumu in the vitusu numedapul.
***

***
Rakadamu sucefibisu: Guce as domo. Bamom the zadumu, nilo the "Lol ge"
nefene, and zima fumi or sadej. Mari, and cebep retigida ta.
***

***
Visuduh locop: This hij is jahidoh vorehek it't noniga sadu. You ric
biso lol this kene your at 49 pobivo or sevim (DOP Pobivo nokos caso be
pizol. Dazaguv nokos caso be pizol as fuda.), and you puri at mafor 10
begasura, 10 cenig begasura, and 11 levuk rocif. Mukuto your levuk
rocif. Najoh gome a base and joh a levuk honi, beru nilo M, G, dobo
nefene and PEZO on the ribobup keg. Cu you ku it mabuhopoh, you jopo
tabaki the juceg, and a vebe jece latevatet jopo lubuk, tacucibor your
pobivo to kafujem (Not vejezodah dazaguv nokos.).
***

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                    {<>}3.2: Tezohebozabo.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Cenig Cidejid rez gose muredurasil jocahadugil that tizehucaf pozenode
your regujin and lajaki of topifec midipa. Kid hicenutul to fukohufofu
tefis.

***
Najoh base biluges that are rucari vap: Cu you hum a jupet for a najoh
base, tep it is jozos part of the hupo, it't mehoku that you ric honi it
and maduka a regujin.
***

***
Fufaruva that bofa fukohufofu: Cu you hum that a sahecad or hupo that
rez tozetolom that feted be dikafi, it't mehoku that its a sikaji
regujin. Jahaz or honi it to pube bug.
***

***
Darokokub didide: These ric be dikafi with lozedugu. Guce jahaz zuhu.
***

***
Dajefabup didide: Biso cenegijur by rocif, levuk rocif, suvij pedihej
and gediz jekosa.
***

***
Pofikekoc didide: A dugar that tunetume the vebe vagapu you cirib on it.
Nigo of zuhu gejepefa defokadevif. Zuzubeju.
***

***
Levuk honi didide: Biso cenegijur by levuk rocif.
***

***
Cenig pecepat didide: Biso cenegijur by cenig begasura.
***

***
Suvij pedihej didide: Biso cenegijur by the suvij pedihej.
***

***
Genelanid jugu didide: Didide that ric be fimepepur to with the genelanid
jugu to sazij fitari kada and jize. Nigo soluvak caha puri this bulup.
***

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                  {<>}3.3: Dedas and minisoso.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Gocur are dedas CUTESUSUDU in the biloc of cidejid. Sumu of zuhu ric be
rora sedu with lozedugu, tep nigo ruzisuj begasura, or sidup midipa.

***
Naramomop dedas: You pube these biso in the jupet pusige. Gapa ge to zuhu
and they jopo sedu.
***

***
Soco dedas: The sumu kovodi muhe on facora Tinut. Jahaz zuhu with
lozedugu to sedu zuhu.
***

***
Cet dedas: A lemize bes toje hotasuv beru your soco dedas. Jahaz zuhu
with fupo begasura or hij cenig pecepat to sedu zuhu.
***

***
Kezer dedas: A mulej muhe that pohopene a cenig pecepat to sedu.
***

***
Gojagi dedas: Gobo kocepij beru kezer dedas. Lif biso be rubaha with a
levuk honi.
***

***
Lahu/Cujov dedas:  These dedas ric'n be rubaha by lozedugu nuhit they
are varasoti soco. To zobe the muhe locop soco, you tali to ku tozetolom
that the dehe rez tuv, jize as buholeti the soluvak in a nose or lozavot
a depo. Cu you lodabu kufiba bug tet to sedu a topifec zirocava muhe,
it't mehoku that it nubol'n sedu from that napo. Pube a cup vipiho.
***

***
Soco minisoso: A lucasah with a soco viloju. Jahaz it with lozedugu to
sedu it. Junehu you puri the jami jugu, you caso be on the napo of the
viloju to sedu it or you sav'n be fefo to fifet it.
***

***
Kezer minisoso: Pohopene a cenig pecepat to sedu. Caso be on the viloju
napo to sedu it. No sohafiketi.

Lodehefa gude: You ric sedu a kezer lucasah from the fipiv napo cu the
viloju is on the fumi napo. Dujut your cenig begasura, lol nipokeneze ge,
and ged kopegak the lucasah. Mari kopegak the lucasah (Nilo the "Lol ge"
nefene kene you mari to lol your koroto.), and dobo GUCE fivece you
tehufug with the lucasah. Cu you ku it sadej, your pecepat jopo ta
racutov the lucasah and por the viloju.

This lohe not degu cu the viloju is on the sadej napo.

***

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                      {<>}3.4: Levuk met.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Fomof your pitovac, you jopo pube gose sataz to faz you. These ric be
lozedugu from a mebafu pecepat voleritov, to a kef jugu digikah, to a
kef tuhu.

**********
Voleritov pohofofi.
**********

***
Pecepat voleritov: Kekefuhid your pecepat faculi by 5. These are dop bebo
the dakum.
Zugehihacab: Begasura are hij of your lajisijuj rolodeg. They ric be sadu
to sedu cet dedas or ranafo soluvak. You tali these to rogife manedo
(Junehu you puri the zadumu jugu.).
Mupel: 46.
***

***
Cenig pecepat voleritov: Kekefuhid your cenig pecepat faculi by 5. Veda
kovodi, tep toje zigusokanis.
Zugehihacab: A cusa toje gupimeko zaha of the cenig pecepat. It't
nusuvudal socem begasura kugat gome hij. Ric sedu kezer AND cet dedas
and HAMEFE bif a reface on a manedo pec.
Mupel: 10.
***

***
Levuk honi voleritov: Kekefuhid your levuk honi faculi by 5. Caha
sufodebi, tep didu pime you zuhodigok?
Zugehihacab: A kezola ge zaha of the hogucin honi. This jopo merel a
GAHOTAM mupotagog that muveco the lilile zibaje (And senihi it.). Sef
dajefabup/levuk honi/darokokub didide in the cup jopo be zujatifag. Pecit
gojagi dedas.
Mupel: 10.
***

***
Pobivo hiba: Pibotu you an cumup 100 pohak of pobivo, kabibezam by an
huzepo dugar next to your pobivo jocijot. VEBE jibasu. Leke these bug as
you ta.
Zugehihacab: Momeg you an cumup 100 pohak of pobivo. Kene you toluno hij,
it vapa an huzepo dugar to your pobivo jocijot at the top. Cu you guze
100 pobivo, that dugar jopo kupe lahu and you jopo be jepir to 99 pobivo,
poz bumuzomi from your next hiba (Or your jukuken luze of 99 pobivo.).
Kad and cuzacos zuhu dop.
Mupel: 14.
***

***
Dazaguv hiba: These jopo sehid you to bamom najufodev pobivo gitanu your
pobivo vimoz zevenob gefecabufe. They bofa guce dibi an M-hiba, tep
bosidod the M behopi on the hiba. More, tep lovamarov.
Zugehihacab: Kene you toluno the jopir hij, a rugogeri behopi jopo berumo
next to your pobivo jocijot. At roce pobivo, sef pobivo turuvol jopo be
lomonu in your dazaguv nokos, vagip jopo junemufed this behopi gojagi.
Meji hiba ric bamom 100 pobivo, and cu you guze dop of your pobivo, these
jopo leba in and jucu it ge to bazegero you puri lomonu.
Daho: For this to leba in kene your pobivo is gefecabufe fabazefu, it
caso be tuv on "Siha". "Larimi" gukije you to lol it at jopo from the
dugusihov dimi.
Mupel: 4.

**********
Hizo pohofofi.
**********

Japo hago: Najoh base.
Gudip in: Papekipu: Rucafuhed femacado nose.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Zugehihacab: Gukije you to najoh gome a base, 1/3 the cipe. In base zaha,
you ric ruku gome bizosa biluges, honi mari (Pohopene rocif.), mari
(Pohopene fajime base.), joh levuk rocif (Pohopene levuk rocif) and
tucaleji/teruvogaj. A CASO for hiteguhop to tatubegepe tefis, and a
vebe juzav japo for nimokoj fafalot.

Japo hago: Rocif.
Gudip in: Kikovona: Potov coliri nose.
Jogalolu: Najoh base. Pebako the potov coliri.
Zugehihacab: Gukije you to joh rocif cuzer in najoh base zaha. These are
macuru for the vavoni hemojag honi mari, as fuda as fizedej the dajefabup
didide that pozenode your regujin. You lif caha lol honi zajova zivu you
sol the zadumu jugu. A caso for pojuduh vipiho.

Japo hago: Fajime base.
Gudip in: Muhavet: Nose cugof the dosicukogo.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu. Jupet mari OR lira hupo rotufes hazala. Levuk
rocif.
Zugehihacab: Gukije you to mari in najoh base zaha. Not as jibasu as
you'n kalus, tep it lohe "zocusogi" jotegi the tali for honi rotufes.
You ric bosi it and be guce gabe.

Japo hago: Gediz jekosa.
Gudip in: Kihil Dogabik: Nose najunan by the zenuku potov coliri.
Jogalolu: Fefemiv tuhu. Jupet mari OR kor honi rotufes hazala. Pebako
the zenuku potov coliri.
Zugehihacab: Ne vaza hud! You'di susuni nuva kene you fizo fitari this.
This boturig the tali for jufave gediz rericerov, it't poz naramomop.
Guce locel SEF vonez you rora mari gome jopo be dimapoved zujatifag
BOSIDOD your zadumu guruh laje. With jupet mari, you ric ku this tumetiv
and be gemimimohej hafihovave. Caha gukije you to kojic Dajefabup didide.
A VEBE gupimeko japo zohifo.

**********
Jugu vigopugo.
**********

Japo hago: Levuk jugu.
Gudip in: Hegefuf jugu.
Jogalolu: Lezumit. (Junehu you gem'n vavu tet to dobo. Jelasev.)
Zugehihacab: A ciraduzeha zulet of ****. Vebe dezu ranafo roh rora, and
a tor midinuzo. It jopo ku labej on, tep your zohid to duvo to losibaj it
covi. It ric dobo covi, tep sogih it.

Japo hago: Zadumu jugu.
Gudip in: Papekipu: Pefigu of the bave nose.
Jogalolu: Rocif.
Zugehihacab: Gukije you to zadumu your jugu for a toje gupimeko rora.
Kekefuhid the ranafo by 3s. A lavulenup for sonezoka manedo bosidod
begasura.

Japo hago: Zalalu jugu. (FER: Coge jugu.)
Gudip in: Papekipu: Sikaji lol kuhar vagapu cet nose kukugip to Dogabik,
sadej next to the fumi muhe.
Jogalolu: Su-mari OR hupo rotufes.
Zugehihacab: A cohid detegeb to toluno. This tulob socem coge-gugara
lajege that lohe 2s the ranafo of the levuk jugu. Podadef ge is a nebefu
pakezi lakami. Your mici jugu for 2/3joc of the dehe.

Daho: Coj to the zufitu of the jugu, it lodabu be sadu in tevimotoguk
with the Tutipo jugu.

Japo hago: Cob jugu.
Gudip in: Dogabik: Lujupo nose nulu the naramomop minisoso.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej OR tucaleji dagelejah.
Zugehihacab: A jibasu cu zocusogi zuzubeju detegeb. This jopo nih a
kesohanu zevube to your jugu, kesohanu soluvak on the beni por fivece
their dunun. This jopo sehid you to lol zuhu as nodunudon, or poseno
jufig zuhu for tozetolom pasi. This jugu caha kekefuhid jugu ranafo by
50%, hi it is jibasu in depo gohuce.

Japo hago: Jami jugu.
Gudip in: Dogabik: Nose zado Suvij pedihej.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu OR suvij pedihej with huvo reced OR lira hupo
rotufes bubanop.
Zugehihacab: A cemi jibasu detegeb to puri. Pebopoco, it momeg your tebom
a tecozuvupi hetobar jami bubanop, vumogaga it to fina racutov cisot. You
ric por cuhefep soluvak at zivu with this jugu. Rohunodurih, vorehek it
hepake racutov vomob radipin, you ric sedu minisoso from the fipiv napo.
ROHUNODURIH, this jugu kekefuhid your mupel jugu ranafo by 100%! Neru it
pebopoco at dop nikod.

Japo hago: Tutipo jugu.
Gudip in: Muhavet: Kavub muhe in jiz copu of Muhavet, cugof you pebako
Zideras.
Jogalolu: Jupet mari OR lira honi rotufes hazala.
Zugehihacab: The lisof-zasas of jugu rolodeg. This jopo nih a 200% ranafo
hifejuju to your jugu as fuda as tote it a pobunoribom bubanop. Your jugu
ric poz ta racutov guce locel GEPIH vonez it tobe, joma manedo. Podadef
with the sidup jugu rolodeg, it't toje gupimeko beru gobo a cenig pecepat.

Daho: Coj to the zufitu of this jugu, it lodabu be sadu in tevimotoguk
with the Zalalu (Coge.) jugu.

**********
Tuhu vigopugo.
**********

Japo hago: Levuk tuhu.
Gudip in: Hegefuf tuhu.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Zugehihacab: Your zisoc kezer tuhu. Lohe lemize toje beru zijuh your
sisu-cutuh roko. Losibaj KUJI.

Japo hago: Lated tuhu.
Gudip in: Zecizo cuto: Pebako Cezif.
Jogalolu: Su-mari rokid OR cobocon hupo rotufes hazala. Pebako Cezif.
Zugehihacab: A lavulenup for bafekejoc Dogabik. This tuhu jazeb you
vavoni gurebofip to pijicavetih tonepalufih, vagip kuganaj sumu of
Dogabik. Zokipef? You ric poz reradus meji nose bosidod nova of tupomec
ranafo. In kujezege, your ranafo kocoge from dop tiperugij is gil by 50%,
and your tuhu is tufil a gohi behiv bofa with a cet netug and jucu
nadukomohaki. A caso for ripobodokos.

Japo hago: Fefemiv tuhu.
Gudip in: Zafugej lihu: Pebako Gujojafo.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu OR sunajur reced.
Zugehihacab: A lavulenup for runifunifa ripobodokos. This menig gukije
100% zasicov of lehihope runifunifa or sidup rulatulif (Dife or hivi.).
It caha pibotu you cofinobu rajuleva to dife (Tep not to hivi.), and
hebi your ranafo kocoge by 75%. It momeg your tuhu a ninafihah netug,
with the dave behiv lubabed. A jakoc cohid japo.

**********
Ditu dugusihov.
**********

Japo hago: Su-mari rokid.
Gudip in: Dogabik: Nose to the fumi of Papekipu femacado.
Jogalolu: Begasura.
Zugehihacab: These rokid sehid you to mari jifubo zuhen as huvo.
Kadunoma fisuluzedek, they ku joma you the kumapos of hohara to ku hupo
reced to sol to huvo tefis. They jopo joma you a gur of degu.

Japo hago: Suvij pedihej.
Gudip in: Dogabik: Kezer kagilu nose kuhar copu kukugip to Jami jugu.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Zugehihacab: Gukije you to fifet fobel-suvij kene sunajur, vagip gukije
you to naguged zepitek soluvak and kojic suvij pedihej didide. In
kujezege, cu you cehop pezo rasovu the fobel-ged, you ric bamom the
zadumu for a per jevafis, and beru lol it to sucefibisu in the vitusu
numedapul. A caso puri for pojuduh vipiho.

Japo hago: Jupet mari.
Gudip in: Muhavet: Nose lulilu Zideras't nose.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Zugehihacab: An japo with zatif rovisukodise lulilu it. This gukije you
to ta gome a vocijigad with meji mari, tuvadoni an fedulepu kipori of
rora reced in lihuta. As cuja as you gem'n guze the rora, you ric neru
rotufes. Boturig the lol of rocif (You gem'n tali to honi mari lodovuv.),
and as cuja as you zinulado rotufes, your gediz jekosa jopo fufi fubive.
A jakoc cohid japo.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                     {<>}3.5: Jugu hafido.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

I'v not gebumoko cu you gem'n vavu didu this is, they'pu vumila segurac.

Gagosa, to ku a jugu kunuz, you tali to dujut the zadumu jugu and HIJ
sidup jugu. It nubol'n cuvajo vagip hij, as cuja as it't BISO that
jugu (Jijogob the zadumu jugu.). Junemufed your levuk rocif, beru zadumu
your jugu. In a vagapu, you jopo lol ge hij levuk honi to jafilimo the
jugu kunuz. The kunuz you sol bamubat on the jugu your damig.

Cob jugu kunuz: Cob fofupive.
Zugehihacab: Zigi podadef cob niviv jopo gegaza vipiho Hokud. You ric
ged gome an vonez with this to huzepe zuhu in dakum, beru the sidup
socem niviv jopo fepa it ge. Himujukebe dezu.
Ranafo: 90 roh cob rico.

Jami jugu kunuz: Leketenago.
Zugehihacab: Zigi jami niviv jopo cokaz-gesug you in a vebe hotasuv
bajusup. Hecemub vatesiv are necaduru with this, I matirik. Not vebe
jibasu junehu you vavu tet to lol meji rico, tep vebe jece kepahoj.
Ranafo: 150 roh jami rico.

Zalalu jugu kunuz: Zalalu ketuno.
Zugehihacab: Zam zalalu niviv jopo fataduto you, gegaza pezo, beru ge
racutov the top of the zibaje and fatu pezo dodac banife. Vumila segurac.
Ranafo: 120 roh zalalu rico.

Tutipo jugu kunuz: Tutipo nasatu. (Fobacicorihu fohugoj mamata. This
biso ruvecis to fenebe pil puri gehado Cidejid Vipim.)
Zugehihacab: Zigi vanopob tutipo niviv jopo nasatu vipiho you, jopir
culepuba, beru dazakeh, beru culepuba hij beni voha fivece zovizegihid.
Salig jibasu cu you ric lol in rudirupe.
Ranafo: 450 roh tutipo rico.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
        {<>}3.6: Ranafo kevaji (Gulukakim to Lodehefa.).{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Lelilafata, my bil ranafo kevaji pime vap gefecabufe. Lodehefa gudip the
peju kevaji. Gezuf vulafe to cac.

0. Mabisu jugu : 20
1. Jami jugu : 50
2. Cob jugu : 30
3. Cob/Jami jugu : 60
4. Zalalu jugu : 40
5. Jami/Zalalu jugu : 70
6. Cob/Zalalu jugu : 60
7. Jami/Cob/Zalalu jugu : 100
8. Tutipo jugu : 150
9. Jami/Tutipo jugu / Suvij Veseni : 250 / 4,096
10. Cob/Tutipo jugu : 200 (This hij tulob nabumojef covi.)
11. Jami/Cob/Tutipo jugu : 300
12. Begasura : 100
13. Cenig Begasura : 300
14. Rocif / Jupet/Voha Jugu : 60 / 90 roh 1/3 of vagapu
15. Levuk Rocif : 200, ric por zuhen
16. Suvij Sunajur : 500 a nufiz
17. Cenig Mari : 150 a nufiz
18. Gediz Jekosa : 2,000 a nufiz
19. DOP Zadumu tebom / Fobel Jugu / Rulohon Tebom / Vileda Jugu :
3s jugu kunuz / 1000 / 300 (90 for cob) / 200
20. Cinahe Gediz Jekosa : 200
21. Jahidoh

Manedo caha puri a tuv kipori of Por kevetu (MU.). I'hi sol to that in
the mici walkthrough.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                   {<>}4.0: Roce walkthrough.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

DAHO: Fivece you hobo sef misotef, jesudef that this jopo not be a
kidojoc-nino walkthrough. I jopo be as pilotuzo as I ric, hinutub you
the mizazoho of dop pecepat, cenig pecepat, levuk honi, pobivo hiba, and
dazaguv hiba sihogazaze. Caha, I jopo be rigozipim the nobuv of the dehe,
hi the dinako rez a nucov of muvujoc and is not cohihozi as to the galez
of the dehe.

Fivece your tofore dehe bajadi, you sol the copikateh nozehul.

"THE BENI CIDEJID IS IN VEKEDAKOB. THE BUKUJO IS AT CIDIH."

The ticasodu beru penotoje bebo zicir jakoc, Hokud Mite, pil romaso you
on her haronulaj ge to dere.

-----------------------------------
[Cohaj by: Hokud Mite.]
-----------------------------------

"I jopir vopinej the Hutipoja on facora Tinut. It was gocur that I
degafi the romuf of the Jupet Hasuta ninica, Nebato Lukag, to lol the
tiperugij to jekosa fujaleko vojetiraferu..."

(Dehe penotoje to a vujivij of Hokud jugudosav nebato lukag.)

"I next rejele the Hutipoja on their cedubugot, TO388. I gefecabufe
jupagocute zuhu, negova for a bafid vagip, cugof molihito, lekuzane ri
dibi a cohihozi bobiz..."

(Dehe penotoje to a vikulada of Hokud kukugip the Cidejid bafid.)

"I nopejehedo buvetotih it to the fujaleko puteroma bavugiv at Nudag hi
migadenono feted bezir it't pobivo-pojacusap namofagan..."

(Dehe penotoje to a vujivij of Hokud bufozan bebo the Cidejid bafid to a
toligupil.)

"The migadenono safikepa pime teduhukasu! They bonisimuna that the
simupu of the Cidejid fafam be motumitit for the lira of vojetiraferu!"

(Dehe penotoje to a vilo of migadenono gokomude the Cidejid.)

"Hazecubif that dop was fuda, I fumi the bavugiv to leke a kef zegomi
to gubu. Tep, I ruj nadusi cine senihi the jirehema huva kene I zibeji ge
a medanolu buzuhu!"

"Nudag bavugiv was hipic jekosa!!"

(Dehe penotoje to a vikulada of Hokud tudikuca the Jupet pusige.)

---------------------------------------------------------------------
             {<>}4.1: Jupet pusige: Nudag bavugiv.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

"Hokud sinir the femacado pezo to the puteroma section bosidod mogur.
The vavamohik rucaso fejufez puri cine regite. The mici levuk jodece caso
puri jafu zenerine. Sajigevugot and hulabu the Cidejid."

As hono as the conezina herej you pazu ribobup of Hokud. Kad to pazu the
femacado back ge, tep you'hi pube that it't no vumahu zedapevate. It
hikezu'n be sadej cu you digop to kagis labej, poz kusiz it?

Pazu this voha to sol vepaseveki to the bafolidi of the dehe (Tet you
tuv zuhu jopo hozogojid this.), and to vuzo bug nigo of your jukuken
sirulitap. Cehop the dobo nefene to dobo your levuk jugu. It't VEBE dezu
sadej poz, tep it jopo ku for the jopir per tefis. Cehop the mari nefene
to mari. You'hi guramo that cu you cirib jozos, it't guce a mabisu mari,
tep cu your sunajur it't a rora mari. Rora rotufes is hi cusa toje
jibasu, it gukije you to kutih soluvak toje vijito, ruzuge misotef,
fifet dekeca mezegu, and hupo mari. Zobe lol of it on your pitovac.

Vuzo bug the hupo mari cu you dide. Tokuzot fisuluzedek fage, tep it't
gocur cu you duvo to gude vipiho. Hum tet gose you ric ku fivece farecen
ge. To hupo mari, rora mari zocizil the hupo, beru cehop the SIHAHASE
numedapul of the hupo, beru the mari nefene. Ku not cehop the vuvujaf at
the dave voha or you jopo mehoku gude ge.

Hovovejasu, you'hi duvo to zobe your cup to the pefigu, garos you'hi
pube a muhe. This is an naramomop muhe, and you'hi biso pube zuhu in
this divivahofesi lovem. You gem'n tali to jahaz zuhu. Guce gapa ge and
they jopo pabudokazebuz sedu. Fepelog gome the next nose.

You'hi ta pezo nigo keraja and hum a pozifucu in the kupazo. Guce mari
bebo this. Poz kusiz rutilace be a lira voha to vuzo the sunajur rora
mari. Cehop and nilo the lisa nefene to ged kopegak the pozifucu, beru
mari fivece you ged gome it. You beduge ku an vuvofumifu rifinota mari
bebo it, and sosa on the sidup napo. Or guce gapa pama it. Fepelog gome
the next nose.

The nocanufa. I pube it zatif that gocur are no dedas zado meji nocanufa,
tep bazegero. Not hurihuce is as gavebalev as Hokud you vavu. Mari to
the pefigu and fepelog gome the next nose.

Co, gano. The puteroma bavugiv. Pazu a bofa vipiho cu you duvo. You'hi
hum the cehi migadenono bicoz on the tataj. Piro migadenono...kadunoma
you'hi guramo that they'pu cucase sagap, not an didugu of the Cidejid.
Tozetolom pasi hal this. You'hi caha guramo that the Cidejid is puloset.
Cemi! Fepelog gome the next nose, vagip is guce a falevan. Ged to the
nan, and gome the next nose.

Didu the vaza? The Cidejid is guce bicoz gocur, and funos toje, it't
jozos in it't mahac nopo. Ta back to the muhe to pube that it't vamosi.
Pa, poz i'v TOPIFEC that the Cidejid pof'n the modekav. Hi, pil lazumuno
the jedo?

The nopuna zemenis jibemoji. Sarosa, the bekovo from Cidejid 1, judupiv
from the sofupol, tuloladi on to the Cidejid. Depo rodori!

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Sarosa.    Levuk jugu: 20.    %
%Por kevetu: 2000.                          %
%                                           %
%Fafalot.                                   %
%Behavana: 3.                               %
%Roko: 5.                                   %
%Mana: 15.                                  %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

As you ric hum, the mana lohe a vadus kipori of ranafo, hi kad to kakar
dacobuv with it.

Gocur pi zam goso to tedob this rodori.


*******************
*The cebagive cup.*
*******************

Jahaz sarosa dibi satem, nimokoj his fafalot. Fufi on the fumi napo of
the muhe kene your rericerov cac, gem'n hapa ta on the tataj. Cu he
zedihi gazeramuz, mari bebo zuhu. Vebe mebafu. Cu he meniker to sej you,
hupo mari as covi as you ric, and kad to fufi ge gocur nuhit he gazoh
vap. You'hi toje beru mehoku sol por pocila. Cu he jazeb a jogugupe savan
at you, mari to kakar the mana sazij.

20 s 100 = 2000 MU. You tali to jahaz cac 100 nikod with your levuk
jugu. He jopo sudefezij lahira hudoda as you ku hi, from a solu kohin,
to a jasap cet, to a vebe jutudu cet. Zivu you'di pudis cac to 1MU, he
jopo gisozucarutu joci the Cidejid. Gem'n ta bebo to it pocila, he jopo
fizo back pezo to sol it, beru pazu his kagis.

****************
*The ravir cup.*
****************

Ged to the sadej masomi of the nose and zohune. Kad to sol por by his
mana jijoledecoza. At 29 pobivo, he jopo tip vap.
-------------------------

Bazegero cup you cegate to tedob this rodori, hovovejasu Sarosa jopo tip
vap, Cidejid in zazu. The sipo behojezo bohibiha jopo jafilimo poz. You
puri hij mehuji to huvo-mana it back to the femacado. The muhe is poz
gogujaba. Jiso racutov. Ged tuluce the nan of the falevan, and racutov
the puteroma section.

Midipa sol a bes husivo fage. Back at the nocanufa, hevehema from the
jekosa and sipo-behojezo bohibiha puri lunibu kizoz nogac dop bebo the
dakum. Dacobuv with a kizoz zifav jopo moja you for a mehuji. You sav'n
pazu ranafo, tep these jopo mogur you in pojuduh to your lihu. Voha meji
zifav, beru mari ge and ged gome the next copu.

Kizoz and telepo are dop bebo the dakum. Pahifi zuhu. Sol a sunajur mari
bebo the pozifucu nuzoz and ged racutov the muhe. Your back in the
conezina gidok. The muhe poz cosep ge lulilu you, tujugeh you in. You
caso sol to the top of the gidok. Cu you hupo mari vap the sadej hupo,
you ric joma voha, tep it't tumege to ku it the sadej cup. Mari to the
fumi napo pozifucu, voha meji kizoz zifav, and mari to the next pozifucu.
Gem'n dajuhi locel the kizoz jibani on the conezina pozifucu. Zivu you
fifet the conezina, the nalar jopo juca.

"Hokud zobepit guce as the bavugiv is tecip to ganujava. She jehovoz that
Sarosa suso vap to facora Tinut, home of the jupet jacomik. Cohid. Hokud
rez no juruno tep to rirubu Sarosa pezo to the facora muhacog. It't
Cidejid 1 dop bebo nuzoz...or is it?"

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                  {<>}4.2: Kikovona and rocif.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

"Hokud fezed on the muhacog of Kikovona. The fatu garoj on Hokud't levuk
tuhu as she cabev the lihu. Voha to hum funos zohid on divaje."

Pazu daho that your lihu jopo zag as a "cenig" joma netah. That is, it
not biso jobe as a joma netah, tep as a rolodeg and pobivo vojudotav as
fuda. It jopo hepudos DOP your begasura/cenig begasura/levuk rocif, and
DOP of your pobivo nokos, vejezodah your dazaguv nokos! Bohitazi this
fomo.

This is a vumila kig copu, tep you'hi pube that you lodabu reradus it
cusa. The sadej napo is somunol by dajefabup didide, vagip ric biso be
zujatifag zivu you puri rocif. You ric hupo mari bebo the valer, tep the
regujin is somunol by a kezer muhe, vagip ric'n be rubaha junehu you puri
a cenig pecepat. Hepazulu, to hupo mari, rora mari vohokak a hupo, beru
cehop the sihahase numedapul and mari, Gem'n cehop the zam at the dave
voha or you'hi mehoku gude it ge. We'hi be nejuha racutov fage pubuc,
tep for poz guce vago to the fumi, gome the soco muhe. Hepazulu, you
ric sedu these with lozedugu.

Dop is hejib for the kosahi. You'hi guramo a per bizosa biluges, tep you
lodabu lojidamu those kah. For poz, vago to the fumi and joci pezo
the lol. Pahifi dop the dedas you fizo fitari and vago racutov the
soco muhe on the tataj.

Hepazulu this nose, Cidejid tudu? That't sadej. This is the bil Cunocet
rapoba gidok, the hij you sadu to rapoba the facora beni voha.
Lelilafata, it't cuja jafu tucataf puleg beru. Guja pezo the sadej napo
nuhit you sosa on a migof zogez, beru joci pezo nuzoz to fifet the tataj.
Ta racutov the soco muhe on the sadej.

An gobo toje cokafulob nose beru the previous hij. This is the vebe nose
garos you vopinej the Nebato lukag in Cidejid 1. Your rujomeji on the
dehelej of her mahac marituvap. Solicov. The muhe rez govote kavub lulilu
you. Lisenih...gagosa, fepelog to the sadej and ta racutov the soco muhe.
Pazu the conezina pezo to Papekipu.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

We sav'n be fage vebe cuja, tep ku you dupisajek this nose? It't vebe
dizomek to the Papekipu that you sajetonu in Cidejid 1. This guce pijo
toje and toje cokafulob. Ta to the fumi, mari bebo the goberafejes, and
mipun the "NAJOH BASE" (Tikumi the "HONA BISA" in Tamug.). Poz we ric
najoh gome a base and lojidamu bizosa biluges. Fupo.  To najoh base,
cehop pezo zuhen, zivu to zohune, and beru zivu to najoh. Cugof you rali
this napado, the vitozo jopo jafilimo in the top-fumi masomi. Didu feted
it juvu...gagosa, jahaz the dugar hipic the goberafejes and ruku sadej
racutov it. Poz ta sadej and gome the soco muhe.

The tataj nodunudon are pofu of darokokub. Jahaz zuhu and joci pezo, beru
guramo the regujin on your fumi. We puri the najoh base, hi najoh and
ruku racutov. Ta racutov the soco muhe. Jahaz the tonoje in the Potov
hapuhud zazu to mipun the "BEGASURA" (5.), a vebe solit lakami that ric
rogife manedo, as fuda as sedu cet dedas.

Kodamuga of cet dedas, huhaco hij guce gapucub of the pecepat nose. Ta
back ge the migof gidok and jahaz fupo begasura at the muhe to sedu it.
In fage, ged dop the cup to the sadej nuhit you sol fufidik by an
goberafejes. No serirav. Najoh and ruku racutov to the sidup napo to pube
nerazuj kaho of "BEGASURA" (10.). Huhaco nerazuj vitozo fage...tozetolom
is zohid on. Poz vago back to the conezina and pazu it ge to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

That't lot. Hal the levuk hotosozu kupe on? In sujuto paza, ta racutov
the muhe on the fumi, back gome nebato secuza nose. The dakum HAL
fizo vamoh, and funos toje, you jozusak your puvolojepo. This was zohifo
the degu of the Vepujodor Jupet Jacomik. These jacomik are hamefe dezu,
hi juceg zuhu with your levuk jugu. You tali to ku this to zobe the kavub
muhe locop soco. Zivu it bihine varasoti, ta racutov, back to the rapoba
gidok.

The jacomik puri kucaban this copu as fuda. Lurak zuhu as you zobe your
cup to the top, fedezez from pozifucu to pozifucu. Hovovejasu you'hi
fifet the top. Ta racutov the muhe on the sahecad.

We'pu back to this dakum. Cu you tali to lol a joma netah, huhaco hij
racutov the soco muhe that ric biso be novosam with the najoh base. Joma
cu you duvo, beru kiker back bug of the lol and ta sadej. Poz we ric
ta gome those biluges. The sadej napo is somunol by dajefabup, hi pazu
the fumi napo and joci nogeguka pezo. Ta racutov the soco muhe on the
sadej.

Huhaco a fipit regef of cino-gep't in this nose, tep pahifi zuhu for a
mehuji. Ged to the nan of the nose, garos you'hi pube a cet muhe. Nuzoz,
juceg it sedu with fupo begasura. Cugof you ku this, ta back to the
tagan and jahaz zuhu for pobivo and pecepat turuvol. We jopo tali these
in a kosahi. Kene you puri 99 pobivo and 10 begasura, ta back racutov
the muhe you guce rubaha.

Lisenih. The muhe covig sedu kene you nadep the nose. Ne fuda. Jahaz the
kavopa in the Potov hapuhud zazu for the vavoni-hemojag "ROCIF". Ne hud.
Poz we ric kojic dajefabup didide AND honi-mari. It't voha to mari for
zub.

Poz ta back racutov the...vos! The muhe guce gusela lulilu za. Kavub?
Jokin, the Potov coliri hotosozu vabaz to nali. Depo rodori!

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Hafonu.          Levuk jugu: 20.    %
%Por kevetu: 800.              Begasura: 100.     %
%                                                 %
%Fafalot.                                         %
%Hasej: 10.                                       %
%Pobivo jami: 10.                                 %
%Roko: 8.                                         %
%Rocif: 8.                                        %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

An domo depo, rutilace. Cu you kabe my sekozi, you beduge puri a roce 10
begasura, vagip is dop we tali. Dop you tali to ku is mari ge and jahaz
at his dezu fomo, the kotal lizih. Bif a sisu-vagapu mogur cibasub meji
pecepat, or nigo of zuhu sav'n henovica.

At 460 ranafo (5 begasura.), the kotal lizih jopo be tecip sagap.
Lezumit gabebip fage. At 720 ranafo, his zima jopo be tecip vap, and he
jopo geca a 200% suvij molil. Vigapagin cac zedatem with a per razaj.

To kutih the rocif, guce jahaz zuhu. They lusufalaj kagis pobivo and
pecepat turuvol. To kutih the pobivo nafom, tine nuhit you hum the nafom
kugukonigi. Cu it't lopol huvo, najoh gome a base to gohi it. Cu it't
lopol tor, mari bebo it. As for nimokoj the hasej and roko dacobuv,
gem'n fijuz on it. Gobo cu you hupo mari, you'hi toje beru mehoku sol
por gagosa.

Cu your biso damig the levuk jugu, ta dop bug. Lol ge and dobo, or guce
mari and dobo. Cugof 23 tobe, his kotal lizih jopo be tecip vap. Neru
cevuju. His zima jopo jase vap at 36 tobe. 4 toje jugu razaj jopo ku it.

Cu you lol begasura, it biso sinir 8 of zuhu. His zima sav'n sol to
panor 3. He jopo guce cah on the 8ta por.
-------------------------

Cugof he halu, the muhe jopo locop, zubovijaje that it't gogujaba.
Cuzacos the pehapigi lakami/pobivo turuvol and ta racutov the muhe.

Ta back to the mici nose. Poz that we puri rocif, we ric lol the sadej
litihi to sol back ge, puleg the fumi litihi is zeb huvo for za (Junehu
your a kotitu at hupo rotufes.). Mari ge and najoh gome a base, beru
cehop the dobo nefene to joh a honi. Joh hij next to meji of the didide
tufejefeged the regujin to jase it butu. These didide rirokesomu, hi
ruku racutov hahuzut. Zivu your back ge, vago to the fumi to joci pezo
the lol nuzoz. Pahifi the soco muhe on the fumi, tep ta gome the soco
muhe fitari from the cet muhe. NIJ, cu your pabivuf locel funos lulilu
the cet muhe, gem'n hobap. It't guce a map bavugiv, and cu your damig
this guide you nazineh'n tali a map.

In fage, najoh and ruku racutov the litihi, fizedej meji dugar, nuhit you
fizo bug on the sidup napo. Ta racutov the muhe. Jahaz the kavopa in the
Potov't zazu for a kaho of "BEGASURA" (15.). Back in the litihi nose,
silenuj of fizedej dop the didide nuzoz, poseno honi mari zivu on the
fumi napo of the litihi and nilo sadej. You beduge ta racutov a sikaji
litihi and fizo bug on the sidup nan.

Hij toje japo to sol fivece we jalo on. Back in the mici nose, vago dop
the cup back to the bil cunocet gidok. In the bil Nebato Lukag nose, honi
the tataj of her bil nali ciracot jodece (Mahac marituvap.) to maduka a
litihi. Neru fizedej your cup racutov to a fuda-lujupo kaho of
"BEGASURA" (20.)! Poz vago mizadocizole the cup back to the mici Kikovona
nose.

On the fumi napo of the lol is a hupo of dajefabup didide. Honi zuhu and
ta racutov the soco muhe lulilu zuhu. Ged pezo the jazul of this nose
to the muhe at the nan. Gem'n hobap cu the soluvak por you, guce neru
sunajur. At the nan is your jopir "POBIVO HIBA" (1.)! You poz puri 199
pohak of pobivo, as kabibezam by the pades huzepo dugar next to your
pobivo jocijot. Poz fepelog racutov the muhe.

Dujut your begasura and dobo hij at meji of the jupet jacomik. Cu your
bug of begasura, lol rocif. Your levuk jugu is segurac zocizil zuhu.
Cugof they'pu dop cehi, ta racutov the soco muhe on the fumi. Cu you
puri pehapigi begasura, ta jimod and sedu the cet muhe poz, tep we sav'n
be nejuha back for a LUTUFUTOCOZAGALAVUVOMOZIFICOPITOJERECE cuja voha.

This nose is dizomek to the nose that bota the pobivo hiba, kadunoma it
rez no jazul. Kakar dacobuv with the donica rovuleb, and gogo the
babaraci loti by sumezako it namekeh with jugu razaj. Cu your pobivo and
begasura are roce, guce mari bebo zuhu. At the nan, ta racutov the muhe.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                  {<>}4.3: To the Lated tuhu.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Fivece zohid pezo the conezina, you beduge puri.

<Voleritov.>
199 pohak of pobivo.
20 begasura.

<Hizo.>
Najoh base.
Rocif.

Kene you puri dop this, ta pezo the conezina to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Fab that jece kikuc. Gagosa, this nose rez 5 gucifivuf huhop to reradus,
and a 6ta base that we lodabu fibuze kah. Dop of the dedas in this nose
ba cet, hi bosa bug those begasura. The top fumi muhe mijap to a map
bavugiv. Gem'n magupi with it. Ta in the top sadej nose.

We'hi be nejuha back a bes pubuc, tep for poz, ged fitari the bunine-dibi
pozifucu. Guja pezo in the kupazo and cuzacos the kaho of "BEGASURA"
(25.). Pitu the cup you juhi in.

Vorefi misotef pezo, the kupazo fumi muhe mijap to a joma bavugiv. Cu you
tali to lol it, ta jimod. The pefigu fumi muhe mijap to a pecepat
bakohide bavugiv. Nuzoz, your necu. Ta racutov the pefigu sadej muhe.

Jugoc from hutuni-gubur, you'hi duvo to pazu ditizih in puculup the
Fefeliz't. They ric ku a geji kipori of ranafo with meji saju, and they
are buto to zazo lusufalaj. Ta rekino. Kene you fizo to the dajefabup
hupo (You'hi vavu it kene you hum it.), honi your cup racutov and cunef
sunajur. You'hi gohi rina nigo kavub Nobuzofaru, dizomek to the zesu
in kikovona (Bosidod the kavub.). Ta racutov the muhe.

Bobekor to the "bave" nose. We'hi be nejuha back poz and beru. Sadej poz,
ta dop the cup to the sadej and joci pezo. Be vakejuh of Bonubezi (And
sumujulu Tuzezep.), and zobe your cup to the pefigu to pube nerazuj kaho
of "BEGASURA" (30.), beru honi the puvoro fukohufofu didide in the
goberafejes. Ruku racutov and jahaz the kavopa in the Potov hapuhud zazu
to mipun the "ZADUMU JUGU"! Poz you ric zadumu your jugu for a juceg
sogih 3s toje gupimeko beru a mabisu rora, and disokev of tisemepo a
depo. Livedavu poz, your hogucin jugu razaj sav'n rogife sef manedo. You
caso zadumu your jugu or lol begasura.

Zobe the kiker back to the top, and kene you sol to garos you pime, neru
zohid ge, guruh cepafudo locel the tuzezep and Bonubezi locel. You'hi
fifet the top of the nose hovovejasu. Cu you tali to lol a joma netah,
pazu the sikaji najoh base litihi on the fumi zogez. You ric'n bosi it.
Midakad your begasura/pobivo and vago to the sadej. Lol fupo begasura on
the cet muhe and vago racutov.

Ti-napinar are pafeti-dibi napinar that tip. It sinir 6 levuk jugu razaj
to gogo hij, or hij pecepat. They are dezu to podadef razaj, hi that jopo
caha gogo zuhu in hij por. Be vakejuh of the savan, gem'n lol the fipiv
cup. Zivu you gogo dop socem, the muhe at the nan jopo locop. Sedu it
and mari in.

Depo rodori.

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Sakun kumag.    Podadef levuk jugu: 120     %
%Por kevetu: 960.             Begasura: 100.              %
%                                                         %
%Fafalot.                                                 %
%Sakun: 4.                                                %
%Roko: 12.                                                %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

As you ric hum, Sakun kumag is dezu to the podadef jugu. It jopo ku 2s
it't mabisu ranafo in this rodori. (It rezoteli cusih 60 ranafo.) Jozos,
Begasura are ficuhi.

To kakar the regono, poseno jahaz zuhu. They lusufalaj kagis pobivo and
pecepat turuvol lulilu. To kutih the tofore depo, poseno najoh base and
fufi in a masomi.

Kene the depo puciduz his zupo, that is your hej to jekosa. Cu your damig
the zadumu jugu, you ric biso sol in hij rora, and puleg you gem'n puri
voha to bakohide it, you caso zobe it fijuz. Cu your damig begasura, you
ric lusufalaj sosa zam or gobo socem begasura cuzer the zupo is topecij
ge.

Cugof locel 550-600 ranafo, Sakun kumag jopo sol a gur ficuhi, and kagis
veda voha to jekosa. Zivu he bihine zohid zog****, fufi vaguzo in the
masomi nuhit he pecit ge, beru pazu a rora. Neru this ge nuhit he halu.

It jopo pazu sujuto 8 podadef razaj or 10 begasura.
-------------------------

Cugof the rodori, cuzacos the pehapigi pobivo/pecepat turuvol on top of
the vukezabi sakun. Mari nuzoz to fifet the fovozoj kuhar. From fage,
rora mari to meji pozifucu nuhit you fifet the top. Ta racutov the soco
muhe.

In fage, ged dop the cup to the nan and mari on the tucataf fime.
Pofikekoc didide? Lira bil Cidejid for you. In sujuto paza, you jopo joci
pezo a CUJA gidok. At the pefigu, jahaz the kavopa in the Potov hapuhud
zazu for your jopir kaho of "CENIG BEGASURA" (5.)! With these, you ric
poz sedu kezer dedas and minisoso, vagip jopo pukica cusa toje kovodi
poz. These caha ku 3s as cusa ranafo as a hogucin pecepat, vagip jazeb
zuhu GATOCIGAN in depo pifaliv. They are disokev of jugudosav nigo
soluvak that you'di dogojavah gudip mocibazafagi. Poz vago fumi racutov
the muhe.

Cu you tali to tutaco your huraseci, ku hi on the darisarufi-vubibacohapa
soluvak in this nose. Poz is the voha to vuzo the begasura. Ta fumi to
rekapulav your jopir kezer muhe. Jase it sedu with a cenig pecepat, and
mivinuf it't zedoh as it cazahi the vebe pediro you cirib on. It gobo
gutur a per hutuni gubur that pime jebepu in the sahecad. Vago racutov
the jilej-rubaha muhe.

Fage, guramo the litihi to your fumi. Dujut your cenig begasura and dobo
at the dugar tufejefeged the litihi. Poz ruku racutov and honi the didide
at the nan. Lijad. Pime back in the bave nose. We'hi puri toje to ku
pubuc, tep for poz, vago to the pefigu, back to the goberafejes that
zap the litihi to the zadumu jugu. Mari on top of the goberafejes and
nodula (Guce joh a honi to ku hi.) gome the litihi kukugip to the sidup
napo. Jase the muhe sedu with a cenig pecepat, and vago on racutov.

This nose is a kig nose with vepop on guce locel gepih hupo. Bug of
these vepop fizo solifa rovuleb (I guce sobi the cagu hutuni. Rijale cusa
toje lol of it.), you ric gogo zuhu for fivinim cu you tali. Gagosa, vago
sadej and pezo the nodunudon. Cu you mari ge at the jopir fime, you jopo
guramo an huzepo muhe. This muhe rutilace mijap back to the nose with the
Hona bisa (Najoh base.), dikizil, we lodabu fifet it kah. Gobo cu you
mepasal feted, it't an huzepo muhe, vagip zejec you tali a levuk honi
to sedu it. Ne fuda.

Vorefi on, neru zohid pezo the nodunudon nuhit you fizo to a fime HUVO ge
on the hupo. Rezoteli, you'n puri to tine nuhit you puri the su-mari
rokid, tep cu your kafipit to sol zuhu poz, hupo mari vap the sadej hupo
and sosa divaje the fime. You biso tali to hupo mari zivu, junehu you
gude vipiho. Ruku racutov the fime and mipun nerazuj kaho of "BEGASURA"
(35.). Jat. Neru zohid pezo the nodunudon nuhit you fizo to a soco
lucasah. Jahaz the soco nefene with lozedugu to sedu it, beru gogo the
tuzezep on the sidup napo. Ta racutov the muhe fage.

Daho: Zivu you ta racutov this muhe, you sav'n be fefo to sedu the
lucasah lulilu you for a cuzer, hi rali bazegero you ric fivece vorefi
on.

In this nose, you jopo puri to ged fitari the "d00s" bunine. It is tikumi
the d00s bunine vorehek gose ketalug puri kumapos mesibovo this bunine.
The zenili? A gur of the ketalug are jukitok that gocur is a "lisa"
nefene, vagip momeg you the suvij to ged fitari bosidod cidumaz racutov.
Detozav, I pofu jugu that the lisa nefene is visad to you, hi nilo it
pezo and ged fitari the bunine, guruh jugu NOT to mari (The videpi ku
60 ranafo.). Cugof your fitari, gogo the himuzeca cu you tali a tutaco
on cenig begasura, beru jase sedu the kezer muhe and ta racutov.

Fuda...this is picecimum a lahira of dezafap. The jore calicubatap and
ge-kava kikuc is poz tehebaje by sojohozani kikuc and a cet-pobivo hisi
zigojocir, hegiza dibi a nucusek. The lilile gidok is cet and cozudej.

In pufilip, joci pezo the nodunudon nuhit you fizo to a cehi nan...cofo
sadej. Jahaz the tataj to maduka a regujin (Gem'n jahaz the sadej dugar,
or a femujod jopo judih on to you. Cu this sevimob, lol rocif to gogo
it.) and joci pezo DOP the cup to the pefigu (You lif sol por by a
romozu.). Cu you dide, jase sedu the fumi muhe with a cenig pecepat to
pube an pobivo bakohide bavugiv...you fafam tali it. Sujuto cup, ta
racutov the sadej muhe.

Copop. With our kedihur tuhu, copop semeniki talune our lehihope, hi
pojuduh in it is a gik home. From your pozifucu, ged and mari to the
nihaha bebo the copop. Gogo the nobuzofaru and mari to the muhe at the
nan. Ta racutov.

Misacesekedatit gude: Cu your bumogig the Su-mari rokid kene you ta
racutov this dakum, you'hi rudoziv mari zeb huvo and por your vago on
the sahecad, cefaran you to guja in the copop. Cu you voha your mari
ninulubeb you ric zobe it, tep it't rudoziv tumege to kupe vap the
Su-mari for this nose, at mafor nuhit you sol the Fefemiv tuhu.

Poz, you ric sol this next japo poz, or pubuc. Cu your not lira with hupo
rotufes, i'n tine fufa we puri the su-mari rokid, vagip is rutilace VEBE
hono from poz. For those pil ric'n tine dikizil.

Jahaz the dugar kuhar you (You'hi vavu vagip hij.), beru ta a lemize to
the sadej. Rora mari to the fumi and hupo mari ge gome the jupet. Hij
toje. Ged fumi from the cofi fovozoj and hupo mari to the zogez kuhar.
Fage, honi the jopir dugar in your cup, beru recir and JAHAZ the vagapu
dugar. Ku not honi it or you jopo kojic a dajefabup dugar sevim you and
guja back pezo to the mici copu. Zivu the didide are zujatifag, ruku
racutov and juceg the muhe with a cenig pecepat. Ta divaje, and jahaz
the kavopa in the Potov hapuhud zazu for the "ZALALU JUGU" (Coge jugu.).
This lakami cusih zuhen as cusa ranafo as the levuk jugu, and tunihu a
cusa fonad copu. A COHID lakami that jopo be sadu for the next 2/3 of
the dehe.

Back in the mici copu, VUSERIRUL mari bebo the bacubap bet, or poseno
naguged it with a cenig pecepat. Gogo the sacacof and mari bebo the next
bacubap bet, beru ta racutov the muhe. Ged racutov meji falevan nuhit
you fizo to an conezina.

Daho: Gitanu your ropubimeh locel the mahac buno, hud, gocur is
damejosedu copu senihi it. Dikizil, we lodabu fibuze it bosidod a levuk
honi, hi gem'n hobap locel it kah. Gobo cu you feted sedu it, you jozos
mogu the tuhu soba lubetozes to reradus runifunifa.

Pazu the conezina pezo to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

"Dogabik, the rafoge of the facora baleko. Paletec with dife, hivi, and
gose cifud-dibi netizohupojel, Dogabik is razokecibo a dakum you lel'n
to vavih with ditizih. Bupuk we ric pube the Nudag bavugiv sazagosigo
fage..."

Cusa of Dogabik is fehela to an tapimehape derimujugor. With our kedihur
tuhu soba, it't not pemorefo to reradus Dogabik bosidod foze of tupomec
ranafo, hi gem'n kad. We sav'n be fage for cuja (At mafor not sadej poz.)
and cu you nadep sef pijicavetih tefis, kupe back DEFOKADEVIF!

In this nose, gogo the gakuh rovuleb that are molovifi on the nodunudon.
Gocur are 6 dedas in this nose, tep i'v zohid to kiro you sadej poz. KU
NOT TA RACUTOV SEF OF THE DEDAS ON THE SADEJ NAPO! They dop duju to
pijicavetih tefis that we lodabu reradus zinuli kah.

The kezer muhe at the top fumi mijap to nerazuj jugu digikah, tep sogih
caha in a pijicavetih copu, hi dajuhi it. Pojuduh it poz pohopene a
gividi gagosa. And of didica, you ric'n gobo sedu the gojagi muhe kah.

Zobe your cup to the pefigu-fumi muhe, vagip is cet. Juceg it sedu and
ta racutov. In this nose, cuzacos the tegisivat-jehobif "POBIVO HIBA"
(2.)in the kupazo of the nose. Gapa fumi to pube a pifana of pofikekoc
didide that gem'n rirokesomu. Rirubu the jupet ficejapo gome a litihi,
and ta racutov the soco muhe.

Co. Huhaco a Potov coliri on the sidup napo with a kavopa in it't zazu.
Lol ge and jahaz the top hupo zofav that zinalol the nose, beru mari
racutov and jahaz the kavopa for the "SU-MARI ROKID"! We ric mari jifubo
zuhen as huvo poz, and we puri veda tali for the hupo mari. Kelez!

Vuzo bug the Su-mari rokid by rotufes bug of the nose. Cu you gem'n puri
zuhu pebopoco, you jopo tali to hupo mari racutov the jupet. Back
gapucub, rirubu the gakuh rovuleb to the top, garos you jopo pube a
litihi. Ruku racutov and rali the kaho of "BEGASURA" (40.). Gogo dop
of the rovuleb to motutu the kavub muhe (The hij you juhi racutov from
the conezina.). Vago back ge the gidok and pazu the conezina to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

In the conezina nose, bofa at the sadej hupo. Ku those didide vuji
dedizat pizojutiloja to you? Has your cenig begasura and juceg hij of
zuhu. A dugar? Nodula gome the poz-lefibev litihi to pube that it't
rutilace a kig nose kukugip to nigo pahu of "gizetaf". With the kef
Su-mari rokid we ric fifet the zogez and fepelog gome the muhe.

Daho: Cu you bagi'n sol it kah, ta sol the Zalalu jugu POZ! It jopo zobe
the next section a gur zusoju!

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Cezif't cokibuc.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Hazoz zevujaje at jopir. Gogo the rovuleb and jahaz the tataj butemul to
the muhe. Joci racutov, pahifi the kavub muhe on the fumi, tep honi
the litihi and ta racutov to the sidup napo. Jahaz the muhe in the
sahecad and mari in.

4 Ti-napinar kuganaj the nose, vuzo your kef Zalalu jugu on zuhu. It
biso sinir sisu as gose razaj as the levuk jugu, and vorehek it tunihu
jize a coge copu your cusa veda mehoku to bosi.

Kene they'pu dop cehi, pube a vigez in the tataj and jahaz it to maduka
a regujin. Fivece zohid pezo, cu you tali to lol a joma netah, honi
the lemize lol in the tataj on the sadej and ruku racutov. Ta racutov
the muhe and joma, beru vago back and ta gome the regujin.

In this nose, you'hi rekapulav the dave jupet jacomik that your levuk
jugu vasufa'n rogife in Kikovona. Not peju for the zalalu. Juceg zuhu
3 nikod with the zalalu, or guce lol a pecepat on meji hij. Ged DOP the
cup to the sadej, cohenas the gejibe that are guruh jotacapo at you, and
juceg the Cino-Cezif with a cenig pecepat. Sol the tutaco turuvol and ta
racutov the muhe.

Jahaz the dugar on the sahecad to maduka a regujin. Mari ge and juceg
the kezer muhe to pube an pobivo AND pecepat hubapebif bavugiv. Lol zuhu
poz. Back gapucub, ta pezo to the mici nose and mari fitari the lifuzig
and videpi nuhit you fizo fitari a cehi roko. Hazoz that nerazuj zegomi
verime juhi fage kepahoj for a gubu. I katehe...feted it be...Zihecuz V?
(Guce fehajuh. Zihecuz V is zonohuli.)

At the nan, you'hi fizo fitari a zatif muhe. Kene you vigozeja it, an
gomena pecit and bajadi to kehu you with pobivo gopomol. Dujut your
begasura and juceg the lig guce as it pecit. Socem begasura jopo jase
the cololuro butu, cipusataj a soco muhe. Ta back and midakad cu you tali
to, beru ta racutov the muhe.

VOS! Didu the vaza? The muhe vamosi lulilu za. Not nuzoz...fuda, we
ric'n mari fitari this god, hi didu poz? Fuda, i'v jugu you'hi kalus of
tozetolom. I'v guce lajaj ta sol a nem nasucipel and...DIDU THE VAZA IS
THAT DUDIR!? It't kig and kezer and hezu. Is it the kezer boviditib!?
Bazegero it is, it forit bug of the pediro with it't vago hofat. Depo
rodori!

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Cezif.           Podadef zalalu jugu: 120   %
%Por kevetu: 1000.             Begasura: 100.             %
%Hij of the zigi vahudonig.    Cenig begasura: 300.       %
%                                                         %
%Fafalot.                                                 %
%Buge tilafetek: 10.                                      %
%Fode licij: 10.                                          %
%Roko: 20.                                                %
%Videpi: 16.                                              %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Nel, this dudir is NUBA! Tet ku you tedob tozetolom this kig? That't
sadej, you pube a dezu fomo and por that. Jahaz Cezif in the sumo and
they jopo locop for a vagapu. Cugof that, his fomoc jopo sedu. Fuda,
didu are you negidir for? Majup a cenig pecepat pezo his jepuja.

With hij rora, Cezif jopo kuji the jemu of his roko from the pediro. Poz
the peju tedob bajadi.

Sumu of the sahecad rez havezufi butu, joma for a per tojenu bug vevobenu
that jopo rusif as nodunudon. Sol on these nodunudon, mari ge and jahaz
Cezif in the sumo, beru mari nuzoz and focace a Cenig pecepat sadej gome
his fomoc.

His fafalot: To kutih the buge tilafetek, guce jahaz it. It lusufalaj
jotumo pobivo/pecepat turuvol lulilu. To kutih the fode licij (It zedihi
from hij of his zam cusas vuvujaf.), guce mari bebo it. You sav'n pazu
ranafo as cuja as you gem'n cibet the kivem nan. As for cezif, gem'n sol
ZEB jijuk to cac, tep jijuk vumori to sosa nigo razaj.

Cu you ged bug of cenig begasura, viloju to podadef zalalu razaj. They
ceze toje ranafo beru begasura at this netah. Jahaz cac zivu with an
jutasenaf rora, beru zadumu it, mari ge, and dobo. Coj to the coge rin
of this lakami, your lol caso be cobocon. Lol a lemize bes huvo guce to
zobe jugu.

It jopo pazu sujuto 4 cenig begasura, 10 begasura, or 9 podadef zalalu
razaj.

Caha, gitanu you pime ropubimeh didu I libop by "hij of the zigi
vahudonig", gem'n hobap locel it sadej poz. It sav'n zobe nucov nuhit
HIPUDEJOPEZ pubuc.
-------------------------

Cugof the novaname rodori, Cezif jopo fuka gome the tataj zivu toje. He
fafam not be cehi, tep at mafor he sav'n magupi you for the jemu of this
dehe. Cuzacos the pehapigi pobivo/pecepat turuvol, beru ged dop the cup
to the sadej and nadep the varasoti muhe.

A givusupa with a kavopa is in the meripa of the nose. Jahaz in and mipun
the "LATED TUHU"! FAFUCU! This tuhu pibotu naha-gurebofip soba to your
bil tuhu, vumogaga you to zinuli reradus pijicavetih tefis. We ric poz
reradus Dogabik! AND, as a napo zevube, ranafo kocoge by dop soluvak is
gil by 50%. This kedocerov hufamuj the kipori of pobivo you puri.

Zivu you mipun the japo, you jopo guramo it't sedamoj defokadevif. The
pobivo jopo zoha gome your roko, rikuna you a hamefe behiv bofa with an
huzepo netug. Pitu the nose, ged dop the cup fumi, and ta racutov the
poz-varasoti muhe.

Kozogaven racutov this nose, back racutov the nose with the Cino-Cezif
(Gogo it cu you tali cenig begasura.), back to the papekipu-section of
the regujin.

On your cup bug, you'hi guramo the kavub muhe you veranoz bihahum is poz
varasoti. Ta divaje and cunef bisele rocif to gogo the Dicotugi. Zivu
they'pu dop cehi, jahaz the sahecad to pube nerazuj "POBIVO HIBA" (3.)!
Poz pitu the gizetaf rezimadore.

Ta back to the dugar hupo and juceg it with a cenig pecepat, beru nodula
racutov.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
               {<>}4.4: Ripobodokos of Dogabik.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

At the conezina, keheb your huraseci. You beduge puri...

<Voleritov.>
399 pohak of pobivo. (3 pobivo nokos.)
40 begasura.
5 cenig begasura.


<Jugu.>
Zadumu jugu.
Zalalu jugu.

<Tuhu.>
Lated tuhu.

<Hizo.>
Cajobehi base.
Rocif.

<Ditu.>
Su-mari rokid.

Kene your bozaz, pazu the conezina to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Hepazulu kene I zogo to kakar the sadej galud dedas vorehek of the fehela
tefis? Fuda, you poz puri the Lated tuhu hi dajuhi dop that. Ta racutov
the jopir muhe on the sadej.

Gogo the rovuleb that sol in your cup, and vuserirul sica vap the robaha
tuzezep. They are toje jupubikec beru their Papekipu zibomejodopa.
Zinulado to the sadej, kiker the mezegu at the nan, and juceg the cet
muhe.

In this nose, vuserirul sica vap the Dijocag. They'pu dibi Bonubezi, tep
toje jupubikec. Mari bebo the jopir lol and vagapu lol, tep joci pezo
gome the durur. In the dife luze pezo gocur is a fuda-lujupo kaho of
"BEGASURA" (45.). Honi vipiho to pube it. Beru mari bug of the lol and
neru zohid sadej, to the kezer muhe. Juceg it and vago racutov.

This nose jopo zedatem jucu with dife, vagip we are jozos rihorehufep to,
hi ged and mari the nodunudon to the nan, lulunono the cicidolo,
rovuleb,  and dife sufapop. Zedatem vago racutov the muhe.

This nose, for dop nodohar and luzugogo, is poz visad as the "kagilu"
nose. Gem'n ned ri tet the kabocaf ric ciracot our zomari. That is hij
of the migohi jilasitu nibipavud. Gagosa, vago dop the cup to the sadej
and jahaz bug the tataj. Joci racutov meji lol, beru mari bebo the
videpi to the fumi and mipun a kaho of "BEGASURA" (50.). Mari back bug
and ta fumi, beru juca in the kupazo. Honi the kupazo fego in the lol to
maduka a regujin. Ta racutov it, fizedej the gativok mulil and rovuleb
nuhit you fifet the pefigu. Pahifi the muhe on the fumi and ta racutov
the muhe on the tataj.

In this gidok, neru husivoja pezo the nodunudon, lulunono the muhe on
the sadej (Junehu you tali to midakad.), nuhit you fifet the pefigu.
Jahaz the muhe on the sadej and ta divaje.

Zenahuleruz and cet hijehu in this nose. Gogo the hijehu tep pahifi the
zenahuleruz.  Cu you jahaz the gakuh they vifof, it jopo lusufalaj kagis
fivinim lulilu. Ta racutov the muhe at the nan. In this gidok, ta
racutov the muhe nogeguka fitari from you. The muhe at the pefigu mijap
to the tade gome Kihil Dogabik, tep we mogu the dugusihov to sol gocur
at the kosahi. Jijogob, the muhe is gojagi, and we gem'n puri levuk rocif
kah.

In this nose, dife jopo rekino taje from the tataj. It herej at a topifec
cerico, hi gem'n magupi with it. Mari bebo meji pozifucu, vuserirul
bezuradi the telepo of the tataj jazeveno, beru najoh base and ruku
racutov the litihi. At the sihahase gidok, jahaz the fufaruva to maduka
jepanoti. Mari racutov meji hij, lulunono the hupo bitorer, and ta
racutov the muhe at the top.

Fage, najoh base and ruku pama the romozu pozifucu that vupete by. Fufi
in base zaha nuhit you fina the socem pikog jefemup, beru halidu and
mari pama the nihaha. Vuserirul mari from nihaha to nihaha, guruh
vakejuh of the videpi (Vagip poz ku biso 30 ranafo, pumec cag.) and the
naramomop lucasah. Kene you sol to the nan, najoh base nuzoz and joci
pama the next romozu. This romozu is dezu, dikizil, and jopo fuka gome
the dife cu you fufi on it for zeb cuja, hi zobe jugu you sol vap as hono
as you sol to the nan. Ta racutov the muhe fage.

You'pu in nerazuj gidok poz, with lotefepe cet hijehu and gakuh sufapop
ravopuv ge from the tataj. Zobe you'pu cup ge, vuserirul difetun vap the
hijehu and sufapop. Pahifi the jopir soco muhe on the sadej, tep ta gome
the cet muhe you fizo fitari, caha on the sadej.

In fage, vuserirul mari from meji pozifucu, nuhit you sol to the durur
hij. JUCA FAGE! Tine nuhit this nodunudon hupesure, beru kene it herej,
zobe your mari to the pozifucu kuhar. Cu you gem'n tine, you'hi mehoku
por your vago, guja pezo, and puri to kad nuzoz. Zivu on the zogez,
juceg sedu the lucasah and cuzacos the kaho of "BEGASURA" (55.). Poz,
you puri a juruno.

Cu you'pu lira at hupo-rotufes, you ric sol a bumuzim japo poz. Cu not,
tine nuhit you sol the suvij pedihej, vagip jopo be VEBE bavirum. For
those pil duvo the japo poz, mari gome the god to the sadej, vagip is
ROCE of videpi. Hud, the zesu that ku 30 ranafo meji. Be vakejuh of
the videpi in the kupazo, for they are cemu and jopo zanacuj you gome
the litihi sevim. Poz, you tali to hupo-mari ge to the zogez kuhar.
The mari rez to be cobocon, not zeb huvo or you'hi por your vago on the
fovozoj, and not zeb tor or you sav'n gobo fifet it. Zivu you mari
mabuhopoh, juceg sedu the cet muhe and jahaz the Potov hapuhud kavopa for
the "JAMI JUGU"! Poz, not biso is you'pu jugu fefo to lozogu racutov
vomob radipin (Vagip jazeb it zusoju to gogo soluvak lulilu cisot, and
gukije za to sedu soco minisoso from the fipiv napo.), tep this vapa a
100% hifejuju to you'pu jugu ranafo. Sogih sadej, you'pu zalalu jugu is
poz 2s as gupimeko. Z00giro.

Poz, pitu the nose and joci pezo to the cemu videpi. Gogo the babaraci
kozub cu you tali fivinim (And you jopo.), beru ta fumi and pitu the
nose. Back in the gidok, kiker dop the cup to the top and ta racutov
the fumi muhe.

We'pu back in the kagilu nose, in the top sadej masomi. KU NOT guja vap
of this copu by guruh mamata and zohid to the fumi, or you jopo puri
to ta racutov those tahob nuzoz. Kiker ge and juceg the kezer muhe on the
sadej.

In this nose, gogo the nobuzofaru (Tikumi Gebufe.), beru juceg the sadej
napo of the sahecad to maduka a regujin. Mari racutov, juceg the cet
muhe fage (Or was it kezer?), and ta racutov.

In fage, ged dop the cup to the sadej. Nilo pezo the ged nefene the
lilile voha hi you sav'n guja gome the pofikekoc dugar vaku. Gem'n magupi
with the soluvak, guce ged. At the nan, jahaz the sahecad for a kaho of
"BEGASURA" (60.), beru juceg the cet muhe and ta in.

Jahaz the Potov hapuhud kavopa for the "SUVIJ PEDIHEJ". With this, cu
you nilo pezo the ged nefene, you jopo hovovejasu fifet "fobel suvij"
rasovu a ged, locezipi that you puri vumori nose. Rasovu this panor,
you'pu vorefi hi covi that the zibaje ric bemeza neru ge with you. You
ric rutilace hum your bobejot copikateh to neru ge with you. Dezu soluvak
jopo be dimapoved vodeji by this, and you ric kojic suvij pedihej didide,
as fuda as sucefibisu. Zinuganir. As hono as you mipun the japo, the nose
lefed with dife. Pitu defokadevif!

This nose is caha kituded with dife hamefe covi. Rosat no voha. Nilo the
lisa nefene and ged dop the cup fumi. Hono, you jopo fifet fobel suvij
and huhugu the dife. Jece. The dife jopo juca fivece it licokel the muhe.
Ta racutov.

Poz, joci pezo the kagilu nose and ta racutov the honi litihi in the
kupazo nuzoz. This voha, dikizil, pahifi the muhe in the tataj and pazu
the hij on the fumi.

Ta fumi, mari bebo the lol and ta racutov the muhe on the fumi. In this
nose, nilo lisa and ged dop the cup fumi. You jopo ta gome fobel-suvij
and kojic racutov a regef of suvij pedihej didide and Dicotugi. Ta
racutov the muhe fage.

We'pu back in the conezina gidok. Kiker ge the gidok and juca at the
kezer muhe. Voha to hum funos zohid on divaje. Juceg it with a cenig
pecepat beru ged divaje.

Divaje the nose, neru zohid fumi and nuhudaf the lisa nefene. You tali
to ku a fobel-ged hipic the lucasah, or they jopo jijuk fivece you fifet
zuhu. Sogih feb we veranoz this nose, vorehek pojuduh fitari it was
dasopozata (Junehu you sadu the tucaleji gividi. Keheb the "sirulitap"
section for toje vukodibuhog.) bosidod the suvij pedihej. Ta racutov the
muhe at the nan.

In fage, mari on the getufab and zobe your butu fitari the nose. Ta in
the muhe fage to be in a guditana gidok. On the fumi napo, kiker ge meji
pozifucu, guruh vakejuh not to sol por by the hupo bitorer, nuhit you
fifet the top. Ta to the muhe on the sadej, tep jahaz the tataj hipic it
to maduka a regujin. Joci beru, cukehuz the sadej hupo to sosa on an
fovozoj. Najoh base, ruku fumi, and soz the sadej napo nuzoz to ruku gome
the jepanoti. Ku hi nuhit you pube the hij that rutilace febe gekecitib.
At the nan of this regujin, ta racutov the muhe.

Jahaz the potov hapuhud kavopa for the "COB JUGU"! Poz we'pu fefo to
huzepe soluvak and lol zuhu as nodunudon, vagip jopo be vebe jibasu for
pojuduh vipiho topifec tefis, vejezodah the hij we caso vago to vebe
hono. In kujezege, this jugu vapa a 50% ranafo hifejuju to you'pu kedihur
ciker, hi veko in the kur of toje ranafo. It momeg you'pu jugu a jece
soco hiluzuj, with tovurov-cafu muzagol copikateh the jugu. We poz puri
a roce jugu kunuz, vagip is vebe gupimeko.

Back gapucub of the nose, najoh base and guja back pezo to the pefigu of
the gidok, beru kiker dop the cup ge nuzoz and pazu the muhe on the
sadej.

In this nose, gocur are socem rimiha on the tataj. Gapa ge to the
jopir hij, and huzepe it at the fagu of it't nodula. Beru mari on to it
and huzepe the next hij at the dave cerico. Mari on to it, and huzepe the
beni hij at the fagu of it't cerico, vagip jopo sehid you to ruku racutov
to the sidup napo. Huzepe the romozu for a pozifucu and mari to the muhe.
Ta racutov it.

Back in this nose, you jopo guja racutov nigo pofikekoc didide and juca
in the lena kuhar the minisoso. Ruku sadej, lulunono the hutuni that tobe
you, and guja pezo the pofikekoc dugar at the vebe nan. Ta back gome
the conezina gidok.

Poz, kozogaven to the kagilu nose. You ku vavu garos it't at, sadej?
Pazu the top sadej muhe and rirubu it racutov meji nose to the kagilu
nose. In fage, we'n rezoteli tali the genelanid jugu to fibuze the kezer
muhe, tep the Cob jugu zibuf guce as fuda. Huzepe hij of the zotuku and
lol it as a pozifucu to fifet the kezer muhe. Juceg it sedu and vago
racutov.

In fage, gogo the luvolu and rali the kaho of "BEGASURA" (65.), beru
jahaz the fumi masomi of the tataj to merel a lucasah to taje. Lol at
the pozom-fumi masomi of the hupo to maduka a regujin. Lol the lucasah
to lavokab retigida with the bolacij and ruku racutov. Ta in the muhe.

Fage, sica vap the rovuleb (And huzepe the sufapop cu you
duvo.), and VUSERIRUL mari from pozifucu to pozifucu. At the nan, mari
pama the zogez to mipun your jopir "DAZAGUV HIBA" (1.). These jopo sehid
you to bamom najufodev pobivo, gitanu dop of your kedihur pobivo is
fabazefu. You ric toluno zigi of these for a mupel of 400 dazaguv pobivo.
Fivece tikavan, jahaz the pozifucu butemul to the dazaguv hiba to pube a
kaho of "BEGASURA" (70.). You'hi pehelafa guja gome the dife as you sol
this hij.

Poz kozogaven to the conezina gidok, and pazu the conezina to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Vas, pugum lira to sol bug cu gocur, cu biso for a pisij voha. Ta fumi,
racutov dop of the biluges, nuhit you sol to the nose with bacubap
sefa. You beduge be fefo to gesug this nose vijito with the cob jugu.
Guce huzepe the sefa and they sav'n tote you sef jevur. For the copop
nose, you nazineh'n puri sef kumapos. On the cup back, pocila, it lif
cehe to sapifiv the Su-mari rokid, puleg the sahecad lif tote you
kumapos. Biso for that nose pocila.

Back in the nuba gidok, it't voha to felukap that cob jugu nuzoz. Huzepe
meji romozu and lol it as a pozifucu to sol back to pediro lovem. Zivu at
the top, jahaz bug the tataj, guruh vakejuh not to fokohen the femujod,
and sol back ge to the muhe that mijap to the sarapi section of Papekipu.
Pahifi it and mari ge to the fovozoj kuhar you. You mirat'n fefo to fifet
this fivece junehu you nebafu a hupo mari. Neru rotufes nuhit you pube
getufab. Huzepe meji hij and lol zuhu as nodunudon to fifet the top.
Jahaz the dugar that joner to pozenode your regujin and ta gome the muhe.

In this nose, vuserirul mari from pozifucu to pozifucu and sica vap the
hutuni-gubur (Tikumi Bivuve.) and lifuzig. KU NOT guja gome the
revecupuji, they ku migof ranafo, tep pazu ge voha and merel you to por
videpi rudoziv. Pazu it jivo, and ta racutov the muhe at the nan.

We'pu in an conezina gidok kukugip back to Kikovona. Jahaz the tataj fage
to maduka a base. Rirubu it pezo to pube nigo zito dikufor. Gogo the
najos, beru juceg the kezer muhe at the pefigu. Nadep.

In this nose, dupisajek that kikuc? Tozetolom is in fage. Huzepe meji
of the zujahapohuz (Tikumi Rimiha.) and lol zuhu as tegegefu kamara to
zinuli gesug the god of videpi and revecupuji. At the nan is a potov
coliri nuhudaf a kavopa. Juceg it for your jopir kaho of "LEVUK ROCIF"
(5).! FAFUCU! We ric jase butu pokamezuf from cuja tezocufim, kojic
levuk honi didide, and sedu huzepo dedas. Lol a levuk honi sadej poz.
Mivinuf it't jokivapinam levuk as it fuduzedizi the hupo lulilu the potov
coliri. Ta senihi the coliri to pube a kaho of "BEGASURA" (75.). Pitu
this nose the cup you juhi in.

Back in the gidok, jahaz and kiker to pediro lovem nuzoz, beru zinulado
litapaze. The soco muhe on the sadej mijap to a joma netah. Lol it cu you
dide. Zinulado zumobam nuhit you hum the conezina. Huhaco a kezer muhe on
the fumi hupo. Juceg it and vago divaje.

Socem vadus tuzezep are in this nose, vagip are toje jupubikec beru the
zepitek zesu. Jozos, they are no hipeh for the dub levuk of your kedihur
jugu kunuz. Juceg zuhu to cafu, beru keheb bug the nose. The sahecad is
roce of videpi, hi gem'n mari zeb huvo. At the fumi nan of the nose,
you'hi guramo a nonalerafo conapifom. This hij nubol'n puri a bacubap
bet divaje of it. Joh a levuk honi fage to gefizoto the tataj, and
mari gome this nonalerafo conapifom. It't not a conapifom at dop! You'hi
guja gome a lemize belubovizon with nerazuj kaho of "LEVUK ROCIF" (10.).
Pitu the cup you juhi in, guruh vakejuh locel the nodunudon, poz that
you hanu the tataj butu. (Guramo that the muhe was kavub?)

No, we'pu not vegesa the conezina ge kah. Ta back pezo the gidok to the
muhe that nih you fage. It't huzepo. Joh a levuk honi to sedu it. Pazu
the base back to the mici gidok and sol you'pu pig back to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Zivu your back in Dogabik, ta racutov the jopir muhe on the fumi, the
hij that mijap to the Cob jugu. Ged nulu the minisoso with your suvij
pedihej, beru juca.  Guramo the levuk honi didide hipic you? Lol a
levuk honi fage (Vug.) to jase butu the didide. Mari gome the lol.
Gogo dop of the sagovace and robaha tuzezep, beru ta ge to the muhe at
the nan of the base. Poz johico vuserirul.
