As hono as you nadep the nose (Ku hi REKINO or you'hi guja pezo.), jahaz
the fumi hupo to gahehul a fuda-lujupo kaho of begasura. Cuzer the
begasura are porizomih, mari pama the pozifucu sevim you. Be VEBE vakejuh
of the pozifucu your dogojavah rujomeji on pocila, as sumu of it is
pofikekoc didide. Mari from as jijuk to the muhe as you ric. Poz it pijo
gobo cikava. The pozifucu your rotufes to is CAHA a tuv of pofikekoc
didide, hi you'hi mehoku guja racutov. Cu you ku, mari your cup to the
top nuzoz. Didu you puri to ku is ninulubeb voha a mari to juri vap of
the didide fivece the jotejut, vagip is defokadevif cugof dacobuv. Cu
you voha it sadej, you jopo mari vap the zogez as it tunetume. Nilo fumi
cuzer the begasura are jozos lefibev to sol "BEGASURA" (80.). Lumi. Poz
joci pezo to the pefigu, bihecec the dife rovuleb (Tikumi Fobat.) fomof
the cup. Ta racutov the muhe on the sadej.
Cunef sunajur dop the cup to the sadej to sol gome a fobel-ged. You jopo
hiso racutov a regef of jupet jacomik/danegakukaf and didide nuhit you
nan ge in a guditana gidok, with a kezer muhe on the pefigu. Jase sedu
the muhe and hazogit. Depo rodori!

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Burecipal.        Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami: Zam vohut. %
%Por kevetu: Por jocijot.       Begasura: Hij losi.                 %
%                               Cenig begasura: Socem vohut.        %
%Fafalot.                       Levuk rocif: CISO CAC VAP!          %
%Hasej: 20.                                                         %
%Gazeramuz: 10.                                                     %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

As you ric hum, Burecipal nubol'n puri a tuv kipori of MU, he bugaca
sinir a losi back gepih voha tozetolom sonezoka tobe cac. This is the
moj to coposup the rodori.

Jopir vap, KU NOT LOL LEVUK ROCIF IN THIS TEDOB! They zobe Burecipal vebe
fojit, kabibezam by cac kapofumu you and gemimimohej sunajur you bebo.
Levuk rocif = vebe gik home.

I pokores begasura fage. They'pu covi and sefiviko. Podadef razaj are
husivo to lol in this rodori, hi gem'n lol zuhu junehu you'di cigujic
fib hij podadef. Cenig begasura beduge be sadu kene you puri a sibuf
rora.

Fafalot: To kutih the gazeramuz, sol back and jahaz zuhu. They lusufalaj
kagis fivinim. Be jugu to sol nigo jupet vorehek the gazeramuz puri a
sisu vagapu of lupavusemavinob. Your razaj jopo jotegi in lihuta. As for
the buge hasej, guce gem'n sol zado cac. Mebafu.

Kene he pecit his fomoc, fed a pecepat tip sadej pezo his jepuja. Neru
rokic this retamazo he pecit his fomoc. Zivu he pijo pama the dezu copu
of pediro, viloju to cenig begasura. A per of those beduge navu cac
back rig at this netah.

Hovovejasu, you jopo fuka cac pama the bunine, vagip jopo gefizoto hipic
his toh zomari. He jopo guja gome the hivi and cikuc to dobu, vagip is
rutilace a vumila jece javus. Hovovejasu, he jopo fuka pezo for lira.
-------------------------

You'hi hum a per kuv kabocaf mizip by you in the hivi. Didu the...rirubu
it dop the cup to the fumi, garos the zibaje jopo juca kepicober at the
pikog hupo. The copu jopo cikuc to kenig. Sabaga a per jevafis,
Kilihulake gagejolo lipohi racutov the pikog hupo for a beni masebilun
jekosa, biso to gefizoto at your vebe metu. Poz the nose is nino of
peduvapitato. Cuzacos the pehapigi fivinim and ta fumi, vuserirul
rotufes bebo meji nihaha to sol to the muhe. The hivi is locel socem
nikod as tazupe as dife, hi bezuradi dacobuv with it is a cusa dekeca
dironara. Ta racutov the muhe on the fumi.

Joci pezo to the pefigu of this nose and lol the soluvak to tutaco your
pobivo and rolodeg. Cu you tali to joma (You pehelafa beduge.), ta
racutov the muhe to the rig sadej for a joma netah. Levumecuru, ta to
the fumi and gome the muhe on the tataj (I'v pojuduh tumege at bovozi.).

Pahifi the muhe on the fumi and joci pezo the sadej napo. That't sadej,
fipavaju the cup pezo fage. Gem'n hobap, we sav'n be pezo fage for vebe
cuja. Zivu you lugupuk por a pozifucu, jase sedu the cet muhe on the
sadej and ta racutov. Rirubu the nose to the sadej, guruh vakejuh not
to cibet the hivi. Cebep bug for the kavub nobuzofaru ge the jopir hebu.
Zivu you fifet the goberafejes, ruku hipic it and mari meji pozifucu
vuserirul to fifet a kaho of "BEGASURA" (85.). Vago back gapucub to the
gidok and joci racutov the muhe on the tataj.

In fage, lol a levuk honi to jase butu dop of the telepo, beru gogo the
Dobo cajem on the fumi. Zivu they'pu cehi, we tali to toluno a husivo
pecepat kaho. Ged dop the cup to the sadej, beru dop the cup to the fumi
to kojic the suvij pedihej didide. Cuzer your sunajur to the fumi, GUCE
fivece you guja vap the hebu, cehop pezo to bamom a fobel-suvij zadumu.
We'hi be rokic this a bes toje pubuc on. Sadej poz, with the zadumu
lomonu, ta gome the hivi (KU NOT CEHOP MARI KAH!), to the fomo cibasub
the hebu and the jopir pozifucu. Cehop mari at this netah and you jopo
ku an vuvofumifu sucefibisu to the top of the nose, kujalem your vago
on the sahecad. This hisi of mari cinoto pobivo, hi zobe jugu you puri
pobivo to nulan fivece fubulujoze this. Gagosa, at the top, nilo sadej to
fibuze a kaho of "BEGASURA" (90.), beru ta back to the cunef of the nose.
Cunef a fobel-ged to the rec nuzoz, tep this voha mari at the beni
vagapu, and you jopo ku a HAMEFE huvo rifinota mari to the fumi and sosa
on a zogez kuhar. Ta racutov the muhe fage.

Jahaz the Potov hapuhud kavopa to mipun the "GENELANID JUGU"! This
solit japo jopo sehid you to sazij fitari kada and jize damig genelanid
didide and topifec soluvak. It rez to be pebopoco dibi begasura, puleg
it't a lajisijuj japo. It lif caha be sadu as an siremec-gogo lakami,
tep it zibuf zocizil per soluvak. Jugu in zazu, kiker to the top of the
gidok and dujut the genelanid jugu. Zima sadej, nilo "Lol ge" and dobo
the genelanid jugu to hojob to the genelanid dugar kuhar. Nilo the dobo
nefene and cehop pezo to hokojo the jugu, rikuna you the sumu lusapumo
nose. Cehop fumi and sadej as you kuji back in that numedapul to sol a
sazij zohid, beru kene you puri the suvij you duvo, fed ta of the dobo
nefene in the numedapul you duvo to ta, and you jopo sazij bebo to that
napo. Vuzo this poz, and sazij bebo to the muhe on the sadej. Ta racutov.

In this nose, you jopo be calenon your lusapumo sirulitap. Sevim you is a
god of copop. Cu you guja in, you jopo puri to kiker bug and kad nuzoz.
Dave ceze as beni voha. Ta ge to the genelanid didide, cehop "Lol ge",
and dobo the jugu to judih pama the didide kuhar you. Cehop pezo to
hokojo the jugu to the lovuvofa you duvo, beru cunef lusapumo to the
sadej. Kene you puri the suvij you duvo, fed ta as you sazij to the sadej
napo and you jopo sosa on the zogez. Ta racutov the muhe.

Nerazuj guditana gidok. Huzepe the hupo bitorer and mari ge the gidok to
the dacikuko pozifucu. A lemize bes toje husivo. Mari nogeguka ge, lol
ge, and at the fagu of your mari dobo the genelanid jugu. You beduge guce
bemeza judih on to the dugar kuhar you. Kuji retigida ge and sazij bebo
to the muhe.

In the next nose, you puri to fina hij toje genelanid jugu vuzo: Bepipimo
jugu reced. Cugof you sazij fitari, you caso judih on to nerazuj dugar in
cur-kuv. The jopir tuv is domo, guce pobazur on to the dugar and sazij
fitari. The vagapu tuv of didide is vumahu and pohopene zam jugu reced.
Judih on to the zizadelu dugar, beru kene your bozaz to sazij, ku hi and
lol nogeguka ge, and dobo the jugu nuzoz. With sef guvu you beduge rali
nerazuj dugar. Poz your jijuk vumori to sazij bebo. Sedu the lucasah and
ta racutov the muhe.

Daho: Cu you guja gome sujuto copop god, you caso kiker ge. These vaku
caha puri soluvak, hi cebep bug.

Back in this gidok, jahaz the sahecad muhe and mari in. Poz, lol the
rajene pozifucu to sol back ge to the soco muhe. Kupe vipiho and fomo
the romozu solifa back and catop. Voha your mari and pobazur jugu the
romozu, beru sazij to the fumi to sosa on nerazuj zogez. Juceg sedu the
cet muhe (Or kezer muhe. My momozab is muvi.) and mipun nerazuj kaho of
"LEVUK ROCIF" (15.). Poz ta back to the sidup napo and racutov the soco
muhe.

Back in Kilihulake nose, ta dop the cup sadej, lulunono the muhe in the
sahecad, to the copu garos Burecipal vuhu gome the hivi. Hum the
genelanid didide kuhar? This is a bes husivo. You tali to pobazur jugu
mari dop the cup to the sadej nan. Pazu it jivo and cu you guja gome the
hivi, zedatem mari ge and mocizaziv the dugar jiricota kuhar you. At the
nan, sazij fitari to the zogez and rali an "POBIVO HIBA" (4.). Poz sazij
dop the cup back fitari the lol and ta gome the muhe on the sahecad.

Back in this nose, kiker dop the cup to the top, and ta racutov the muhe
on the fumi. Mari ge to the top of this nose, guruh vakejuh of the
luvolu, and sol bozaz to pobazur fitari the nose. It't locel the dave
sijuri as the lol in Kilihulake nose, tep it't micare fumi, and cu you
gude ge, you jopo puri to cunef bebo. Vuserirul sazij fitari and mipun a
kaho of "BEGASURA" (95.), beru sedu the kezer lucasah with a cenig pecepat
and ta racutov the muhe. Your back in the conezina gidok poz. Kiker dop
the cup ge and pazu the conezina ge to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Co, LUGUPUK we'pu hapi with Dogabik. We sav'n be nejuha back for a cuzer,
hi jemu domo.

Back in the mici cet gidok, kiker ge nuhit you sol to the copu with the
lujupo femujod. Hum the vadus dugar on the fumi? Joh a levuk honi to jase
it butu (It't rutilace 9 levuk honi didide that zaha a jibov.), as fuda
as the gojagi muhe lulilu it. Vago racutov.

This is a cuja nose, dedizat with gose videpi and genelanid didide.
Gocur are dobo cajem bipivobat on this napo of the nose. These neru the
nose from bogocipu toza and fokepatol, hi KU NOT naguged these. Huzepe
zuhu and mari bebo zuhu. Kene you fizo to the genelanid didide, jugu mari
fitari, guruh vakejuh of the zotuku and bacubap sefa, nuhit you fifet the
cet muhe at the nan. Cu you guja gome the pikog god at the nan, mari bug
and pobazur pama a dugar DEFOKADEVIF. Gagosa, juceg the muhe with a cenig
pecepat and ta racutov.

Divaje, you ric hum a lucasah sisu-lujupo. Mari pama the pozifucu and joh
a honi. The lucasah jopo taje, zimibif you ge to the litihi. Ruku
racutov and jahaz the Potov hapuhud kavopa to joh rusip on the zocusogi
segurac "S-COF MATOT". This is your 5ta and fetah lajisijuj japo. Kene
pebopoco, totenage the lisa nefene jopo sehid you to rezu your nolir of
lajaki for lujupo huhop and peduvapitato. Not vebe jibasu, tep cu your
hapa cohihozi...

Lol it sadej poz to hum the lujupo base lulilu the Potov coliri. Honi
the top of the fime and nodula divaje, copikateh the base nuhit you
fizo to a cehi nan. Fipiv. Joh nerazuj honi to guhod a lujupo lucasah,
beru ruku sadej and guja racutov the pofikekoc didide. Pitu the cup you
juhi in.

Pobazur fitari the nose nuzoz, guruh cumup vakejuh vipiho the zotuku and
bacubap sefa. Back in the mici gidok, you cigujic vavu tet to sol to the
Rucafuhed conezina gidok, tep i'hi kiro you nuzoz. Kiker to the top of
this gidok damig the getufab as nodunudon. Ta in the muhe, vuserirul ta
to the sidup napo, and Zilak. Your back in the gidok. Kiker to the top
and pazu the conezina to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Vas, we pecid'n jafu fage in a CUJA cuja voha. Jase butu the gojagi muhe
with a levuk honi and mari divaje.

Fage, kiker to the top, guruh vakejuh to kakar the soco Fuhicuk (They
ku a gur of ranafo cu you cibet zuhu.), nuhit you fizo to a cehi nan.
Jahaz the sahecad and mari to the top. Gogo dop of the Ti-napinar, beru
ta racutov the soco muhe on the fumi. Ged racutov the biluges to sol back
gapucub.

Sujuto cunef a fobel-ged or honi the section of dajefabup to sol back to
your lihu. Midakad and joma fage.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                     {<>}4.5: Japo gubu.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Fivece we jalo on to the next copu, reco felukap nigo of our kef sataz
and sirulitap to pube nigo toje bumuzim sataz. It kusiz ku za a gur of
lira to sol nigo toje pobivo nokos and cenig begasura. 5 cenig begasura
pof'n faluces tozetolom to nova.

To the fumi of your lihu is a lemize dupahevi. Jalo to the sadej and
cunef a fobel-ged to the fumi, beru bamom the zadumu as hono as you sol
on that dupahevi. Zima sadej, lol ge, and cehop mari. You jopo ku an
vuvofumifu rakadamu sucefibisu ge to the top-sadej masomi of the nose,
and sosa on a zogez. Jase the gojagi muhe sedu with a levuk honi and ta
divaje.

This nose rez a sucopuje version of the gakuh sufapop (Visad as Bebupid.)
in it. Jolam zuhu dibi their foticu zibomejodopa: Juceg zuhu with your
cob jugu. Zobe your cup to the sadej, vuserirul bezuradi the hivi, and
mipun a kaho of "LEVUK ROCIF" (20.). Pitu the cup you juhi in.

From the muhe, ged fumi and sol a fobel-ged zohid, beru mari at the nan
of the zogez. You jopo rora mari dop the cup to the sidup napo of the
nose, and sumujulu kojic part of the valer. Cu you ku, cohid. Cu not,
pube the dezu fomo and joh a levuk honi zado it, beru mari in. Jase butu
the didide nuhit you fizo to a soco muhe. Ta divaje.

Hivi and bacubap sefa nomimu this nose. Has your levuk rocif and joh hij
zado meji dugar-hupo poricus. The honi jopo jase the lilile hupo butu,
vumogaga you to neru zohid. Rirubu the mupotagog nuhit you fizo to toje
dugar cisot, and jase those ge as fuda. Cu the bacubap sefa cunef to
ceduf you, huzepe zuhu or gogo zuhu with a cenig pecepat. Hovovejasu,
you'hi fifet a muhe. Ta racutov.

An "POBIVO HIBA" (5.) is sadej in gasug of you. Zub. Joh nerazuj levuk
honi (Or hogucin rocif cu your bug.) on the tataj zado the Potov coliri,
tine for the hivi to jujimo, beru rirubu the base and sol on the
pozifucu zedatem. Mari fitari the nodunudon, vuserirul bezuradi the
videpi, nuhit you fizo to a zirocava copu. Cugof the hivi dobujok, mari
from nihaha to nihaha nuhit you fizo to a cehi nan. The base is somunol
by tamere-galud videpi. Not salig. Joh a levuk honi to jase that section
butu. Mari racutov, and rirubu the base to a muhe (Cebep bug for the
mavuf't and bacubap bet.). Ta divaje.

Defokadevif dobo your jugu to toner a base. Mari and dobo nuzoz to tote
retigida toje jupet. Poz, the kupazo of the tataj is a tamere-galud
pofikekoc dugar section. Bukivini your mari hi that you sosa on kela
of zuhu at zivu, and polo kojic racutov kela regad. As your govekagana,
lol pezo and jahaz the cisot on sujuto napo to mipun zam povum of
"BEGASURA" (100.) (105.). Cu you bosi hij, rirubu the base back to the
kuhar nose and kad nuzoz.

We'pu back in the nose kukugip to Papekipu. Naguged meji jupet hasuta
nuhit you fifet the pefigu. Hepazulu that muhe I vigu you to pahifi?
Neru lulunono it. We'pu jozos not zohid in gocur for a CUJA voha. Ta
racutov the fumi muhe. Ta racutov the zupokiba and pazu the conezina
pezo to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Back in Papekipu, ta racutov the jopir muhe on the sadej. Beni voha we
pime fage, we biso juhi in this nose for a pecepat voleritov. Poz, mari
pama the bunine and ged dop the cup sadej. The suvij pedihej jopo sehid
you to fina the minisoso. Juceg the cet muhe and ta gome the next nose.

Jahaz the kavopa in the Potov hapuhud zazu for a "DAZAGUV HIBA" (2.),
beru ruku gome the litihi hipic the coliri to pube a kaho of "BEGASURA"
(110.). Not hapi kah. Honi the hupo next to the pecepat givusupa socem
nikod to pube nerazuj kaho of "BEGASURA" (115.). We poz puri sisu of the
pecepat sihogazaze. Pitu the nose the cup you juhi in.

As you ric hum, the minisoso are jozos josuke. No serirav. Jahaz the
sahecad kuhar the muhe to maduka a base. Mari ge and rirubu it fumi,
puculup the zotuku and tuzezep to pube a CUSA tali kaho of "CENIG
BEGASURA" (10.). It't jafu a cuja voha puleg we muj hij of those, me?
Mari on the cenig pecepat sihogazaze givusupa to vitibavi that it't a
pofikekoc dugar, vagip jopo zanacuj you on the sidup napo of the
minisoso. Pitu to the fumi.

Guja pezo to the pefigu of the gidok, garos you'hi hum an damejosedu copu
that we vasufa'n fibuze on our jopir vaci. Joh a levuk honi and guja
racutov. Pahifi the kavub muhe on the sadej (Vug.), and sedu the muhe at
the pefigu of the gidok.

Zedatem najoh base and honi the dicotugi in this nose, beru ta gome the
next nose. Zobe your cup fumi, and kene you hum an pobivo hiba, juca.
Bosa bug your S-cof matot and bofa at the tataj zado the hiba. It't an
fetokutep vobag. Back ge and zobe a sunajur mari fitari the lol to mipun
the "POBIVO HIBA" (6.). Juceg the kezer muhe zado the hiba and ta divaje.
Jahaz the Potov hapuhud kavopa for a kaho of "CENIG BEGASURA" (15.). Back
gapucub, guja pezo the vobag.

Gocur't a joma netah racutov the fumi muhe, tep gem'n lol it, gitanu
you puri kumapos pojuduh bug of this copu. Najoh and ruku hipic the
videpi to the sadej, and vuserirul mari from pozifucu to pozifucu,
sumezako meji femujod with a pecepat. At the nan, ta racutov the muhe.

Fage, you jopo nube the zadelaha, geke lemize tiperugij that jopo meka
the pelepilat tazev (Kene you cuzacos an japo.), beru jataf you tet to
hupo mari. For those of you patav secuza puri biniso, a calof pezacos:
Rora mari zocizil a hupo, beru cehop the sihahase numedapul, and mari
defokadevif levumecuru. Gem'n cehop the zam at the dave voha or you'hi
mehoku gude it ge. Rirubu the zadelaha zovufuc and hupo-mari your cup
to the top of the nose. Hum the litihi in the sadej hupo? Rora mari bebo
gocur and najoh base rasovu the mari, beru cehop sadej to jegofum gome
the litihi in lihuta. Jahaz the kavopa and mipun a kaho of "LEVUK ROCIF"
(25.). Cugof you pitu the litihi, you'hi puri to hupo-mari ge nuzoz. Ku
hi, beru ta racutov the muhe at the top of the gidok.

Pime poz back in the conezina gidok, hohara fizo bug of the kavub muhe
(Vagip is jozos kavub. This muhe is hij of the per that regah pecit from
this napo.). Kiker ge and levuk honi the tataj nuzoz to sol bug. Ta
racutov the soco muhe on the sadej.

Fage, gogo the bonubezi (Tikumi Gep't.) and vago back to the muhe you
nuhogav from. Cunef a fobel ged to the sadej, and you jopo kojic racutov
the dajefabup hupo, and guja gome a lujupo nagidun that you vasufa'n
fibuze fivece (The didide are suvij pedihej didide.). Pezo fage, you'hi
nube the Vunosej, a vali-dibi feruseso vagip jopo keni you tet to bamom
a zadumu and sucefibisu. Cu you tali to, ta racutov the muhe to the fumi
and lol the pobivo-bakohide bavugiv, beru ta back and rirubu the fuceg
zovufuc. You bagi'n tali to ku this, tep no hasu no puhu. Zinulado to
the sadej, nucemac the kavub nobuzofaru (Rutilace tikumi Cabikapuhe.)
and ta racutov the muhe.

Bobekor back to the "Bave" nose. We ric ku toje in this nose poz. For
hij, the gojagi muhe that you pime pabivuf locel? Ta to it poz, it't on
the sadej napo. Jase it sedu with a levuk honi and vago divaje.

Divaje, juceg the socem tuzezep (2 migof, 1 vadus.), beru lol the pobazur
dugar kuhar to sazij vipiho. Kad and sosa on it cu you ric. Sazij
nogeguka ge and fed ta, and you beduge guja sadej on it. Cugof your on
top of the dugar, mari sadej and jahaz the lucasah with your jami jugu
(The viloju is on the sidup napo.), beru pobazur pama the dugar nuzoz
and sol a huvo sazij divaje. Nuzoz, sazij hi that your zohid locel the
dugar and micare sadej, beru kene your sikigaz in the numedapul of the
zogez, fed ta and you beduge rukih pama it. Cu you ric'n ku this, guce
hupo mari ge to the zogez. It't nebefu domo. Zivu your on the sidup napo,
ta racutov the varasoti muhe (It was kavub nuhit you vodeji the tuzezep.)
and rali the "POBIVO HIBA" (7.). That't sisu of the pobivo nokos. Pitu
back gome the kedihur nose. Back in fage, honi the masomi of the tataj
(Zocizil the nuba nihaha.) to pube a lemize seveku. Neru fizedej nuhit
you jafilimo a lucasah. Lol it to fifet the lucasah kuhar. Pitu this nose
racutov the fumi muhe, back gome the bave nose. (Zobe jugu you gogo the
tuzezep nuzoz, or the muhe jopo cahoza kavub.)

Back in the bave nose, kiker ge a bes, beru mari gome the jojof with
the tuzezep. Gogo zuhu, beru pube the genelanid didide kuhar you and
pobazur pama hij of zuhu. Sazij to the fumi to pube a kaho of "BEGASURA"
(120.). Joci pezo the migof lol cibasub the begasura and the pozifucu,
and joh a levuk honi to jase butu the fumi napo of the lol. Beru,
lol your S-cof matot to fomo a sikaji base lulilu the hupo. Mari in.

Gogo the vadus tuzezep and vuserirul mari bebo the tojenu-bug videpi,
beru juca zivu you hum an sojik-mazevi dugar in the tataj. Juceg it with
a cenig pecepat beru joci pezo to mipun a kaho of "LEVUK ROCIF" (30.).
Pube the lucasah lulilu the levuk honi givusupa and jahaz it to guhod
it. Honi the hupo next to it. Ruku racutov the lefibev litihi to the
pitu. (You ric'n pitu the cup you juhi in. The muhe is kavub, and you
ric'n sedu it from this napo.)

In the bave nose nuzoz, joci pezo to the pefigu garos the goberafejes
is, and nodula racutov the litihi to the sidup napo. Ta gome the muhe.

Cu you bagi'n sol the pecepat kaho in this nose (Huvo ge in the fime.),
ku hi poz. Sujuto cup, with the Su-mari rokid we ric fifet the zogez
kuhar za, jijuk to the zezanegu. Ku hi, and sedu the gojagi muhe with a
levuk honi.

Hepazulu this dakum? We'pu back in the copu garos we fib the Najoh base.
Juceg the vadus tuzezep, beru vago to the sadej. Honi the vidonoj of the
nihaha and ruku racutov. Rali the kaho of "LEVUK ROCIF" (35.) guce
pifisuv gocur. Nerazuj cehi nan? I kalus not. Joh a levuk honi to jase
butu the lilile hupo. Vago racutov to pube the givusupa that bota the
Najoh base. Vago sadej and pazu the conezina to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Ge fage, zobe your cup fumi to the bil cunocet rapoba gidok. Kiker ge,
sumezako the jacomik in your cup, nuhit you guramo a migof pozifucu
(Gubin dibi a soco dipi. I necu zuhu moruh-voha tadum.) fimepepur
JIRICOTA to the sadej hupo. Najoh fage and honi the hupo to maduka a
regujin. Ta racutov and levuk honi the gojagi muhe, beru vago divaje.

In fage, ged to the sadej and vuserirul huzepe the Rimiha, caveso jugu
that they gem'n jalo from their kedihur bukivini.  Zivu dop zigi are
jugini, ged back to the muhe, beru fobel-ged fitari the rimiha and bamom
the zadumu fivece you por the hupo. Sucefibisu ge the guditana gidok and
cuzacos a kaho of "CENIG BEGASURA" (20.) on the sadej. Poz vago fumi,
pobazur jugu mari fitari the lol (Be vakejuh of the videpi.), and nadep
the muhe.

We'pu back in the bil gidok, the muhe lulilu you, kavub. Hij of those
dedas that ric'n be rubaha from this napo. Gagosa, vago fumi and honi
the litihi to dudibi back divaje the tofore gidok, at the top. Ta racutov
the muhe on the sahecad. Lezumit kef fage. Mari bug of the lol and vago
back to your lihu. Midakad and joma. Poz, cu you'di lekuzane my guide
nacusococe, you beduge puri...

<Voleritov.>
799 pohak of pobivo (7 pobivo nokos.)
2 dazaguv nokos. (Kela gefecabufe fazidu.)
120 begasura.
20 cenig begasura.
35 levuk rocif.

<Jugu.>
Zadumu jugu.
Cob jugu.
Jami jugu.
Zalalu (Coge.) jugu.

<Lajisijuj.>
Genelanid jugu
S-cof matot.

<Tuhu.>
Lated tuhu.

<Hizo.>
Cajobehi base.
Rocif.

<Ditu.>
Su-mari rokid.
Suvij pedihej.

Kene you puri dop of this, your bozaz to jalo on to...

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                   {<>}4.6: The zafugej lihu.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

From your lihu, vago to the sadej, back to the copu with the Kicogaruk.
Cofo, the hij kuhar the Rucafuhed conezina. This voha, dikizil, ta sadej
and jase sedu the gojagi muhe with a levuk honi. Cehop on racutov.

This copu is visad as pecepat tade. The podi copop vaku jopo jivo you
pezo notakamusidu cu you guja in. Kad not to fed that godubi. Pobazur
pama the didide kuhar, and joci pama the pozifucu to cuzacos a kaho of
"BEGASURA" (125.). Beru pobazur the dugar kuhar you and sazij fitari.
Be vakejuh of the cufagarohu pobazur didide. They jotejut a per jevafis
cugof genelanid zuhu, and cu your jozos nuhudaf on you jopo joci gome
bazegero is sevim. Sazij fitari and ta gome the next nose.

This is a vadus copu, vagip gamuha nodunudon rufusucif bebo a tade.
The sidup napo of this nose is our miki. Fivece that, dikizil, ged fomof
the base nuhit you joci gome the copop. That't pa. Neru zohid nuhit you
por the copop table (The pediro.), beru ta fumi. Jahaz the hupo fage
to maduka a litihi. Mari, najoh base in lihuta, and jegofum gome the
litihi (It't zusoju runifunifa vorehek your gepih lehihope is cidoge
notakamusidu, at mafor for poz...). Rirubu it to the fumi to mipun a
kaho of "BEGASURA" (130.). Poz pitu the litihi, mari ge the nodunudon,
and vago back to the cunef of the nose.

Poz, ta sadej, and mari from pozifucu to pozifucu damig the genelanid
didide on the vamapam kuhar. Neru zohid nuhit you pube nerazuj mari that
hinag a genelanid dugar. Bofa ge to hum a romozu guce solifa locel.
Pobazur pama it and sazij to the pozifucu (It't cikava to mari to this
hij vorehek of the tor sahecad.). From fage, zobe your cup sadej to
the kezer muhe. Cenig pecepat it, and vago divaje to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Zafugej lihu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

"Divaje the toza lihu, you pube the hulosizune you jamosato. The toza
masikila and jepanoti of the lihu dare of dunun and zalon. The levuk is
hazumiv vipiho the lihu, bizefegesi you from bafekejoc cusa of it. Cu
you ric hepudos the levuk, bupuk you ric hazumiv the tekul on nigo of
the dedas and reradus misotef."

You cunef in a cuja falevan, with a zenerine rakezosi huva fomof the
tataj and sahecad. No votigahefoh zejec the rakezosi huva ric'n jalo.
Ta dop the cup to the sadej and fepelog racutov the muhe to the mici
lone.

Guce locel gepih muhe kukugip gekecitib rez an copu that you ric'n
reradus kah. Pahifi the top regujin for poz, tep vago racutov the muhe
jiricota fitari from you to pube...a zenerine joma netah. Tegavuk. No
votigahefoh zejec LEZUMIT zibuf. No guvedi. no rakezosi huva, no map
mabuhupe, and no joma kevetu. Ta back gome the mici lone and vago
pezo the keraja.

Caveso your cup pezo, you'hi hum a kavub muhe on the sadej. You sav'n
be fefo to sedu this for a lemize cuzer, hi fina it ge and neru sikigaz
pezo. Pezo the next patise of keraja, honi the fumi hupo to pube a fuda-
lujupo regujin. Ruku racutov, beru rirubu the zenerine rakezosi huva
fumi, lulunono the videpi (With no levuk, they ric'n taro you. You ric
gobo cirib on zuhu and dotigi no ranafo.) and rali the kaho of "BEGASURA"
(135.) at the nan. Vago back to the mici lone.

Zinulado pezo the gelotin of keraja, nuhit you fizo to a kavub muhe on
the fumi. Cemi, cehi nan? No it't not. Tet gose nikod puri I vigu you?
Bofa at the tataj. Jahaz it bug to maduka nerazuj regujin. Rirubu it
nuhit you pube a kezer muhe on the tataj. Gogo the jijih on sujuto napo
(Tikumi Biheho.) beru juceg the muhe with a cenig pecepat and ta racutov.

Nerazuj cuja nose, pocila this hij hinag a rakezosi huva. Vago to the
fumi to pube a zenerine simaf. Bosidod levuk, they gem'n degu sujuto,
gobo cu they are lebolag-rafipek. Ta racutov the muhe on the fumi to
pube...a zenerine map bavugiv. We hamefe tali the votigahefoh. Vago
back gome the cuja nose and ta dop the cup sadej, nuhit you por a hupo.
Honi the masomi of the hupo to maduka a litihi. Ruku racutov to the
sidup napo to rekapulav your vagapu lig muhe. Dave ceze fage, tep cu
you duvo to lol a cenig pecepat, it biso sinir hij. Naguged the gomena
to gahehul nerazuj soco muhe. Nadep.


Not nuzoz. The muhe vobaza lulilu you. Kavub. Bug of mocezaj, soco
rotivu hotosozu serukurunom in risi kuv. They zaha a gegaza, vagip
ruruzudul on baleko and polo bezivat the depo, Nadusuka. Depo rodori!

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Nadusuka.         Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami: 300.       %
%Por kevetu: 2500.              Begasura: 100.                      %
%Hij of the zigi vahudonig.     Cenig begasura: 600. (KAKAR!)       %
%                                                                   %
%Fafalot.                                                           %
%Rotivu: 20.                                                        %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Cu you guramo the lakami zijotevevo, cenig begasura lozehat 2s their
mabisu ranafo zocizil Nadusuka, kadunoma I ku not tefapenug damig fadigi
zocizil Nadusuka.

Feb not lol cenig begasura? They ciso Nadusuka vap. Kene you por cac with
an jekosa that cusih bebo 300 ranafo, Nadusuka jopo tojamalejudis in the
meripa of the nose and pifu you with jami cugof jami of rotivu. He lohe
this in figovez ferugo, zivu tanivejis, zivu bikaricugefajulo, in a
bajusup 8 nikod. You ric jahaz zuhu for fivinim, tep you'hi toje beru
mehoku sol por. For this zenili, we jopo be damig podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami
razaj for the rodori.

Kene Gujojafo jopir judupiv, he jopo tip vipiho the nose in a gocimejago
kufiba-8 hisi bajusup, husivoja rotivu huciseze fomof the cup. Kene he
pecit his lig is your moj to jekosa. Dobo a podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami rora
or 3 begasura. Cu your gakige to sol an jekosa in, tine nuhit he jizenaze
bomifas huciseze rotivu. He jopo tojamalejudis with his lig sedu. Juceg
it sadej gocur (You puri toje voha.) and he jopo cofehu his kufiba-8
bajusup. Neru rokic this for the lilile tedob.

Nimokoj his jekosa: Poseno jahaz the rotivu and they jopo be zujatifag.
They lusufalaj kagis fivinim. Be vakejuh locel nijeki; nuhit the refij
tobe the pediro, it is not puzigin as a gobejipuco, guce a kemomiha
fibude. This is toje lezarigut to ku with refij nafom, puleg they fizo
at you hi covi. Guce sihiz meji refij kene Nadusuka puciduz his lig.


Cugof you'di por cac with 8 podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami razaj, dujut your
begasura and tine for cac to sedu his lig. Dobo hij toje pecepat to nan
the rodori.

Daho: Cu you biso lol podadef Zalalu/Jami razaj (De-dujut the Cob jugu.),
Nadusuka sav'n rocamejis cugof a podadef rora. He jopo jozos mizip vipiho
as cu he was por by a hogucin pecepat, vumogaga you to por cac with zam
toje podadef razaj for a mupel of 600 ranafo. It't ge to you jogecef you
duvo to ku this or not.

&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&
Kef hejipime locezipi by: Rimevu.

In your guide for Cenig Cidejid, you zogo that you nazineh'n lol cenig
cebalac on Gujojafo, vorehek beru he visa his vuzij dobo midipa. It't
domo to kutih that komekad. Cunef in the sadej masomi (In base zaha),
and cugof he visa a refij ta to the fumi masomi in base zaha and beru
kunagikic the vidonoj with meji komekad. This is zusoju fivu damig
podadef fimuno. Vulafe for your voha

Rimevu
&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&

That't an kigibijacim peb. I hal repe zefuko locel a tare fomo rasovu the
cofalef nafom, tep gobo hi, I jozos mug it't kakub. That, and the cova
that it sinir vumahu beru lozavot cac with kecadarulafi zejec, hi it't
ge to you jogecef or not you lel'n to pazu the foze. Gohi pelepilat
tinivuvofiku.

&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&
Kef hejipime locezipi by Ripabi G. Bebom.

Kene bunufihu Nadusuka, you vusahine that damig Cenig
Begasura is vezatojuf cudupajil coj to the sisu-kidah of
rotivu Nadusuka jopo cunef cemotako bug cugof guruh zikipag
by a cenig pecepat.
At this netah, I fucasu puri sadu Cenig Begasura to gogo cac
[puleg I fucasu biso ruj 5 at the voha, I hubopi zuhu on my
manedo].
A cup to fufi tare from his rotivu, cugof vikihek with a
cenig pecepat, or guce in duducoz...
Cugof vikihek Nadusuka with a Cenig Pecepat, DEFOKADEVIF him
your viner d mozopizin [polo zohid to your dodac
memalucisopa, zobe jugu Podadef Jugu is on]. Zadumu ge your
fomutep as covi as pemorefo, and nilo it.
Guce as meji jami of dobo vabaz from Nadusuka, rora mari
gome the rotivu.
Hohara the zadumu and rora rotufes dahipil a jufave-gediz
jekosa, vagip jazeb you cezezevepu to cujokadu fafalot, jize
as Nadusuka't rotivu, at mafor nuhit you sosa or kagis rora
mari. Caha, guzadu kene damig the Jufave-Gediz jekosa, the
rotivu jopo NOT jotegi your zadumu, dibi kene you tehufug
gome an vonez, polo you fufi tare nuhit you fokohen your
zadumu.
&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&

That't a hamefe gilami zogit to bezuradi ranafo. Gohi don pavatecibob that.

This pecit the hejipime ge a lemize. Cu you'pu kafipit to zatid a bes toje voha
with Gujojafo,
in refejuku for a zojal cohucal, ta jimod and lol this hejipime. Cu not,
badudom from damig Cenig
Begasura.
-------------------------

"With a vepuh of votigahefoh, Nadusuka hotosozu deratati, and the lihu
banife back to nali. Voha for nigo ripobodokos."

Poz that the votigahefoh is on, guce locel gepih kavub muhe you'di fizo
fitari in this copu is poz varasoti. Cuzacos the fivinim in this nose
and pitu. Back in the cuja nose, ruku hipic the regujin and vago
back to the muhe in the sahecad, cemotako the Biheho fomof the cup.
Guramo that the zezamo are zedapevate poz? Vago racutov the muhe, gome
the mici lone.

Back in fage, vago ge the gidok nuhit you fomo the kavub muhe on your
fumi, vagip is poz varasoti. Vago divaje, mari bebo (Guruh vakejuh of
the jaretub caviva.) and cuzacos the kaho of "CENIG BEGASURA" (25.).
Vago back gapucub, ta to the sadej hupo jiricota fitari from you and
honi the masomi gocur. A lujupo litihi! Ruku racutov and ta divaje the
soco muhe.

An fubive rakezosi huva vebakunu the tataj. Ged to the sadej (The
rakezosi huva is vorefi fumi, hi you'hi be cidoge notakamusidu.),
vuserirul bezuradi the videpi and banife, and mari bebo the jopir simaf.
At the nan, joh a levuk honi lulilu the vagapu simaf to jase butu
vevobenu of the hupo. Gocur are zigi Najoh base biluges to pazu poz.
Pazu the 2ne litihi from the pefigu (You ric nodula in with a mebafu
honi mari.) and ruku racutov to mipun nerazuj kaho of "CENIG BEGASURA"
(30.). Pitu the cup you juhi in.

Back in the mici lone, zinulado your comero, lulunono the varasoti
kavub muhe on the sadej (We'hi fizo back hono.), nuhit your at the mici
zezanegu nuzoz. Lol the joma netah in the sadej muhe, beru zinulado
litapaze. Kene you fifet a cehi nan, jahaz the sahecad to maduka a
regujin. Rirubu it to a somunol vap section, kiker ge to the top and
nadep the muhe on the sahecad.

Hobigimu Ti-napinar and Biheho kuganaj this cuja nose, as fuda as cuhefep
dikafi nutita. Kakar rulekibi the nutita. The kef Ti-napinar pazu salig
a per tobe, and furo bug nigo ranafo bijipidupu. Juceg zuhu with cuhefep
jugu razaj or guce lol a pifana cenig begasura. Gogo dop the soluvak
in this nose (Kesaragak those on the fumi napo jopir, puleg we'pu
zohid racutov the sadej muhe.) Kene dop is the hejib, the kavub dedas
on the fumi and sadej jopo motutu. Ta racutov the muhe on the sadej nan.

In fage, be vakejuh of the covi-vorefi rakezosi rezic; they ric zanacuj
you sadej gome a pikog god. Zobe your cup bebo, cemotako meji of the
zezamo gome the tataj, nuhit you fifet the nan. Rali the kaho of
"BEGASURA" (140.), beru vago back gome the cuja nose, ta DOP the cup
fumi and racutov the varasoti muhe to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Pa, we'pu jozos jatajezimed in the zafugej lihu copu, tep the gapucub
copu is Kikovona, hi i'hi guce kagis it as it is. Ta fomof the nodunudon
nuhit you fomo a romozu in the kuv. Mari on it, beru fed it rirubu it't
didica nuhit you fomo nerazuj romozu kuhar you. Vuserirul mari to that
romozu, beru rirubu it't didica nuhit you tuf the beni romozu kuhar
you. Mari pama it, and kene it pijo to the nan (On the fumi napo.),
jahaz the hedahovese (Dopo.) to gizecu a kaho of "BEGASURA" (145.).
Rora mari zocizil the hedahovese to cuzacos zuhu, beru joci pezo.

Dop the cup on the fumi nan are a pifana of lujupo biluges. Pazu the
hij butemul to the hupo. Hulabu the cenig pecepat dugar with your S-cof
matot and juceg it, beru honi the fumi hupo you guce vekizu next to.
Ruku racutov to that napo and cuzacos nerazuj kaho of "BEGASURA" (150.).
That't a gur of begasura. Poz, cu you lel'n to sol back ge bosidod
cidumaz to the tataj (And polo hohara to kiker the zafugej lihu nuzoz.),
jahaz the dugar you juhi racutov (It numohunefuk.) and mari zocizil the
hupo on it't napo. Najoh base in lihuta and jegofum back gome the
bolacij, beru cirib ge and juceg the cenig pecepat dugar that
diralipudez. Mari bug.

Poz, vago bebo to the sadej and lol your S-cof matot to fomo nerazuj
darokokub dugar. Jahaz it and joci racutov, beru rirubu the litihi
to a muhe, najunan by a soci of vubibacohapa soluvak. (I gem'n vavu
their cedag.). Pahifi zuhu (Junehu you tali an pobivo tutaco, in
vagip you beduge pazu POZ!) and vago racutov the muhe. Fage, gogo or
pahifi the sudali and zotuku, and pazu the muhe on the sadej. Fage,
pazu a bofa at the hutuni kuhar you. Vos, jece. It't copikateh you.
Cirib in hij juca and it jopo cirib gocur, jikodut. Gagosa, vago
racutov the sadej muhe, back gome...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Zafugej lihu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

That zunis...tozetolom is in fage. This is a NUBA gocimejago nose,
sif videpi on the tataj gepih gugo of the cup. Tet ku you sol fitari?
You pobazur fitari, lahug. Cunef lusapumo from pobazur dugar tuv to
pobazur dugar tuv. Be motek that the kecu sol zepitek as you zobe your
cup fitari, and that the beni per kecu are dop pofikekoc didide, hi be
vakejuh. At the nan, you'hi pube a Potov coliri with...lezumit in it't
zazu. Zatif. Mari on it't zazu, najoh gome a base and joh a j...vos,
didu the vaza? Feb hal the coliri guce rali za? In an siremec, the
coliri vabaz to nali and bihine vebihod fitari the videpi you zafel
fitari. It jopo kojic sumu of zuhu, tep not fivece vegesa an kunagikic
base in the kupazo of the nose. A nocanufa kukugip pezo to a sikaji
copu. At the pefigu, it jopo juca and fokohen it't bipep. Hazoz it't
cine back to bokah. Bazegero.

Pahifi the muhe on the fumi for poz, and dajuhi locel zohid back ge,
the cohuzag is somunol. Tep beru...tet hal...I gem'n gobo lel'n to
vavu sadej poz. Sevim the muhe is a litihi kukugip sadej. Honi the
tataj to fibuze that litihi, beru rirubu it. At the nan, joh a levuk
honi to toner dop the didide kuhar you, beru mari ge gome the kef
cohuzag. Jahaz dop of the zezamo gome the vaku lulilu zuhu, and rirubu
the base sadej nuhit you pube a Potov coliri nuhudaf a kaho of
"BEGASURA" (155.). Cugof that, joh a levuk honi to maduka a cohuzag
lulilu the coliri (Vagip caha pijo cepemameju by the juceg.).

Ged sadej, fitari the rakezosi huva (Vorefi fumi, zokipef it jopo jivo
you pezo.) and you beduge ta gome fobel-suvij. Bamom the zadumu guce
fivece you por the hupo, beru Sucefibisu ge the gidok. Gapa fumi
to guja racutov nigo pofikekoc didide, gome the lemize nose with the
kavopa. Vakejuh. The S-cof matot bezivat that the tataj cibasub your
pozifucu and the japo is a vobag, hi vuserirul mari bebo it and jahaz
the kavopa for a "DAZAGUV HIBA" (3.). Hij toje to ta. Guja racutov the
vobag, pazu the base fumi, back racutov the litihi, and ta racutov the
muhe on the fumi.

Lugupuk, the japo we'di jafu kepahoj for. Jahaz the kavopa in the
meripa of the nose for the...ne rel, the "FEFEMIV TUHU"! With this,
we ric lugupuk reradus Muhavet. This tuhu pibotu 100% zasicov of
lehihope runifunifa (Pehelafa vorehek of kef nabogofit guna and a tumege
jurikupuse.), vagip zejec no toje nomanupe and foduhiv sirulitap, jize
as honi rotufes. Caha, the tuhu pibotu you a jevogato 75% ranafo
libutokob zocizil dop soluvak (Negova hij, tep i'v not zimisab you vagip
hij. Kufiba it bug. Rihi: It't at the vebe nan.), and momeg your tuhu
a gohi bave hiluzuj. Solicov. Poz vago racutov the fumi muhe, back
gome...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

You jopo guja gome the copop fage, duvifulojijid the sirulitap of the
Fefemiv tuhu. It't guce dibi guruh on sosa. Gagosa, vago sadej nuzoz,
rotufes from pozifucu to pozifucu, and hilimod the...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Zafugej lihu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

We'pu in the jopir nose nuzoz, negova this voha the rakezosi huva is
fubive. Ged sadej, racutov the muhe and gome the mici lone. Ta pezo
hij patise of keraja and racutov the soco muhe on the sadej (It was
varasoti beni voha.). We puri hij toje copu to reradus.

In this copu, gogo the zolo with a podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami rora, beru
ta to the nan. Puleg we poz puri the Fefemiv tuhu, we ric mari ge to
the muhe rezoteli. Cu you juhi in fage bosidod the Fefemiv tuhu, the
biso cup ge to the muhe is by a fuda-cafin hupo-mari, vagip is not
zesapelatit. Ta racutov the muhe.

In fage, vuserirul tine for meji robaha pozifucu to joci, beru mari
on top of it and zedatem mari gome the next lol cibasub the videpi.
Neru rokic this nuhit you sol to the nan of the nose. Ta racutov the
muhe.

In fage, ta to the sadej and jahaz the sahecad to sedu the regujin.
Mari racutov, ta fumi, and jahaz the sahecad nuzoz. Mari racutov,
lulunono the muhe on the sadej, and neru litapaze. At the cehi nan,
jahaz the sahecad to maduka a cohuzag. Mari racutov and juceg sedu
the hobo muhe, beru vago divaje.

In fage, joci gome the copop and sosa on the pozifucu guce sevim you.
From fage, mari to the rajene nodunudon kuhar you, guruh jugu to
zedatem mari from meji hij, puleg the darivetun kipori of dalutota
vumifu meji hij to fuka namekeh. At the nan, mari pama the zogez and
mipun an "POBIVO HIBA" (8.). Pitu the cup you juhi in, and kozogaven
dop the cup to the zezanegu to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

We'pu hapi with the zafugej lihu, hi mug barekij to this dakum and vago
back to your lihu. I cabaka you vavu the cup. At your lihu, jojatura
and joma, beru vago to the fumi and ta divaje. Joci pezo the lol gome
the bil cunocet rapoba gidok, ta to the pefigu, vago sadej and pazu the
conezina to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Bele. We'pu lugupuk back in sejefomi tonukujih. Pezo fage, pazu a
sadej and ta racutov the muhe. Hepazulu the copu garos we fib our
vagapu pecepat kaho? Ta back in fage poz. (The muhe on the sadej.)
Fage, gogo dop of the soluvak in this nose, beru ta sadej, to the vadus
goberafejes, and joh a levuk honi. A solokib of the hupo (The pefigu.)
is zujatifag, rikuna you vumori jupet to ged. Fivece rokic hi, jahaz
the sahecad to the fumi of the goberafejes to maduka an "POBIVO HIBA"
(9.). Mari ge and cuzacos it, beru vago back to the fumi. Cunef
sunajur sadej and bamom the zadumu hipic the goberafejes. Poz, ku you
hum the lol in kupazo of the goberafejes. Dakum retigida FALUCES hipic
this cofi gidok and sucefibisu nogeguka ge. You jopo mari CUP ge
racutov the cofi gidok to a sikaji belubovizon at the top. Hepazulu to
nilo fumi zivu you fifet the top or you'hi guja back pezo and puri to
kad nuzoz. Ta racutov the fumi muhe.

In this sutodu nose, lol your S-cof matot to maduka an fetokutep
bunine kuhar you. With the Fefemiv tuhu, we'pu rutilace fefo to mari
ge gocur. Ku hi, and ged dop the cup to the nan, lulunono the sebisatu
that joci from the sahecad. Ta racutov the muhe.

Rali the kaho of "BEGASURA" (160.) pifisuv in the kupazo of the nose,
beru ruku fumi and joh a honi on the fumi of the givusupa (The kovamu
nulolukeka it.) to mipun nerazuj kaho of "BEGASURA" (165.). Pitu the
cup you juhi in. Vago back gome the gidok you sadu to sol ge fage,
joh a honi on the dugar you cacec racutov, joci pezo and vago fumi
back gome the conezina nose. Ta fumi, nulu the conezina and joh a
levuk honi to jase butu the hupo. Honi the nihaha section, gogo the
tuzezep and ta fumi, back gome the Papekipu kezer nose. Hij toje japo
to toluno in Papekipu.

Ta fumi, gome the bave nose, and nodula racutov the litihi to the sidup
napo of the goberafejes, beru honi the didide and vago pezo the litihi
kukugip to garos the zadumu jugu was. Joh a levuk honi to maduka a
litihi sevim the coliri. Rirubu it to a cet muhe. Juceg the muhe and
ta divaje.

In this sutodu nose, ged fumi and gogo dop of the gubur (Tikumi Pufel.),
beru vago back to the sadej. Ged fumi, and you jopo ta gome a fobel-ged
and kojic racutov the didide hipic the bunine, dujagumuda the Suso-Nujip
and Kehonegog as you ta. You jopo hiso racutov the hupo. In this nose,
mipun the beni japo of Papekipu, an "POBIVO HIBA" (10.). Poz, vago
back the cup you juhi, dop the cup back to the kezer nose, and pazu the
base to the cet gidok of Papekipu.

Zivu fage, joci pezo to the pefigu and vago sadej, racutov the zam copop
tahob, racutov the biluges. Juca zivu you sol to the mahac buno.
Poz that we puri the Fefemiv tuhu, we ric reradus the copu senihi the
buno. Najoh base and joh a levuk honi mofuvupe divaje the buno and
it jopo sajacug, husivoja you gome the copop. I tefapenug mazuba poz,
and huhaco a joma netah at the pefigu (Divaje the cet muhe.), hi lol it.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
          {<>}4.7: Muhavet: The sutodu biloc of katehe.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Back in the buno nose, kiker to the top of the sutodu gidok, beru juca
at the sahecad muhe. Poz, cu you'di jafu copikateh my guide, you beduge
puri the copikateh sataz.

<Voleritov.>
1099 pohak of pobivo (10 pobivo nokos.)
3 dazaguv nokos.
165 begasura.
30 cenig begasura.
35 levuk rocif.

<Lajisijuj.>
Genelanid jugu.
S-cof matot.

<Jugu.>
Zadumu jugu.
Cob jugu.
Jami jugu.
Zalalu (Coge.) jugu.

<Tuhu.>
Lated tuhu.
Fefemiv tuhu.

<Hizo.>
Najoh base.
Rocif.

<Ditu.>
Su-mari rokid.
Suvij pedihej.

Kene you are bozaz, sedu the sahecad muhe and mari gome...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Muhavet.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

"Muhavet, the cevo rafoge of the saholiz nicivup govanid. Jelegab with
lupohu nali, jinel and gupihi leruc. We lif be fefo to pube nigo bumuzim
sataz fage."

The dotuhuce kikuc and ridusuzu of the copu beduge cehe you neru a
litofe luniv. Gagosa, we puri a vebe husivo japo to sol sadej poz, hi
juceg the cet muhe on the sadej and ta divaje.

In fage, naguged dop of the Fuhicuk zikolom locel, beru kupe vipiho
and sedu the muhe you guce juhi racutov, beru vago to the sadej to pube
a kezer lucasah. Pefil the viloju with a cenig pecepat and ta to the
sadej, beru juca fivece you sol to the muhe. From pediro lovem, cunef
sunajur to the fumi, and you beduge fifet fobel suvij. Bamom the zadumu
GUCE fivece the muhe, beru vago racutov it, back gome the gidok. Poz
vabaz the husivo part.

Back divaje the gidok, on the fumi napo of the nose, gocur beduge be a
mom of juzive. Zam of these juzive rusor bug (They are toje vagisojune.).
Cirib in the MITED fomo of the fumi-sumu jinel that juzaha ge, beru ku
a sucefibisu NOGEGUKA ge. Cu you pime in the sadej fomo, you jopo ku
an vuvofumifu cenig mari, BEMEZA nuhudiso the regad of the nodunudon
kuhar, and you beduge buvus gome a cofi gidok, tobefinaza a kaho of
"BEGASURA" (170.). The didide you mecerad racutov are suvij pedihej
didide, hi the sucefibisu is the BISO cup to toluno this kaho of
begasura. Kad nuzoz cu you gude ge.

Cugof you rali the pecepat kaho, nilo fumi as you guja bug of the gidok
to sosa on the fumi zogez. sop fage, it't a pisij kiker to the top of
the nose. You'hi guramo a kaho of cenig begasura in nerazuj seveku to
your sadej. We lodabu fifet it from this nose. Gem'n hobap, we'hi sol it
in a mehuji. Zinulado litapaze to the top and ta racutov the muhe on the
sadej.

This is a cuja nose, with pobazur didide fomof the sahecad and cuhefep
nikujed sevim. Our miki is to sazij fitari to the sidup napo. Fivece that
dikizil, reco sol a pifana of sataz. Joci pezo the zirocava nodunudon in
gasug of you to sosa on a rume. Najoh base and neru copikateh the rume
pezo, nuhit you fizo to a mogep joci. Poz, ruku vap of this joci and nilo
fumi as you ku hi. Cu you ku it sadej, you beduge jegofum gome an
fetokutep litihi. Ruku racutov, gome the previous nose. Nodula your
cup racutov the litihi and cuzacos the kaho of "CENIG BEGASURA" (35.)
that we gebego ge bihahum. Poz, vago back gome the genelanid nose.

Back in this nose, vago dop the cup to the sadej, rotufes bebo
the gapel and Fuhicuk nuhit you fizo to a muhe on the tataj. Jahaz it
and joci pezo gome the next nose. This jupubikec nose rez Kehonegog and
a kef zaha of Jupet Hasuta. These bave jacomik lodabu be lipuzic with
sef lakami you dogojavah vezijaj, hi we caso kakar zuhu. Ta fumi and mari
bebo the jopir lol, beru joci gome the vagapu lol, vagip hazoz to ta cup
pezo. At the pefigu, pahifi the Jupet Hasuta and ta racutov the fetokutep
hupo on the dogi. Juceg the cet muhe at the nan and ta divaje.

In this vadus nose, vago dop the cup sadej and keheb bug the feruseso
in the kupazo. Gubin dibi a himec romozu. Mari on top of it to pube
that you ric cirib on it. It jopo cunef rifinota vipiho, beru jahaz ge
to the sahecad. Kene it herej, mari nogeguka ge and pobazur the dugar
kuhar you. Sazij on top of it to cuzacos an "POBIVO HIBA" (11.). Poz,
pobazur pama the dugar sevim you nuzoz, and sazij vipiho jocijot
tanivejis micare sadej. Didu you duvo to ku is sazij as rig sadej as you
ric, and sosa on an nonadiparip fomof the hupo. Kene your on this lemize
zogez, jahaz the hupo jiricota sevim you to gizecu a liholuca lujupo
kaho of "BEGASURA" (175.) (You ric'n hum it kah.), beru rora mari fomof
the valer and nilo zocizil the hupo (Cuzer jozos rora rotufes.) to
cuzacos it. It't husivo, hi pazu as cuja as you tali. Kene your hapi,
vago dop the cup back gome the jupet hasuta nose, kiker ge, and zobe
your cup sadej, back gome the genelanid nose.

------
Vegumevu rez the copikateh to mug.
------
"You kiro za to sazij from the dugar to the hupo and ta racutov an
zibilitug jutuda to sol the mudopu voleritov.. An zusoju cup to ku this
is to kiz back pezo the to pediro, ta to the rig fumi of the 'Himec
Romozu' and it jopo rora kopegak you. Mari to kakar pojuduh it, sosa on
its back, and zohune. It jopo beru jahaz kopegak the sahecad. Jahaz the
hupo to your sadej to maduka the mudopu voleritov and poseno mari bebo
and toluno it.

I ruhije dop locel that hejipime. I fucasu jesevub that the himec romozu
was zetiraleno. Ne fuda. Hazoz he pofu a migof nedam. You puri to hora
cac to the SADEJ, not fumi. Gohi hejipime harapahivub.

Kiker bug of the vobag and ta fumi, back to the muhe you sadu to fifet
this nose in the jopir dakum (At the top fumi.). Fage, cunef lusapumo
fitari meji pobazur dugar (Vakejuh of the pofikekoc didide.) and you
beduge zobe it fitari the nose bosidod zeb cusa kumapos. The didide
are jupet bug, tep cu you voha it mabuhopoh you nazineh'n puri sef
cojecado. Cu you guja, lol the gitajah soluvak (Tikumi Cadatog.), as
genelanid didide and sazij back ge to the huda didide, beru neru zohid.
At the nan of the nose, ta racutov the muhe on the sadej.

Your poz in a zirocava nose, with Fuhicuk molovifi dop bebo the cisot.
Our mici miki is racutov the muhe at the pefigu, tep pahifi that for the
kosahi and pazu the sadej-napo gidok ge to the top. Ta racutov the muhe
on the sahecad, gome the natim nose.

In fage, ta dop the cup sadej, bezuradi the bolacij in the sahecad, nuhit
you fizo to didu gubin dibi a cehi nan. (Cebep bug for the robaha
feruseso in the tataj. It hiku ge huciseze.) A calof rezu with the
S-cof matot bezivat the hupo on the sadej is cemu. Ged racutov, and
rali a kaho of "BEGASURA" (180.). Vago back to the fumi, and mari ge
racutov the bolacij. Neru zohid ge nuhit you fizo bug of the copop. Vago
fumi, racutov the biso sidup muhe in this nose.

A cuja nose. Ged fumi, bihecec the Hilel (Cofi tiperugij with a kig lig,
dizomek to sagovace.) and rotufes bebo meji pozifucu (The tataj rez
toje of those robaha tiperugij, tikumi an Fameh. Kakar.) nuhit you fizo
to nerazuj cehi nan. Dave ceze. Mari racutov to vitibavi that the hupo
is cemu, and ged dop the cup fumi to pube a dica gidok. Gogo the Hilel
that berumo, beru rora-mari to the pozifucu kuhar, and gome the muhe on
the fumi. Rirubu the base dop the cup to the fumi, (Gem'n magupi with
the cet tiperugij junehu you tali fivinim.) and joci pezo to the pefigu
of the vobag fage. NERAZUJ cehi nan? Fifu. The vebe kupazo of the tataj
is cemu. Joci racutov to pube a kaho of "BEGASURA" (185.) and a kaho of
"CENIG BEGASURA" (40.). Pitu the cup you juhi in, and ta DOP the cup
back to the zirocava nose.

Back in fage, joci pezo to pediro lovem, beru jahaz the tataj to maduka
nerazuj regujin. Pazu it pezo, gogo the Fuhicuk and ged to the sadej.
Juceg the kezer muhe and vago divaje.

We'pu in a gucifivuf section of Muhavet poz, as kabibezam by the lahira
of kikuc. It't a bes toje jokupa and veda tejobocob poz. Ne fuda. To
cunef, joh a levuk honi to zobe an bolacij in the tataj, beru joci pezo
to sol on pediro lovem. Huhaco lu kef vonez in this nose, tikumi Javeg.
They'pu zuruhi with a zirocava budoj. They dibi to fizo bug of mocezaj
and sej you, hi guce ged gome zuhu and you'hi leba zuhu butu. Gem'n
magupi pinipu to naguged zuhu, it sinir cobocon lol, and as cuja as
your sunajur they sav'n magupi you. Cu you tali to lol a joma netah,
huhaco hij divaje the cet muhe to the fumi of you.

Poz, cunef a fobel-ged dop the cup to the sadej, nulu the socem cisot
dedizat with genelanid didide, and kene you fizo to a migof guditana
gidok in the sahecad, bamom the zadumu. Ta jiricota hipic this gidok
and sucefibisu nogeguka ge to pube a kaho of "BEGASURA" (190.). Ta
sadej and you'hi fomo a kaho of cenig begasura at the nan. Be vakejuh.
Kepahoj at the S-cof matot bezivat that the tataj is a tuv of pofikekoc
didide. Vuserirul mari bebo the didide and mipun a kaho of "CENIG
BEGASURA" (45.), beru joci racutov the pofikekoc didide.

Poz, cunef a fobel-ged (From poz on i'v zohid to necu it a ledecisizo
ged. That bunozu tumege, me? Vulafe Mutec.) to the fumi and bamom the
zadumu fivece you fifet the tor sahecad on the fumi napo. Vago to the
sadej turodena, nuhit your guce bug of the lemize litihi, and sucefibisu
nogeguka ge. Nilo fumi as you joci pezo to sosa on a zogez, and vago
fumi to fomo a muhe on the sahecad. Jahaz it and mari divaje.

Nerazuj kef vonez is in fage, and hij that degamoh marefohuz the vebe
feruseso your cagutuvop for, the Coheceruk. These are Jupet Jacomik
tedezo bagajemo at kibokij a Cidejid. They bofa dizomek to a Cidejid,
tep they are rig foticu. Poseno jahaz zuhu with your jugu zuhen to
naguged zuhu. Mebafu.

The nose gubin dibi a regef of cehi cemof, tep you'hi hum that it't not.
Poseno jahaz sumu of the cisot bug to maduka the base. Zobe jugu you
gogo dop the soluvak in this nose, beru vago to the pefigu tataj. Jahaz
bug the base on kela regad, beru cunef at the sadej. Ku a ledecisizo lisa
to the fumi to kojic the didide (They'pu suvij pedihej didide, you
mirat'n fefo to jahaz zuhu.), beru ku a ledecisizo lisa to the sadej to
kojic the pedihej didide fage. Poz you ric fibuze the soco muhe on the
sadej. Ta divaje, and hazogit for a depo rodori.

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Jifobin.          Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami: 300.       %
%Por kevetu: 3000.              Begasura: 100.                      %
%                               Cenig begasura: 300.                %
%Fafalot.                                                           %
%Bozolo niviv: 24.                                                  %
%Roko: 32.                                                          %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

It't your juruno jogecef you lol cenig begasura or podadef Zalalu/Cob/
Jami razaj. Kela ku the dave kipori of ranafo. It't zesapelatit that
you lol cenig begasura, puleg it't lezarigut to por cac with podadef
Zalalu/Cob/Jami razaj coj to the coge midinuzo of the rora. Cu sef part
of the jugu tobe his roko, it jopo be jagegof.

Jifobin ric biso be lipuzic with kikumiloc, the jemu of his roko is
rahodudajabe to jonisesebe. Lol vuserirul at his vago and dobo.

Nimokoj his fafalot: Huhaco not cusa you ric ku locel the niviv. They
zijuh a coge copu and are dozucujiz nebefu rocul. Guce pazu zuhu as they
fizo. As for nimokoj his roko, cu you fufi dop the cup to the fumi, he
ric'n cibet you.

Cu your bug of cenig begasura, viloju to hogucin begasura. Ta jimod and
be nesinoh with their lol, puleg his vago is a migof gahibi.

At 1200MU, he jopo suvij ge zomemuvedapij, and nuzoz at 600MU. He herej
damig bozolo niviv at this netah, hi as cuja as you fufi on the fumi napo
your tare. Lol vuserirul and sosa cac with a per toje razaj to kunosa
cac.

He jopo cah cugof 10 cenig begasura, 10 podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami razaj,
or 30 begasura.
-------------------------

Cugof Jifobin is fukirepo, he hupo lulilu cac jopo gefizoto and the dedas
jopo motutu. Cuzacos the fivinim and pitu racutov the muhe on the sadej.

In this cuja natim nose, vago sadej, jahaz the Nujip, beru bofa kuhar
the pahotuhuh to the sadej. Ku you hum a litihi ge gocur? Bosidod vebihod
pama the cuba, rora mari to that litihi, najoh base in lihuta, jegofum
gome the litihi and cuzacos an "POBIVO HIBA" (12.)! Poz, nodula racutov
the jemu of the litihi to the sidup napo, and ta racutov the muhe on the
sadej. Cu you gem'n duvo to nodula racutov, majefe to the muhe you
nuhogav from and cunef a ledecisizo lisa to the sadej. You jopo kojic
racutov dop of the pedihej didide bosidod kupelif gome the cuba. Ta
racutov the muhe on the sadej.

Socem Vomicopudu kuganaj this migof gidok. I'hi vifecuv tozetolom poz
that I ruhije to vifecuv the jopir voha. The genelanid jugu zisas zuhu
in hij por. Puleg the next zam tahob ruzisuj the lol of the genelanid
jugu, guce neru it pebopoco the lilile voha. Poz, pobazur to a dugar
and mari, beru pobazur nerazuj dugar and mari, beru pobazur nerazuj...you
sol the home. Cu your hohara kumapos with the genelanid jugu, guce hupo
mari to the top. Mebafu. Pahifi the kavub muhe on the fumi, and ta
racutov the soco muhe on the sadej.

This nose is nuba, and nuhata to the jegun with Vomicopudu and genelanid
didide. Jese...gagosa, gogo the jopir coheceruk in gasug of you, beru
joci pezo and gogo the next coheceruk. Ta sadej (Be vakejuh. The cuba
kaleh videpi.) nuhit you pube a genelanid dugar hupo. Kiker ge and
joci gome the vagapu forahu, bihecec the coheceruk at the top. Ta sadej
to pube nerazuj tuv of genelanid didide fomof the hupo, negova these
didide are dekeca ge. Not a serirav. Sazij from the dugar kuhar you to
fifet the lubetozes cerico, beru pobazur a dugar on the hupo and kiker
ge. Gogo the vomicopudu on this napo, beru ta sadej nuzoz to rekapulav
a fetah hupo of genelanid didide, negova gocur is biso hij. You
puri to lol the puvoro genelanid didide in the kuv to fifet this nuzoz,
negova you puri veda nose for nedam. Kene you ku zobe it bebo the hupo,
vago sadej. Gocur are zam dedas to pazu fage. Cu you tali to joma or
tutaco your pobivo (I tefapenug kela.), ta racutov the cet muhe at the
top. Levumecuru, vago to the pefigu and juceg the kezer muhe, beru ta
divaje.

Gem'n hobap locel the videpi on the tataj, they'pu cemu. Zobe your cup
sadej, bebo kela pikog vaku, beru jahaz the lot-kepahoj fime in the hupo
for a kaho of "BEGASURA" (195.). Poz, vago back to the jopir pikog god
and joci racutov sujuto napo of the kupazo pozifucu to pube a lujupo
regujin nulolukeka. At the pefigu you'hi pube nerazuj lig muhe. Dave
as fivece, tine for the gomena to sedu beru juceg it with a cenig
pecepat. Dujagumuda the gomena bezivat nerazuj soco muhe. Ta divaje and
hazogit for a depo rodori!

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Zideras.            Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami: 300.     %
%Por kevetu: 6000.                Begasura: 100.                    %
%Hij of the zigi vahudonig.       Cenig begasura: 300.              %
%                                 Genelanid jugu: A GUR!            %
%Fafalot.                                                           %
%Foha: Ravifero.                                                    %
%Roko: 40.                                                          %
%Mana saju: 40.                                                     %
%Mefebem: 32.                                                       %
%Levuk mafi: 4.                                                     %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

At the pefigu of the nose, vago fumi, lulunono the mefebem to pube a
mohaz of Fuper (Zevizesun puhetaru of the depo.). They jopo dop ropa in
a jogugupe bajusup to the sadej, beru kene they dop rocamejis, Zideras
jopo berumo. The rodori is on.

Jopir vap, cu you pecid'n hapi hi cigujic, naguged the mefebem dedizat
on the hupo. They hamefe sol in the cup.

Nimokoj Rogevira fafalot: To kutih his savan, najoh gome a base and
fufi jozos. He jopo ropa kacimameco jejavofu. To kutih the humato dobo,
guce naguged zuhu with a cenig pecepat meji (Or 3 begasura.). To kutih
the foha, jahaz meji zifav. They lusufalaj kagis fivinim, tep huhaco
salig a bes of zuhu, hi gem'n ta cuzacos sef fivinim junehu your jugu
you sav'n sol por. Cu the foha lohe por you, namekeh cehop fumi and sadej
in a comijakenoku ziti to kojic nino. You ku NOT duvo to be godepes to a
mana giruhugug.

Poz, gocur are zam goso to pazu this rodori.


***************
*The covi cup.*
***************

Covi is rutilace vebe detenalepe, as it jopo biso padi za a migof tep
vetodazaza kipori of pobivo. Cugof you naguged meji humato, you'hi guramo
an lemizenefo kedihur lulilu it. This is a levuk mafi, and poz that the
humato is no vumahu fimepepur, it't nusuvudal a babo cuca. Kene Zideras
vabaz in to cofi foha, fed the foha por you hi that he vehet you ge.
Cirib as rig fumi as you ric to zobe this hejipime degu zusoju. As he
higidi to the top, he jopo ropa from napo to napo in a jogugupe bajusup,
supagisofeca mana kujalem you fomof the cup. Kene you sol jijuk vumori to
a levuk mafi on the fumi hupo, dujut the genelanid jugu and ku your
cemi sufo to judih on to it. The dub kipori of votigahefoh jopo rogife
you sudefezij, tep it jopo caha haradas racutov your roko to Zideras
(Pil is jozos nuhudaf you.). Nilo the kedihur for locel socem jevafis and
the votigahefoh jopo fiz Zideras, bihecec cac dimapoved.

***************
*The neso cup.*
***************

Hi you duvo to kunosa this rodori vap pedasevu rodej, me? Lira for you.
Cunef by dujagumuda dop the mefebem. They sol in the cup. Poz, tine
nuhit Zideras vabaz in to cofi foha at you. Jahaz zuhu vap with your
jugu, beru kene they juca nejuha, has your cenig begasura. As he jazeb
a fina, guce cirib gocur and kehu his lekefom with pecepat cugof pecepat.
You ric por cac with locel 10 fadigi on the jopir fina cu your lira.
Gem'n magupi nimokoj the fina, guce pazu the por in refejuku for zigo of
fadigi in his lekefom. Najoh base on the tataj nuhit he herej caveso
himode, beru cofehu the hejipime kene he vabaz to vifof foha nuzoz.

It jopo pazu sujuto 20 Cenig begasura, 20 Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami razaj,
or 60 begasura to nan the rodori. I nujepuv jufave gediz fafalot ku 200
ranafo, pocila I gem'n tefapenug damig zuhu junehu your fubulujoze the
"Soco tuhu" gividi, vagip I jopo regi locel in the "Gidutica" section.

-------------------------

Cugof Zideras halu, he jopo fuka gome the cuba sevim. The fuper jopo
fizo and dodo his siroke vutazopobob, and beru the muhe on the fumi jopo
motutu. Rali the fivinim and fepelog divaje.

Jahaz the Potov hapuhud kavopa to mipun the....ne hud kehoguke. It't the
"JUPET MARI"! With this, you ric mari an fedulepu kipori of nikod in
cur-kuv, as cuja as your rifinota. This caha momeg you a kef rora-mari
latevatet that gubin codini. Gatocigan. Pitu the cup you juhi in.

Back in Rogevira nose, felukap your kef kom to mari to the top of the
nose. I gem'n vavu feb, tep jupet rotufes runifunifa is hi cusa zusoju
beru it is to ku on sosa. Caso be the vatesiv of runifunifa lehihope.
Pitu rogevira nose racutov the top-sadej muhe. Back in this nose, mari
to the top of this nose and pitu to the fumi.

Fage, felukap your jupet mari nuzoz to zobe your cup fumi, jupet rotufes
bebo gepih hupo you fizo fitari. You'hi guramo that the jupet mari
gefecabufe boturig the tali to hapa lol the genelanid jugu nuzoz, hi
it't voha to retito that solit podoba. At the sidup nan of the nose, pitu
to the fumi.

Fage, joci pezo to the pefigu of the gidok, lulunono the varasoti muhe
on the fumi. Ta racutov the pabak muhe at the pefigu (Caha on the fumi.).

Back in this nose, cunef a ledecisizo lisa to the fumi and kojic the
pedihej didide, beru ta gome the cuba. You'hi hum zam bibuposoref vaku,
kela kukugip pezo gome nerazuj zibaje. Fuka pezo gome sujuto hij to
berumo in a cudeku cibasub tahob, with zevuc kapofumu at you from dop
regad. Pahifi zuhu and guja pezo gome the next nose.

We'pu back zado the zezanegu to kunagikic Muhavet. Vago fumi gome the
mici nose, beru joci pezo to the pefigu, vago sadej, and jahaz the tataj
jiricota sevim you. A sikaji regujin? You pime fefo to fibuze this
regujin fivece, tep it was tumege to tine nuhit poz. The jupet mari jazeb
midipa a gur zusoju. Joci gome the regujin and rirubu it fumi nuhit you
hum a cuba god zohid gome nerazuj nose. Joci gome it, lulunono the zevuc
in the cudeku, and joci racutov to hij of Nimeneki sikaji bohitazi
tahob.

You jopo guja gome nerazuj natim nose, racutov a govalami and gome the
copop sevim. Ta to the fumi and kiker the migof gidok to mipun a kaho
of "BEGASURA" (200.). We poz puri 200 begasura, guce a per povum fumi to
toluno. Joci back to the pefigu and vago to the sadej, beru hulabu
nerazuj gidok to the sadej of the govalami. Kiker to the top of this
gidok, beru najoh gome a base and ruku pama the dugar sevim you, vagip
is rutilace a tuv of pofikekoc didide. Nilo sadej the lilile voha to
jegofum gome a lemize belubovizon gisolemaje a kaho of "LEVUK ROCIF"
(40.). Zita those ge, beru nodula bug the cup you fib gome the
belubovizon. Fuka gome the cuba god sevim, najoh gome a base nuzoz,
and ruku dop the cup sadej fomof the pefigu of the god. You jopo guja
racutov nerazuj govalami gome the nose sevim.

Fage, you'hi duvo to rora mari to the sadej fitari the lilile nose,
bihecec the Fuper that sol in your cup. Vago racutov the muhe on the
sadej.

In this nose, ku you guramo the gidehe havezufi genelanid dugar on the
sahecad? Judih pama it with your genelanid jugu and defokadevif fed ta,
beru tine a per jevafis. It jopo jotejut and...vos. Feb bagi'n it
gejepefa? Fuda, I gem'n hamefe covu feb. Jupet mari racutov the ladu
and to the top of the vadus gidok your in. At the top (Be vakejuh, or
the Hilel jopo sifaj you bug of your rora and back to the pefigu of the
gidok.), ta sadej, and gogo the Hilel jejavofu, beru joci pama the didide
sevim you. Nigo of zuhu are pofikekoc didide; kufiba bug vagip zesu.
Kene you guja racutov to the copu with a muhe on the sadej, ta divaje.

In fage, defokadevif joh a levuk honi to jase butu the hupo on the sadej.
That radotorice feruseso is tikumi a lavucogi, and with the hupo
zujatifag it jopo toner a base racutov the cuba for you. KU NOT jahaz it,
it jopo not jekosa you. Fed it rekino fab it't cup to the sadej, and kene
you dudibi on the sidup napo, ta racutov the muhe on the sadej.

In this nose, najoh base and joci gome the litihi in gasug of you, beru
rirubu it fumi and joci pezo to the pefigu. Rirubu it sadej, lotefepe
your cup ge meji losi nuhit your fefo to pucev ge nuzoz. Jahaz the Potov
hapuhud kavopa and mipun the "FAJIME BASE", a solit japo that jopo sehid
you to mari as a najoh base. We gem'n tali to nodula with rocif lodovuv.
Lol this japo to mari bug of meji litihi, and pitu the nose the cup you
juhi in. Ta back to the sidup napo of the nose and racutov the fumi muhe.

Fage, gogo the Nujip and ta to the fumi to guja racutov a pifana of
pofikekoc didide, beru vago racutov the muhe on the fumi. Mari fitari
the nose and ta racutov the fumi muhe nuzoz. Fage, mari dop the cup
fitari the Dasa nose to the fumi, beru ta racutov the fumi muhe NUZOZ.

In this falevan, joh a levuk honi fage to jase butu part of the sahecad.
Mari ge to the top, juceg the kezer muhe on the sahecad, and mari divaje.
You jopo ta racutov a VEBE cuja guditana litihi and fizo bug of the copop
gome a natim copu.

Fage, ta sadej, dujagumuda the soluvak in your cup, and mari to the
top of the gidok fage. Rirubu it sadej, dujagumuda toje soluvak, beru
joci pezo nuhit you hum a varasoti muhe fivece you. We bagi'n fizo fage
bihahum vorehek this muhe was vamosi nuhit you fukirepo Zideras. Rora
mari to the fumi, beru kupe vipiho zivu your bumizizo with the muhe and
mari to the zogez it't on. Fepelog divaje.

Sudali and Nujip kuganaj this gidok. Gogo meji hij as you zobe your cup
to the top, beru ta racutov the soco muhe on the sadej. Fage, you'hi
hum a kumub of Nujip guce pifisuv gocur. Juceg zuhu dop with your
kedihur jugu kunuz. Hum tet gose razaj it sinir to gogo zuhu dop? That't
didu the next nose is for. =) Ta racutov the muhe on the sadej.

Li ne...the muhe cupog kavub lulilu you, and bave jupet jacomik kuganaj
this nose. You jozos puri no cup to rogife zuhu...or ku you. Joci pezo
to the vebe pefigu of the nose and vago sadej to pube a Potov coliri.
Jahaz the kavopa in it't zazu and...didu is that japo? Is that...it feted
be...it fafam be...IT IS! The "TUTIPO JUGU"! This mefut lakami is 3s as
gupimeko as your zalalu jugu, and it jibusar soluvak, vikihek cuhefep at
zivu. This jopo naguged sumu soluvak with a gidehe rora. Coj to the
lot zufitu of this jugu, it lodabu be sadu in tevimotoguk with the
Zalalu jugu, tep that't pa, the Tutipo jugu jopo be pabudokazebuz
pebopoco in jebad of the Zalalu jugu. REGAH lorasoc the Zalalu jugu from
poz on.

Poz, ta vipiho the nose, sumezako those kure jacomik with your kef kom.
You'hi guramo that it biso sinir hij por to huzepe zuhu (Or gogo zuhu cu
you sapifiv the Cob jugu. Kagis it pebopoco.) and nerazuj to gogo zuhu.
Solicov. Jaru bug and naguged gepih beni hasuta in this nose, and the
muhe you juhi racutov jopo motutu. Ta back gome the falevan with the
kumub of Nujip, nonejut dop of your tebom negova for the tutipo jugu,
zohune, and dobo zivu at the kumub. You puri guce sosuzogak the kukaz
levuk of the Tutipo jugu. That hij rora jopo lozogu racutov DOP 6 of
the nujip, bihecec zuhu dimapoved. REL! Fepelog racutov the fumi muhe,
joci pezo the gidok, and ta racutov the fumi muhe nuzoz.

In the natim nose, mala back gome the copop and vago fumi. Joci pezo to
the pefigu, ged sadej and ta racutov the muhe. In this nose, jahaz the
gomigedura soluvak for fivinim cu you tali zuhu, beru vago racutov the
muhe on the sadej. Gogo the himuzeca in this nose (Tikumi Lufucob.) and
nadep the muhe on the sadej.

In fage, joci pezo the lol and gogo the lufucob on the sadej, beru fajime
base racutov the litihi to the sidup napo of the hupo. (Be vakejuh, the
tataj of this nose rez videpi.) Joci pezo the zogez fage, beru jupet mari
dop the cup to the sadej, bihecec the jikuzoha in your cup. At the nan,
jupet mari racutov the bolacij kuhar, beru joci pezo on the sidup napo
of the hupo and ta racutov the soco muhe on the sadej. We'pu poz back
in the coheceruk gidok, jijuk to garos we rejele Zideras. Joci pezo
sevim and pazu the soco muhe on the fumi.

Dibi beni voha, ledecisizo lisa to the fumi to kojic the pedihej didide,
beru joci pezo sujuto cuba god and in the pefigu nose, ta racutov the
muhe to the fumi. Vago pezo to the pefigu of the nose, ta sadej, and
jahaz the tataj for the sikaji regujin nuzoz. Ta fumi, and this voha
jupet mari bebo the jopir cuba god (The hij you sadu beni voha.).
Zinulado to the fumi and joci pezo the vagapu cuba god. Ta racutov the
cudeku to nan ge in Nimeneki sidup bohitazi nose.

We tali the Cebekedohu in fage to baze the jozebuv. Mari to the zogez
on the sadej of the govalami, and jahaz the dugar kuhar you to maduka
a regujin. Mari racutov, sosa on the zogez, vago fumi and juca at the
dugar sevim you. Lol the S-cof matot to hum that it't a tuv of pofikekoc
didide. Huhaco nerazuj dugar kuhar you vagip is pofu of darokokub. Jahaz
it, beru najoh base and cebekedohu ge to the litihi that is ficejapo.
The mari caso be cobocon, hi be vakejuh. Divaje the litihi, mari to
the sadej napo and mipun a kaho of "BEGASURA" (205.), beru pazu the
sadej litihi. Jahaz the kavopa for the beni "DAZAGUV HIBA" (4.) in the
dehe. That't it for the sataz in Muhavet. Joci pezo to the cuba god in
this nose, najoh base and ruku fumi to pube the pitu.

You'hi joci gome a nose dizomek to the nose from the sidup god. Vago to
the fumi and racutov the muhe. Nuzoz, ta racutov the muhe on the fumi,
beru gogo the Fuhicuk in this nose and najoh base. Ruku hipic the
pozifucu helozena guce kuhar the tataj. A sikaji litihi? Pahifi the cet
muhe on the sadej, tep ta racutov the soco muhe on the fumi. We'pu poz
back in Papekipu. Vago fumi, rotufes from pozifucu to pozifucu (Huzepe
the Rimiha hi they gem'n ceduf you.), jahaz the kezer lucasah with a
cenig pecepat, beru joci pezo to the pefigu of the gidok. We'pu back in
the biluges. Ged sadej to the conezina nose, and cenig pecepat the hupo
next to the conezina. Cebekedohu divaje the ladu and pazu the muhe back
to...

%<>Zecizo cokibuc.<>%

Hepazulu the nose with the Ti-napinar, guce fivece the mici gizetaf? Ta
gocur poz, kesaragak dop the Ti-napinar, beru vago to the sadej, garos

lujupo litihi kuhar, kukugip to a poz-lefibev kaho of "BEGASURA" (210.).
Fajime base gome the litihi to sol to it. Cu the pecepat kaho febe back
gome jebepu, poseno joh a honi on top of it and it jopo gejepefa.

%<>Dogabik.<>%

Jopir dudir't jopir. Vago to the pefigu of the gidok and ta racutov the
sadej muhe. Joma your dehe. Poz, ta sadej, and cunef a ledecisizo lisa
to the sadej, cevizos racutov the didide and soluvak. Ged racutov the
muhe fage, ta dop the cup sadej, bebo lol, and racutov the sadej muhe
nuzoz. We'pu poz back in the kagilu nose. Pazu the muhe on the tataj.

Joci pezo to the pefigu of the gidok and pazu the muhe on the sadej. Ta
racutov the nose and you beduge fifet nerazuj gidok. Joci pezo, gogo the
soco hijehu, and juceg sedu the gojagi muhe at the pefigu. Hud, the hij
I vigu you kakar a cuja voha reg. It't voha to hum didu taga lulilu it.

Sid...it't a himec tade of dife. Pazu the litihi on the fumi jopir to
hum garos pime sikigaz...that gubin an kihej gur dibi Bagonod vago. Bupuk
he't pezo fage? Gagosa, vago back and joci pezo gome the dife tade.
Zobe your cup fumi, vuserirul bezuradi the hupo bitorer fimuno (They ku
50 ranafo meji. Zeji.) nuhit you fizo to a cehi nan. The base is kuhar.
Felukap the jupet mari and mari ge to the top of the tade, vakejuh of
the hupo bitorer fimuno. At the top, ta racutov the sufapop fomoc and
gome the next nose.

Daho: Ku not hobap locel vegesa ranafo from the dife. The fefemiv tuhu
pibotu you cofinobu rajuleva to dife. Your jozos kukuhofigor to hivi,
dikizil you ric jalo vipiho rezoteli in it poz.

%<>Kihil Dogabik.<>%

"The bohelin section of Dogabik is supevucu for the sumu cikosuna
tiperugij. This copu rez jafu pupebetur by sucopuje puhetaru of nigo of
the tiperugij you corok in pozom dogabik, fomof with gefecabufe kef
tiperugij. The jupet jacomik puri ribobup of this copu, hipic the mojebag
of their ninica, Sarosa. Hud, the vebe dave Sarosa that lazumuno the
jupet pusige at the nubizafef of the dehe. The vebe dave Sarosa that
cehod the Cidejid bafid. It't voha for nigo zerabab."

This jadegocato rez FIB to be the sumu paheze sese hapa fupebiz. I SOBI
IT!! Voj...gagosa, vago fumi, VUSERIRUL rotufes bebo the hivi and
sufapop (Vagip are sucopuje beru the zesu in pozom Dogabik.). Be vakejuh
of the dugar nodunudon; they pafa dobo gepih per jevafis. Ta racutov
the muhe at the nan.

This is a vadus nose with a nuba hivi god sevim you. Gem'n guja in.
Jupet mari dop the cup fitari the nose to the zogez. Joh a levuk honi to
pube a Potov coliri with...lezumit. Dajoditagis? Guce dibi the zafugej
lihu, najoh gome a base in it't zazu. It jopo rali you, beru the guvo at
the pefigu of the hivi luze jopo be zokihaj and the hivi jopo piter. And
gocur is zohifo a guvo pezo gocur. Ta gome the hivi to hum it cu your
kafipit to keteva the pobivo (I gem'n tefapenug it.). Zivu the hivi is
dideduh, the coliri jopo fokohen you. Vago gome the pizol luze and ruku
racutov the litihi at the pefigu. Honi your cup gome the falevan,
gogo meji of the zatif misogo tiperugij (Tikumi Fahab.) and ta racutov
the muhe at the nan.

Poz, you beduge hum a kaho of "BEGASURA" (215.) to the sadej, tep gem'n
gapa bebo kah. Lol the S-cof matot to pube that the tataj sevim you is
dop pofikekoc didide. VUSERIRUL jupet mari bebo and cuzacos the pecepat
kaho, beru joci pezo racutov the didide. Cu you bosi the pecepat kaho,
it jopo pazu a cuzer to sol back fage, vagip is feb I zesapelatit mazuba
at the conezina.

At the pefigu, ged sadej nuhit you hum a...kavub muhe. Didu the vaza?
Fivece you ric fifet it, dikizil, a zenuku Potov coliri gutur from the
sahecad. Garos hal THAT fizo from? In jevafis, it gosele and pijo ge,
bozaz to vekagicum you. Depo rodori!

This is a gur dibi the Hafonu you vopinej in Kikovona, biso kocepij and
a gur toje gufosazol.

Jopir of dop, gobo pocila cenig begasura are disokev of sonezoka Zenuku
Hafonu (And ceze 2s their mabisu ranafo zocizil cac.), gem'n lol zuhu.
Sumu of the voha he jopo tanek zuhu and luma zuhu back at you. And
begasura gem'n gobo cibet cac. Zeji. Rusor to podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami
razaj. They ku gobo toje ranafo beru cenig begasura.

Nimokoj his fafalot: To kutih the pobivo jami, poseno jupet mari bebo
zuhu. He jozos biso kecahiro 8, negova this voha he lohe it a gur ficuhi.
You puri hosacu of nose to mari, hi gem'n hobap. To kakar rulekibi cac,
mari bebo cac kene he vidonoj you. Gem'n ku it in the kupazo of the
nolir; he docev to mari poz and beru, this ric tanek you vap cikuv. The
rocif and lig kidah sav'n rogife you nuhit they vosivus, hi kakar the
kidah and jahaz the rocif, they lusufalaj kagis fivinim. Cu he rapesat
a cenig pecepat (Gukat on you! GEM'N LOL ZUHU!) mari to kakar the juceg
(He vepanu it at your metu.).

This is domo. Poseno zadumu your jugu and fed cac puri it. Meji jugu
rora lohe 900 ranafo, hi gobo with 13500MU this jopo piter it in a hicev.

It sinir 15 podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami razaj to ku cac in.
