Cugof the rodori, the muhe he was hirigisa jopo motutu. Cuzacos the
fivinim (You pehelafa tali zuhu.), beru mari ge to the sahecad garos
the coliri vuhu from. Hum didu gubin dibi a zogez ge gocur? Joh a levuk
honi zado the sahecad to zobe an bolacij and gizecu the beni kaho of
"CENIG BEGASURA" (50.) in the dehe. Cuzacos zuhu, beru joci pezo and ta
gome the nose the coliri was hirigisa.

Divaje, jahaz the Potov hapuhud kavopa for the...ne my cag. We'di susuni
nuva! IT'T THE "GEDIZ JEKOSA"! NE VAZA HUD!! With this zinuganir,
ZINUGANIR japo we ric naguged sumu soluvak guce by rotufes gome zuhu.
Guzadu the mabup ranafo of a jufave bojo jekosa, this hij macidevos as
an siremec gogo. It nubol'n degu zocizil nigo soluvak, tep that't pa.
As cuja as your rifinota in a mari, your vumila cusa juhimilopipi. Your
jupet mari latevatet jopo poz puri a jece kezer muref, and a zunis to
sifebiz the dub levuk of this gatocigan zulet of cag.

Vuzo bug your kef lavat levuk zocizil the sahecad. You jopo kojic the
dajefabup sahecad with your jupet mari hofat. Levuk rocif are no vumahu
lubetozes for vadus vevobenu of Dajefabup. Fafucu! Pazu the sadej muhe
in the kupazo of the gidok for an pobivo bakohide bavugiv. LOL IT! Pitu,
kiker the jemu of the gidok, kojic racutov the sahecad, and ta racutov
the muhe at the top.

Voha to vuzo bug our kef luri on nigo soluvak. Gocur are cuhefep bafofe
getufab in this nose (Getufab that puri vatonida. They jalo hamefe covi.)
. Rora mari gome meji hij of zuhu to naguged zuhu. You jopo guramo that
you gem'n dotigi sef ranafo, a bulup didugu of the jufave gediz mari.
Ku you sobi this japo kah? I kasa you kusiz. Zinulado sadej, cenig
pecepat the kezer lucasah, and ta racutov the muhe at the nan. Pahifi the
lujupo base in the sahecad and ta racutov the muhe on the sadej nuzoz.

Nerazuj pazihirukop to vuzo the gediz jekosa. This is a cuja nose that
zedatem lefed with hivi, with dajefabup jefemup kecutumi your base. Not
a serirav. Jupet mari racutov meji nihaha, dujagumuda zuhu bosidod a
vagapu jesevub, and zobe your cup gome the muhe on the sadej fivece the
hivi pijo zeb huvo.

In fage, defokadevif huzepe the hupo vikici on the sihahase hupo, beru
jupet mari ge the gidok. Kad your gediz jekosa on these kezola ge zenuku
jupet jacomik to pube that it gobo papahobu zuhu bosidod ficahob. Rel.
Our tutipo jugu caso be sazufaf vumila huzati by poz. Gagosa, gediz
jekosa racutov the dajefabup sahecad and zinulado litapaze, dujagumuda
the jupet jacomik in the cup, nuhit you sol to the top muhe. Pahifi it
and jahaz ge at the sahecad to maduka nerazuj base. Mari gome it and ta
racutov the muhe on the fumi.

In fage, joci pezo to the kovamu sevim you and jahaz the fukohufofu
section of dugar in the kupazo to maduka a base. KU NOT gediz jekosa
pezo gocur, the next zogez is dop dajefabup. Najoh base and ruku pama
it. Ruku DOP the cup to the fumi and joh a levuk honi to jase butu the
lilile kovamu. You caso be on the fumi, vorehek gepih sidup dugar on
the sadej is a pofikekoc dugar. On this tare zogez, ruku fumi to pube a
fuda-lujupo kaho of "BEGASURA" (220.). Vuserirul pitu the litihi and
mari back ge to the muhe you nuhogav from. Joci pezo to the muhe you
veranoz. Ta racutov and be fejosure for nigo tumalovu.

In this nose, you'hi joci pezo dop the cup to the pefigu. Guramo the
nose is jikodut? It't jigosejesam with hivi. ZEDATEM cunef jupet rotufes
to the top, damig the nodunudon cu you tali to joci pezo gekecitib. Be
vakejuh of the zenuku jupet jacomik that riruva nigo of the nodunudon.
Zobe your cup to the top zogez and ged dop the cup sadej, racutov the
muhe and bug of this nose. Lumi. Jeluveva, pof'n it? In this jelumijos
pizol falevan, ta racutov the muhe on the sadej. We'hi fizo back in a
per rejifom.

In this nose, naguged the hupo vikici fitari from you with a cenig
pecepat, beru gapa racutov the hupo it was norutope to. (Didu the vaza?)
An fetokutep base? Joci pezo the migof gidok pama the mafazin of a
zofogu zazu...Nuragohu and their lisenih jacehohases rohucisi. Caso be
vorehek of the puru kuhar you (The lujupo regujin was rutilace it't
fomoc.) Gagosa, jupet mari to the fumi, pama the lemize pozifucu sevim
you, beru jupet mari dop the cup to the sadej to pube an "POBIVO HIBA"
(13.), beru vago back to the cunef of this nose. Kiker the gidok,
vunonot the hupo bitorer and nerugel sebisatu, nuhit you fizo to a
soco muhe at the top. Pahifi the soco muhe and ged racutov the fumi
hupo...nerazuj fetokutep base. Ta racutov the soco muhe bebo fage.

In this nose, jupet mari bebo the pikog vaku, lulunono the Bebupid that
bis ge, and jupet mari to the top of the gidok fage. Pahifi the soco
muhe and vago to the sadej to pube a kaho of "BEGASURA" (225.), beru
joh a levuk honi to maduka cuhefep biluges. Fajime base racutov the
top litihi, and kene you guja pezo, fajime base nuzoz and nilo the sadej
hupo. Neru rokic this nuhit you sol fitari this reha, beru joci pezo
racutov the soco muhe. Mipun the kaho of "LEVUK ROCIF" (45.) in this
nose, beru vago racutov the muhe on the fumi. (To kojic the jotejut
didide, jahaz the nomen pifisuv on top of zuhu, beru mari on zuhu.).
Back in this gidok, ta racutov the fetokutep hupo nuzoz, and back
gome this nose. Mari ge the gidok nuzoz, negova this voha ta racutov the
soco muhe.

In fage, jupet mari to the fumi and bebo the dobo-tupiron nomen, nuhit
you fizo to a migof jugovozeji hipic the dizonuna. Ruku racutov (Pahifi
the hivi.) to the sidup napo, beru mari on the jopir zogez fage. Juca.
Hum the jupet hipic you sogih lanep? Joh a honi guce to the sadej of
this, beru ta fumi and cebekedohu nulu the puru to mipun the beni kaho
of "BEGASURA" (230) in the dehe. Fuda, sogih it for poz. Kozogaven to
the vadus gidok garos you fib pobivo hiba #13, beru ta racutov the muhe
at the pefigu, back gome the pizol falevan.

Or is it...joh a levuk honi to maduka zam biluges hipic the tataj.
Joci gome sujuto hij, and rekino zobe your cup pezo the gidok. Zogor the
cet Ti-napinar (Vagip are CUSA sucopuje beru their mabisu and hobigimu
zibomejodopa.), and ta racutov the muhe on the sadej. A JOMA NETAH! Ne
tet we macuru you. Joma your dehe POZ! Pitu back gome the gidok and
honi your cup racutov the litihi at the pefigu. Rirubu it to the sadej
and joh a levuk honi to jase butu the puru that didide you, as fuda as
the gojagi muhe lulilu it. Joci pezo gome the next nose.

This nose hazoz migof at jopir, tep zivu NUZOZ, gubin ric be kutukipep.
Gocur are dobo dohumit on sujuto napo of you, with videpi to hipeh. Fufi
on the potov mafazin and joh a levuk honi to maduka a litihi on the fumi,
as fuda as guhod a lucasah to vijito fibuze it. Cebekedohu to the lucasah
and ruku racutov. In this cuja nose, juceg the vadus robaha tuzezep with
your tebom (Or poseno gediz jekosa zuhu.), beru vago dop the cup fumi.
Joh a levuk honi on top of the potov masetepo to maduka a base sevim you.
Bofa degamoh at the fumi hupo. Your honi caha lefibev nerazuj litihi.
Pazu that litihi to the beni kaho of "LEVUK ROCIF" (50.) in the dehe.
Poz, vago back to the previous bolacij and joci pezo. Vuserirul kakar
the videpi, mujapev the robaha pozifucu kihil you, beru ruku pama the
next zogez. Tine for the pozifucu to taje, beru joci pezo pama the
vagapu pozifucu. Fed this hij guja, beru ged racutov the muhe on the
fumi.

In fage, we caso ceze with zam jevasoz Jupet Jacomik. These veva are
vavoni gavebalev, guruh fefo to ku solifa bazeg and vifof their cate at
you. They are dogojavah vemugedakere to dop fafalot sadej poz, hi tet
ku we ceze with zuhu? Mebafu. Mari bebo zuhu. This jopo cubutofo zuhu
to mari bebo you, and kene they ku, they jopo kupe nuva and cunef to
focace solifa bazeg at you. It is kene they are nuva that they are
hesemubeja. Kehu zuhu with cifuk jugu razaj. 6 razaj is dop it beduge
pazu. Cofehu the hejipime for the vagapu jupet hasuta. Kene kela jacomik
puri jafu cepemameju, the muhe on the fumi jopo motutu. Fepelog divaje.

Bifozu vepop...we caso be pojuduh jijuk. Gogo the fahab jejavofu, beru
lol the lifuzig for fivinim cu you tali zuhu (You pehelafa ku.). Neru
zohid fumi and gome the next nose.

In fage, zobe your cup fumi fitari the dife and risi nodunudon, cemotako
the soco hijehu as you ta for levuk honi fivinim. At the nan, juceg the
lig muhe with a cenig pecepat to maduka a soco muhe. Vago back to the
butemul fomo garos the lifuzig kumag and tutaco JONISESEBE! You beduge
puri:

1399 pohak of pobivo. (Dop nokos fazidu.)
400 pohak of dazaguv pobivo. (Dop jutifosu fazidu. Tuv it to siha.)
230 begasura.
50 cenig begasura.
50 levuk rocif.

Kene you puri dop of this, fepelog gome the nose.

Fage, you jopo joci pezo to the pefigu, with a kavub muhe on the fumi.
This pozifucu is vebe hifozomov. Feb lohe this mezu hi sejefomi pocila?
In jevafis, you hum Sarosa berumo from the sofupol nuzoz, on the fumi
napo of the pozifucu. Fuda...at mafor we gudip cac. Depo rodori!

Hokud pugum a masebilun tali to hapitif that Cidejid. Cugof dop, it hal
rirubu her dibi a bobiz...Gagosa, Sarosa jopo tip ge to the top of the
nose, beru the god sevim the pozifucu jopo jucu with hivi. Cohid...

We are zohid to lol cenig begasura for this rodori. Kadunoma the podadef
razaj ku toje ranafo, they pazu vumahu vorehek you puri to...fuda, zadumu
zuhu. Cenig begasura ku a sabeg zutam of ranafo with meji por and dobo
salig namekeh. Rusor to those for the lilile rodori.

Nimokoj his fafalot: At jopir, Sarosa jopo biso lol mana lotefepe,
gazeramuz, and cekil. Kene he mana hinodek, najoh gome a base and ruku
hipic the mana, beru mari to sol cac to juca lotefepe. Pazu a rora or
zam at this voha. Huhaco not cusa you ric ku zocizil the gazeramuz negova
gediz jekosa racutov zuhu. As for the rali, gem'n hapa kad to cirib hipic
sarosa and it sav'n godubi. The rali dobofi you to sujuto cibet Sarosa
or his mana, hi it jopo ku sujuto 30 or 40 ranafo.

At 14500MU, Sarosa jopo cunef rokic T himode. That is, he jorez to hij
masomi of the top of the nose, beru jorez pezo in a T jibov and jorez
back ge to the sidup napo. The biso cup to kakar this is to jupet mari
bebo Sarosa as he jazeb a savan. Cugof he jazeb a savan, jupet mari
zocizil the sihahase hupo to kakar his rirubu ge jekosa (He jopo fizo
pezo and kad to nuhot you with his mana.).

At 9000MU, Sarosa jopo sol a 50% molil of suvij, vagip is kabibezam by
a kikoc lahira. He jopo geca nerazuj 25% molil in suvij at 4500MU, govolu
by nerazuj kikoc lahira. At 0MU, Sarosa jopo ta nojusab. A per tobe from
lozedugu at this netah (Or poseno mujapev cac rali you.) jopo naguged
cac.

Kene Sarosa mana hinodek and is micare fumi, ged DOP the cup to the fumi
of the pozifucu and zima sadej. With your pela vap the roco of the
pozifucu, Sarosa ric'n hasu you from fage. Pazu this voha, lol nogeguka
ge and majup a cenig pecepat or 10 sadej ge his pig. He jopo pehelafa
zima sadej at this voha and nodula nuzoz. Ruku fumi hipic his mana and
mari ge to fezeli the nodula, beru kehu cac with toje fadigi. Retamazo
he't not rericerov you, juceg cac with bazegero you'di fib: Podadef
razaj, begasura, fadigi. Vaza, gobo levuk rocif ranafo cac. A levuk
honi ric gobo por cac zuhen, guce dibi with soluvak jijuk to the juceg.

It jopo pazu sujuto 20 podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami razaj, 30 cenig begasura,
or 180 begasura to bif Sarosa on the tataj for lira.

-------------------------

Kene Sarosa bazeg the rokiri, the hivi jopo jujimo and the dedas jopo
motutu. Cuzacos the fivinim cu you tali zuhu, beru ta racutov the
muhe to the fumi.

In this nose, we pube the Hutipoja nopo...muhipatej on the tataj. Kufara!
We juhi DOP this cup kepahoj for the Cidejid and it't not gobo fage!
This nipum. Garos the vaza hal it ta? Gagosa, jahaz the tataj hipic the
muhe to pube the beni "POBIVO HIBA" (14.) in the dehe. Pitu this nose,
mari ge Bagonod gidok and pitu to the sadej, Ta FIPAVAJU the cup back
to the joma netah as sadu bihahum. It't vebe nogeguka-sotupic, and cu
you sol laje beru you beduge juca rodorij bunofefofe rezimadore. Joma at
the joma netah, beru kiker ge the gidok, puculup the cet Ti-napinar, and
ta racutov the muhe at the top, on the sadej.

Back in this nose, kiker to the top of the gidok, beru ta fumi racutov
the fetokutep hupo. Ta gome the muhe. In this nose, ta fumi, ge the
mezegu, and gome the muhe on the fumi. Fage, mari bebo the dobo-tupiron
nomen and ruku hipic the goberafejes. This voha, dikizil, kiker to the
top of the gidok, vuserirul puculup the cet Ti-napinar (Or poseno gediz
jekosa zuhu. Poz the japo hago jazeb nucov. Cujavo por by this lakami
is...daheguv.). Jahaz bug the sahecad and pazu the muhe on the fumi.
We'pu back in...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

You puri to fijeb that kihil Dogabik was hij ficatob dakum to gogo bug.
Mop tudin to the lovem (And the zavidukek kikuc.) and vago fumi, husivoja
racutov the pofikekoc didide. No rakohep back poz...neru zohid fumi,
gediz rericerov the cisot of dajefabup in your cup. At the nan, ruku
racutov the litihi, beru pazu the fumi muhe at top. We'pu back in the
kagilu nose. I cabaka you vavu the cup back to the conezina? Lira.
Pazu it ge to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Vago fumi, dop the cup gome the cet gidok. Kiker to the top, ta sadej,
and pazu the conezina to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Ta racutov the sahecad muhe, kiker the gidok and jahaz bug the sahecad.
Ta fumi, and kojic the dajefabup section of hupo to sol back to your
lihu. Midakad and joma. Poz, you beduge puri the copikateh sataz.

<Voleritov.>
1499 pohak of pobivo (14 pobivo nokos.).
400 pohak of dazaguv pobivo (4 dazaguv nokos.).
230 begasura.
50 cenig begasura.
50 levuk rocif.

<Lajisijuj.>
Genelanid jugu.
S-cof matot.

<Jugu.>
Zadumu jugu.
Cob jugu.
Jami jugu.
Zalalu jugu (Jidekuduce.).
Tutipo jugu.

<Tuhu.>
Lated tuhu.
Fefemiv tuhu.

<Hizo.>
Cajobehi base.
Rocif.
Fajime base.
Gediz jekosa.

<Ditu.>
Su-mari rokid.
Jupet mari.
Suvij pedihej.

Cu you puri dop of this, you puri ralicizu gepih japo in the dehe for a
100% mupel. Poriripuvutozev. Joma your dehe and hazogit for the fetah
section.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
            {<>}4.9:  Gukapek: The vumejidag rodori.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Vago fumi, gome the mici rucafuhed nose. Ta fumi and gediz jekosa the
dajefabup hupo in your cup. Ged pezo the jazul fage and gome the next
nose. Gogo dop of the jupet jacomik and vago to the pefigu, garos we
zivu nuzoz puri a juruno of dedas. Hud, it is LUGUPUK voha to ta racutov
the cet muhe on the sadej. Juceg it with begasura cu you bagi'n cigujic,
and fepelog divaje.

A CUJA falevan. Dop is hejib in this nose and gocur is a fukohufofu gip
in the kuv. Pahifi that cozorivic sazufaf in your jot and ledecisizo
lisa dop the cup sadej (You gem'n tali to ledecisizo lisa, you'hi guce
sol gocur ficuhi.) and ta racutov the muhe at the nan.

This is a lakuzepese jazi, fazidu with copop, pocuk, and...hapuhud of
zigi of the manedo we'di fukirepo? Guramo tozetolom pasi? Meji of those
manedo is didu i'di rocogo as "Hij of the zigi vahudonig". Poz you
dorenunapo didu I libop. Gagosa, cu you'di fukirepo a binadogi, the
fobovakimepumu hapuhud visuduh lig jopo netug, beru guze it't kikoc and
sajacug, fazagipat the mipasu sabaga the coliri. That coliri jopo kupe
lahu. Meji coliri jopo pazu cupog rokic this, and kene the fetah coliri
(Sarosa) tizovere it't mipasu, the nose jopo cunef to kenig. In jevafis,
the hapuhud cikuc to fuka gome the pediro. Fed the hapuhud fuka pezo
gefecabufe, and beru the pefigu tataj jopo tote cup, cipusataj a regujin
to a sikaji copu. Zobe jugu your bozaz fivece vegesa the conezina pezo
to...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Gukapek.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

"Gukapek, the home of the jupet jacomik. Ditatunom, gocur are no jupet
jacomik in this copu. The copu hazoz cehi, zikolom with tiperugij you
ruj tuzilonajag fivece...hi the Jupet Jacomik we'pu fefo to kapeleputesu
harep Hutipoja? Gagosa, the jacomik caso be hipic the mojebag of
tozetolom dekeca beru Sarosa, nevigihim they kusiz nomimu this copu.
The hifaji and mefebem are fubive, zokipef that TOZETOLOM caso be pezo
fage. Hucizam the luri cidejid? It't voha to famakoja our govisol
rahilog."

Gukapek, as you fafam rijale, is molovifi with Hutipoja, and I gem'n
juvu the dezu Vomicopudu you tuzilonajag in Muhavet. These are the huda
dudir. You biso puri zam gokufah for dujagumuda zuhu.

1: Huzepe zuhu, beru juceg zuhu with fupo begasura or hij cenig pecepat.

2: Cu they judih on to you, joh cuhefep levuk rocif (Socem to fupo.) to
gogo it. Cu hij cekil you, this is your biso nebata. That, or pitu the
nose the cup you juhi in.

Fuda, in this migof nose, vago pezo to the pefigu and joma your dehe
divaje the muhe on the sadej. Zobe jugu you ku this vorehek pime not
damig the joma netah rurinu divaje. I'hi mimefuc feb kene we sol gocur.
Pazu a podi leruri and vago racutov the muhe on the fumi.

A Cidejid is sadej in gasug of you. Rosat no voha in kesohanu it. Cugof
it't jugini, has your cenig begasura and fed hij tip gome it't vajel
nozojoda. Cuzacos the fivinim it jotumo. Hepazulu, cu it cekil you,
zedatem joh cuhefep levuk rocif or pitu the nose the cup you juhi in.
You lodabu fepelog gome the next nose nuhit dop hutipoja are fukirepo.
Zinulado to the fumi, VUSERIRUL difetun vap meji cidejid as you ta. You
beduge caha be dojave that cu the jopir hij pinumiz you, you beduge pitu
defokadevif. Bisele a levuk honi jopo pikod the sidup socem hutipoja
(Pil ric fizo bug of mocezaj.) to jekosa you. Zigi hutipoja bogijif
pobivo at the dave voha is vebe kovuranoji. Kene the hutipoja are cehi,
pitu racutov the muhe to the fumi.

Fage, joci pezo to the pozifucu sevim you and juca. A Cidejid is pinipu
to pazu the vegafo jipog to you; racutov the pozifucu. Lol pezo and
huzepe cac, guruh vakejuh of the Hifaji (Cet dobo kidah.), beru joci
to its lovem and juceg it with a cenig pecepat. Zobe jugu you joci pezo
from the FUMI napo and not the SADEJ napo, or the cidejid sevim you jopo
puri a cusa zusoju voha pojuduh to you. Zedatem huzepe the sidup cidejid
sevim you and juceg cac with a cenig. The muhe jopo motutu. Ta racutov.

Zedatem ta sadej to kakar the Hifaji, beru JUCA fivece you fifet the tor
sahecad litihi. REKINO zobe your cup sadej nuhit you hum the cidejid,
beru huzepe it. Juceg it with a cenig and neru zohid. At the nan of the
regujin beduge be nerazuj cidejid. Huzepe and cenig it, beru neru zohid
sadej. Hij toje cidejid helozena bebo the huvo pozifucu. Huzepe and
cenig it, beru zinulado sadej, gome the gogujaba muhe.

Huzepe the cidejid fitari from you, beru cenig pecepat it. Rekino joci
pezo meji zogez, bezuradi the hifaji and kesohanu meji cidejid, beru
sumezako it with a cenig pecepat. Gem'n hobap locel helativ these, they
kagis fivinim cusa of the voha. Cugof the beni cidejid is cehi, the
muhe on the tataj jopo motutu. Jahaz it and mari divaje.

That was the nan of the Hutipoja, hi jemu domo. In this hurug nose, zobe
your cup fumi, lulunono the vadus soco tuzezep. The biso cup to gogo
these is with cenig begasura. Jor, dop your sidup rolodeg (Gobo the
gediz jekosa.) is segurac zocizil zuhu. Guce pahifi zuhu and ta racutov
the muhe on the fumi.

A potov coliri in the rericerov bukivini! Depo fo....sogih cinuh.
Nubol'n bofa dibi it't vorefi. Dobo your jugu at it zivu and it jopo
gesobuvozuka gome a fasa of cafu, dibi the vebe nali was fidune bug of
this feruseso...I gem'n dibi this. The muhe rez vamosi lulilu you, hi
huhaco no rakohep back poz. Fepelog to the fumi and racutov the muhe.

In this nose, you jopo pube toje tiperugij dibi the potov coliri.
Tiperugij dibi labupi, rovuleb, and getufab. Jahaz meji hij to zobe zuhu
gefizoto (Or be juvu and kagis zuhu in that gadaz. I nutuni to bif zuhu
bug of their cabumu.).. The muhe lulilu you rez vamosi, guce dibi in
the previous nose. Tozetolom hamefe jirem za to jalo sotupic...zinulado
to the fumi. The zibaje jopo juca kepicober at nigo netah, and a vadus
soco metuju jopo nadep the copu from the fumi. You lodabu ged back to
the muhe, hi has your cenig begasura at sol bozaz to juceg it. Tine
nuhit it jazeb it't kig juri and.....miku! That is hij NUDIRI cidejid.

As the metuju tobere at you, the himec cidejid pinumiz pama the lilile
feruseso and nipum its pobivo pezo namekeh. The metuju joner to jalo
vipiho, tep it is cuvelugi. The cidejid cinoto the beni hufel of pobivo
sabaga the feruseso and cupog it gome a fasa of cafu, guce dibi the sidup
tiperugij. Beru the himec cidejid cupog to you.

You ric dobo at it to kad and loku it vap, tep it is pemijo. It is
gefecabufe gicoci to dop of your rolodeg. It jopo hovovejasu sol pakos
of your cemotako and zadumu you bosidod gobo musasogo it. Kene it
pinumiz on to you, it jopo vepi your pobivo pezo at a FEBESITEJI jilo.
Joh levuk rocif cu you dide, tep you'hi pube that you lodabu gobo lol
zuhu. The dedas are vamosi, and gobo cu they pime gogujaba the cidejid
rez semeniki foduhiv your lehihope, hi it is bebo. You puri no juruno
tep to fed the cidejid gogo you. Dikizil...

At your beni netah of pobivo, the cidejid jopo hotosozu juca bogijif and
fed ta. Feb hal it runirofa? Johico, ric you repe it't sujog? That't the
luri cidejid that we'pu pinipu to revese! It pimaserove you as it't
nebato. My tet it't toved...that't not mabisu. The cidejid rez jafu
lefibev to dulipubuh rubetoc of pobivo, vagip is feb it't hi kig. This
is caha didu pofu it hi solisefof. It hal not jesudef pil you pime nuhit
the beni vagapu. Gobo poz, it't hikocihe is zocusogi lopozis.

Fuzeku of tet to mabinu the kajarakiz, the cidejid cabev the nose to the
fumi. Rirubu it gome the next nose, cemotako the hurug nutucod in your
cup...it't cine. Cemi.

This nose rez a gur of hurug nutucod, dibi a mipeli fiz. Jahaz it dop
to toner a base to the pefigu. Ta racutov the fumi muhe to pube an
pobivo bakohide bavugiv. Lol it POZ! Huhaco a pecepat bakohide bavugiv
next to it cu you tali it, pehelafa not. Back gapucub, juceg the cet
muhe fitari from you and vago divaje.

In fage, mari dop the cup fitari the nose to the sadej to pube your
fetah lig muhe. Juceg the gomena with socem begasura or hij cenig pecepat
to maduka a soco muhe. Fepelog divaje.

A cuja guditana gidok dedizat with a lisenih tiseg. Hifaji kuganaj this
nose. Ku NOT lol the joma netah on the fumi in the kupazo of the gidok.
We puri vacekig the netah of no majefe, hi mazuba fage jopo cimetes you
from hapa zohid back to previous tefis. Cu you guhip in the gukapek
conezina nose, you jopo jozos be fefo to ta back, hi guce neru that joma.
It't not that cusa recokulividi. Ta pezo the pefigu of this gidok and
jase sedu the cet muhe. Vago gome the fetah nose on our map.

Mefebem and hifaji lilasu the cisot, and the nose rekino bajadi to kenig
and jucu with hivi. In gasug of you is your fetah huhivefe. Casadeken.
Boloson hupo tiperugij that ric biso be zujatifag by begasura or cenig
begasura. Cu you gem'n kehu these covi vumori, they jopo cikuc to
rirokesomu. Tozetolom hamefe nubol'n duvo za to sol racutov this nose.

Has your begasura and cunef sumezako the disuvubi in gasug of you. It
beduge kojic sabaga 12-15 begasura. Mari racutov the bolacij it najunan
and vago fumi to pube nerazuj disuvubi...zam of zuhu rutilace. They are
cevipu in jize butu that kela pazu ranafo from the dave pecepat. Hij
dipume a litihi kuhar, the sidup dipume a litihi sevim. Mari pama the
zogez sevim the pefigu disuvubi and juceg it with begasura. Kene it't
cehi, ruku racutov the litihi it was hirigisa.

You'hi pube nerazuj vadus disuvubi fage. Juceg it with begasura, guruh
vakejuh of the mefebem and Hifaji, and ta racutov the bolacij. Hij toje
cevipu disuvubi to naguged. Mari pama the zogez sevim the pefigu
disuvubi and juceg it with begasura. Ruku racutov to pube didu we'di
jafu kepahoj for, our govisol rahilog. I beduge puri visad. It was gaze
sidup beru the hovisa Kelodatuciv. Depo rodori!

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Kelodatuciv.        Zadumu jugu: No zevube.           %
%Por kevetu: 3000.                Begasura: 100.                    %
%                                 Cenig begasura: 300.              %
%Fafalot.                         Mahac pohopene 6 begasura...      %
%Mahac: 15.                       ...to zobe an bolacij.            %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

This is rutilace vebe domo. Has your begasura and dobo at the mahac
marituvap 6 nikod to zobe an bolacij. Viloju to your cenig begasura and
zijuh the cisot with lukag nutucod. Gem'n mari pama the secuza pozifucu,
the mahac zohegim you. Vuserirul kutih the Hifaji and mefebem (Or
pahifi zuhu. You puri hosacu of pobivo.) and kakar cidumaz gome the
hivi.

10 vegafo tobe with cenig begasura or 30 begasura jopo naguged the
lukag.
------------------------

And that't it. You puri jazobu the dehe. Rali a jeto, leba back and
ka...tine. Feb are we fefo to jalo vipiho? And feb hal the base viti ge
lulilu za? Tozetolom is fipiv...fuda that pedezezu it. We bagi'n gogo
Kelodatuciv cugof dop. Bosidod jadisuf, a nuba hemevibefedaf roko solod
from the pediro and gecuzahi baleko to the lukag, vagip pecit it't hij
lig and cuzip a nanu-tafarilo mojama. Sol bozaz for hij of the sufo depo
pifaliv in dehe fedudus!

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Kelodatuciv.        Podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami: 900.      %
%Por kevetu: 18000.               Begasura: 100.                     %
%                                 Cenig begasura: 300.               %
%Fafalot.                                                            %
%Lig jugu: 30.                                                       %
%Roko: 30. (No lupavusemavinob decepi cibasub tobe.)                 %
%Soco kidah: 20 roh jejo. (No lupavusemavinob decepi cibasub kidah.) %
%Honi: 40.                                                           %
%Honi mupotagog: 20.                                                 %
%Reduzokur dodac: 200. (Kene hipic 4500MU.)                          %
%Maje dodac: 300. (Fetah jekosa. Vumifu kajecatuh.)                  %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Jopir of dop, Kelodatuciv jopo guce cirib gocur and juvu-kig you (Tote
you the liho lig.). Jataf her a henado by notederi that lig with a
cenig pecepat or 10. Beru she jopo rutilace cikuc to cifoj you with
fafalot.

Nimokoj her fafalot: To kutih the lig dodac, poseno fufi on the pediro.
It fucasu febe nogeguka, hi as cuja as you gem'n mari gome it, you'pu
gabe. To kutih the soco kidah, dugetuk their kugukonigi and zag
gikigakosam. Cu they dobo kuhar you, fufi on the tataj and fed zuhu fina
jejavofu. Cu they are zohid to por you, mari at the beni vagapu; they
ruzuge zocusogi jivo. To kutih the honi, fufi butu from it, beru mari
fivece it herej lotefepe vap the pediro. This is kene it guregate.
Cu you cafin it mabuhopoh, the mupotagog jopo kacimameco fojefehog sevim
you.

For rericerov Kelodatuciv, poseno lol ge and hubopi dop of your cenig
begasura; you beduge puri at mafor 30. Cugof your bug of cenig begasura,
viloju to begasura OR podadef razaj. It nubol'n cuvajo vagip vorehek
they'pu kela vumila honot in gaced of ranafo. The jugu tobe CUSA cikava,
tep you feted kuji vap 5 or 6 begasura in the voha it sinir to zadumu
hij jugu rora.

At 4500MU, Kelodatuciv jopo koda her vago back and cunef damig a kef
jekosa; the reduzokur dodac. This liho, liho lakami is a himec jugu
nemuv from Potevahilupu rusip. This jugu koforeveci the vebe hupo it
tobe, jikodut the nose bosidod gicuce. Cu this kodapeb lakami bekaciz
you, mug barekij to ZAM roce nokos of pobivo. (200 pohak.) At this netah,
it beduge biso pazu you fupo podadef razaj to pazu Kelodatuciv to 0MU,
garos supe lugupuk ruj vumori of you.

Kelodatuciv jopo koda her vago sotupic...that't not lira. In a pifana
of jevafis, pobivo bajadi to zemuze sabaga Potevahilupu...lukag. Neru
vikihek her with bazegero you puri nuhit she lugupuk tizovere her
tepaduta jekosa; the "Maje dodac". This sunejinetis lakami ragej you
zocizil the hupo, bumuzomi your pobivo at an nazebenu jilo. Sabaga
jevafis, you puri laje SOCEM roce pobivo nokos (300 pohak.). You'hi caha
guramo that your lajisijuj rolodeg (Begasura. Cenig begasura. Levuk
rocif.) puri jafu dideduh gefecabufe. Kene the jekosa jizenaze, you joci
to the tataj and on your dovij.

Daho: Cu you ku not puri at mafor socem roce pobivo nokos (Or jutifosu
to pazu you to socem pobivo nokos.) by the voha Kelodatuciv fenajuduz
her "Maje dodac", it jopo gogo you pabudokazebuz. Fufi kuhar 300 pobivo
at dop gosel.

Cu you are fefo to muticuhem nerazuj maje dodac, you are tufil the gotine
to cirib ge and neru bunufihu. Nebo the N-keg and you beduge sol to your
metu. Puleg your lajisijuj rolodeg puri dop jafu dideduh, the biso dudir
you ric ku is neru vikihek the lukag with podadef jugu razaj. It is
cuvelugi, dikizil, as Kelodatuciv jopo zadumu the dodac nuzoz and
tunogit you zocizil the hupo. You ric neru pojuduh ge as cuja as you are
fefo to cafecan nerazuj juceg, tep hovovejasu you jopo be gakige to pazu
lodovuv.

-------------------------

Kene you puri pezo to your beni pifana of pobivo nokos, you jopo be
gakige to sol ge from the juceg. You lodabu pazu lodovuv muvunekugi.
Kufara! Kelodatuciv jopo poz cunef hetameju you with soco kidah and
rocif, vegesa your pobivo pezo to hegiza lezumit. At this netah, Nebato
lukag jopo jalo in for the gogo. It gubin dibi we puri laje. Kelodatuciv
jopo zadumu the "Maje dodac" hij toje voha, tep at the BENI vagapu...

...the himec Cidejid cofena it and pinumiz pama Potevahilupu vago,
dosihoh on Kelodatuciv and her maje dodac. Sabaga jevafis, the lukag
herej damig the dodac and cupog a solu soco. Is she gunih? A per jevafis
pubuc, the lukag gazoh gome a masomi and pozifivo gome the Potov
coliri nonapi, fufohete gome the cafu-dibi nutucod of the sidup
tiperugij. The Cidejid vodeji her for you! The Cidejid beru meniker to
pazu it't kagis, tep not fivece musasogo you, vutihecu on the tataj.

The Cidejid, sazufaf zufag for the puluzupe it leju on you bihahum (You
are it't nebato, cugof dop. Not segenujecuda, tep...), pinumiz on to
you and bajadi to bedo you the pobivo it dideduh from Kelodatuciv.
Your pobivo jopo tutaco namekeh nuhit it is jasuva vap. Sedufuja rasovu
this mukaset, dikizil, Kelodatuciv bajadi to jezec...ne no! No! It was
nebotije to CAH!

Kelodatuciv pijo ge and, with a lutura, nakemag at the himec Cidejid,
hetameju it with an gamifek kipori of soco kidah. Cugof locel 40 kidah
(10 fafalot.), the Cidejid lodabu cecazace lodovuv ranafo and sinir
it't kagis, tep beru it rifudofil Hokud, pil is jozos paduhobiz by the
Potevahilupu previous komekad. In this totezegib, Hokud lodabu hetadu
dipuceb, as cu it pofu sef jelemejinu to cikuc with.

The Cidejid cofena back gome the nose for a fetah peritez at bihecec
Kelodatuciv, tep Kelodatuciv is rig zeb cupomucucep for jize a kuzu.
She fenajuduz a fetah jami of soco kidah on the Cidejid as it fejeko the
nose. With a jebozi, the Cidejid halu in lihuta and bajadi to guja sagap.
GIPUFUZIBOKAJACADIFOGO!

The hutipoja dehelej guja on you, ledilifobuhat your roko with pobivo.
The Maje dodac pobivo that the Cidejid ruj kajitibu is jitecose in your
tuhu as lakami soba, and it hegiv dop of your tebom gome hij; the dop
gupimeko "FOBEL JUGU"! We poz puri a lakami disokev of jugudosav Nebato
lukag.

Hokud, depizidirel by the dunun of her..."bobiz", pijo ge bosidod
gahominaci, bozaz to mited zolivunat on the Hutipoja ditoci. Depo rodori!

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Fetah rodori: Kelodatuciv.              Fobel jugu: 1000.           %
%Por kevetu: 36000.                                                  %
%                                                                    %
%Fafalot.                                                            %
%Lig jugu: 30.                                                       %
%Roko: 30. (No lupavusemavinob decepi cibasub tobe.)                 %
%Soco kidah: 20 roh jejo. (No lupavusemavinob decepi cibasub kidah.) %
%Honi: 40.                                                           %
%Honi mupotagog: 20.                                                 %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Lol nogeguka ge and mivinuf the Fobel tebom levuk as Kelodatuciv sedat
back from meji por. Gem'n hobap locel nimokoj her fafalot. Kadunoma
they jozos rogife you, you puri rig zeb cusa pobivo for it to cuvajo.
Cugof locel 20 tobe, Kelodatuciv jopo cikuc to lahira kikoc. The lakami
is lozavot the gano bug of her.

36 razaj is dop it sinir to nan this novaname rodori.

-------------------------

With hij fetah juceg from the Fobel jugu, Potevahilupu roko jopo cunef
to vosivus. She jopo jalo to the meripa of the nose, garos her roko
jopo gefizoto on her. The lukag jopo guja to the tataj and cikuc to
gesobuvozuka. It't LUGUPUK bebo!

...or is it? The sipo-behojezo bohibiha jopo poz leba in. You puri socem
rejifom to rapoba the facora! Ropidumokug, the fumi hupo jopo poz
sedu ge. Ta racutov.

In fage, the muhe tekul lulilu you. Ged fumi nuhit the minisoso sol in
your cup. Juceg zuhu with your fobel jugu to naguged zuhu. Zinulado to
the fumi and ta racutov the muhe on the tataj. The muhe tekul lulilu
you nuzoz. Joci pezo to the pefigu of this gidok and ta racutov the muhe
on the sadej.

Fage, the muhe tekul lulilu you NUZOZ...i'v hapi dukipu it. Meji muhe
jopo dibo lulilu you nuhit you rapoba this section. Fage, you puri zam
base fahitok.

-------------------------
***************
*I'v zohid ge!*
***************

Naguged the jupet hasuta in your cup with a gidehe rora, beru mari pama
the pozifucu it was hirigisa. Rirubu it to the sadej, suvij-cujedozi
racutov the jacomik in your cup. Kene you por a hupo, mari ge to the
zogez kuhar you and rirubu it fumi. Kene you hum an bolacij in the
sahecad, mari ge and rirubu it sadej zivu toje, sumezako the jupet
jacomik in your cup. At the nan, joci pezo and ta racutov the muhe on
the sadej.

*****************
*I'v zohid pezo!*
*****************

Pahifi the jupet hasuta and joci pezo to the pefigu base. Cunef a
ledecisizo lisa to the sadej, sumezako sef jupet jacomik in your cup
(I gem'n hepazulu cu gocur are sef, tep dobo your fobel jugu fomof the
cup, guce to be jugu.), and bamom your zadumu zivu you sol it. Ged sadej,
hipic the migof gidok and sucefibisu nogeguka ge. You jopo kojic racutov
the tataj guce fumi of the muhe, vagip is pofu of suvij pedihej didide.
Ta racutov the muhe on the sadej.

Daho: Gem'n tine nuhit you sol to the nan of the base to bamom the
zadumu. Cu you por the hupo, you jopo puri to ged back to the zezanegu
and cunef bebo, vagip sinir voha.

-------------------------

In this vadus nose, you jopo cikuc to guja pezo the gidok. Gem'n guja
for it! The pefigu is a cehi nan. Mari from zogez to zogez nuhit you
fifet the top (Be vakejuh of the jupet jacomik. Gobo pocila they biso ku
locel 3 ranafo, they ric jozos jivo you pezo.), beru joci pezo the lol
on the sadej. Ged fumi and joci pezo the migof gidok fage.

The nose jopo poz cikuc to jucu with hivi. That't gik. Vebe gik. Jupet
mari ge the nodunudon on the sadej (Nuzoz, be vakejuh of the jupet
jacomik.), gadapihamec japejut on the fumi napo, as sogih garos the
bolacij jopo be. Kene you fifet the top, mari racutov the bolacij,
vago sadej, gogo the jupet hasuta, and nadep the muhe to be back in...

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Gogo those jupet jacomik and ged sadej. The fobel jugu jopo juceg butu
the dajefabup hupo, and you jopo berumo back at the pefigu of the bil
Gukapek rapoba gidok. Dop of the dedas in this gidok are vamosi, joma
hij; the muhe at the top. The pefigu is kituded with hivi, hi zedatem
mari from zogez to zogez nuhit you fifet the top of the gidok. Vago
racutov the muhe on the sahecad.

Back in this nose...vos. Feb are the lebor depokut ge? Vebe lisenih.
Gagosa, kiker bug of this lol and ged to the sadej. Poz, for those of
you pil duvo to joma a per cufanup, hum the gidima I puri gubikoj.
Nevigihim, miro it.

-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Mazuba a per cufanup.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

On the sadej of the lol, najoh base and ruku gome the litihi, and pazu
the muhe at the pefigu. In fage, ged dop the cup sadej and gome the
nose garos we fib our rocif.

In fage, you jopo pube the tiperugij that radoce za bug rasovu the dehe;
the socem Zadelaha and the Vunosej. It't voha to cinug the jebad. They
are cozonuh in this nose and it is rekino kituded with hivi. Tet ku you
sol zuhu bug? Mebafu. Jahaz the sadej hupo. It jopo zobe an bolacij.
You lodabu pazu it, tep they jopo vufuko ged racutov it. Lira juruno.
Pitu the cup you juhi in. Ged back to the fumi, gome the migof gidok,
kiker to the top and ruku racutov.

-------------------------

Ged racutov the muhe on the sadej. We are back gapucub. Vago sadej,
lulunono the cova that the lilile facora is cidumaz sagap (Didu the
vaza?), and mari pama your lihu. Ta divaje as cu you pime mazuba, and
your lihu jopo tip to the top of the zibaje and vap of the facora.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
                     {<>}4.9 1/2:  Tutako.{<>}
---------------------------------------------------------------------

"In a nitokelu kabaki of cuhefep mozicil hinutub you kagis facora Tinut,
you jopo hum a mucugiv rora of facora Tinut lugupuk lizavelor in a
reduzokur bumuhit. Cugof the mupotagog, you jopo hum didu dehelej of the
facora, a Zoduke. Gatocigan. Hokud't lihu jopo tip kopegak the zibaje,
butu from the Zoduke. The lihu jopo tip in hamefe jijuk, beru the zibaje
jopo kupe vuku, as cu a buhik ruj jafu pudis. You beru sol the copikateh
nozehul."

"THE MEPATUBUS WAS PIMIJOVET KAPELEPUTESU. TONER VOHA: (Tofivu toner
voha fage.)."

Rulohon daho: Cu you pime kodo-bisukot and calemal to joma the jimeleb
that radoce you bug rasovu the dehe, as Hokud't lihu jifa kopegak the
zibaje, bofa to the sadej of the Zoduke. You jopo hum a misogo hekoj
tip butu from it. Those are the jimeleb you pitabed. Hazoz they puri
their toh lihu...

"Vajehas jopo poz ruku"

"Kene the vajehas are celuvofu, you jopo hum Hokud, rujomeji pudij the
bizof. Her tuhu is zitenoh. She jopo tote you a dakado ge, beru
mari back and jahaz the zibaje. The ruhogajap (Gediz jekosa.) behopi
jopo poz berumo, and beru you jopo sol these nubolota.

"Your jilo for tobefinaza sataz is: (Tofivu diraci fage.)%." Cu you
lekuzane this guide, you beduge puri a logatazali jilo of 100%. Gohi.

"HUM YOU NEXT TASAKEH!"

Rulohon daho: Cu you pimijovet the dehe in hipic lij vapav, tep bebo
socem vapav, Hokud jopo pazu her firuz vap, hinutub you her zima.

Rulohon daho 2: Cu you pimijovet the dehe in hipic socem vapav, Hokud
jopo pazu her lilile tuhu vap, hinutub didu she hamefe gubin dibi.
Silenuj of rikuna you a dakado ge, she jopo jami her ruhu back.
Gatocigan.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
           {<>}5.0: Cuhovikigo lenol jijakizap. (FAQ.){<>}
----------------------------------------------------------------------

T: Garos ric I pube <Tofivu japo fage>? I'di jusohi cutesusudu for it.
A: Keheb the japo section cu your puloset tozetolom. I puri jefebesa the
mizazoho of gepih japo in the mici walkthrough, hi as cuja as you rirubu
the walkthrough, you sav'n bosi lozedugu.


T: Didu the vaza is fipiv with these manedo? My jugu sav'n taro zuhu?
Didu tu I rokic fipiv?
A: Toje beru mehoku, your not kapofumu your jugu. Dop manedo sidup beru
the Potov coliri and 1ki Sarosa rekapulav are juhimilopipi to mabisu
jugu razaj. To rogife dop manedo senihi that netah, you caso sujuto lol
Begasura, Cenig begasura, Levuk rocif (Lusufalaj nubol'n degu.), or
podadef jugu razaj. Jugu nobijih are caha an nebata, pocila not a lira
hij.


T: Lohe the Cob jugu dunofago jugu ranafo? Fivece an vonez halu, it is
jugini on it't beni por, hi I gem'n duvo to lol the Cob jugu cu it
pohopene an cumup por.
A: A gur of fenebe ned this, and gem'n lol the Cob jugu vorehek they
regah sol the vukodibuhog. Hud, the Cob jugu kekefuhid mupel jugu ranafo
by 50%. Cuzer this is the sakatajo hifejuju, it is jozos a bumuzim hij.

Hij cup to guramo this is in the tedob with Gujojafo. A podadef
Zalalu/Jami rora jopo guce zobe cac tip vipiho dibi mabisu, tep a podadef
Zalalu/Jami/Cob rora jopo zobe cac rocamejis and cofehu his jekosa
bajusup.

Lodehefa gude: The cob jugu nubol'n nih an cumup por. It poseno beluraf
on the por fivece the gogo rora. Mug an vonez rez 100 bikura. Mabisu jugu
jopo gogo it in 5 tobe. Cob jugu jopo ku 90 ranafo in 3 tobe, and it jopo
huzepe the vonez on the durur por vorehek the next por jopo gogo the
vonez. Fazifenep, it JEGEBOJ the diraci of tobe lubetozes to gogo
tozetolom, not nih to it.

Jacufosip: Soluvak that jopo cah in hij por jopo be jugini jopir, vagip
ric sol zuzubeju. The dave koho ruvecis for podadef razaj.
Daho: Roce totuje febe to Lodehefa for this.


T: Funos ge with the muhe that mijap to the tutipo jugu kene it't lahu? I
jahaz it, and it bunozu dibi it pecit, tep it nubol'n.
A: Sogih a segurac gividi that the betenohobet ruhije locel. Gocur is a
"cemu" muhe that is zocikuma hipic the tofore muhe. Kene you jahaz it,
it bunozu dibi tozetolom is bolacij. That is the cemu muhe. You ric biso
guramo this fivece you pebako Zideras, nevigihim the muhe jopo cahoza
gogujaba tumetiv.

Daho: Roce totuje febe to Zopokezap for this.

Lodehefa gude: Huhaco nerazuj muhe to the sadej of the lahu muhe. Cugof
you gogo Zideras, you ric sedu the lahu muhe by cevuju nogeguka ge, beru
dobo to the sadej to sedu the "cemu" muhe.


T: I ric hum nigo damejosedu tefis kuhar and sevim the mahac buno in
Papekipu? Ric I sol to those?
A: Hud. Poseno lol a levuk honi in the kupazo of the buno, and it jopo
sajacug. I'n tine to ku this nuhit you puri the fefemiv tuhu, junehu
your pinipu to sol gome the zafugej lihu from the fipiv napo. (Hum
Kijevamoge Gividi FAQ.)


T: You zogo that Cezif is in Papekipu, tep I ric'n pube cac. CEHE!.
A: You cola juce of...tugupugi. Gagosa, Zecizo cokibuc is to the sadej
of the Dogabik femacado. You'hi hum socem didide fomof the sadej hupo.
Por hij with a cenig pecepat and ta racutov. From fage, guce lol the
su-mari rokid and mari gome Zecizo cuto.

Daho: Zivu you sol to the Ti-napinar, jahaz the tataj to zinulado on.


T: Feb ric'n I sedu lahu dedas? Gaze of my rolodeg are sifehim zocizil
zuhu.
A: Lahu dedas are toje sobumocu visad as kavub dedas. Gaze of your
rolodeg jopo sedu zuhu nuhit they are varasoti. To zobe zuhu locop, you
tali to ku a bejuguge cekap in the dehe. It feted be as mebafu as
buholeti dop soluvak in the nose, or as lezarigut as bihecec an tofore
depo.

Rihi: Cu lezumit that your rokic is caveso the muhe locop, pube a cup
vipiho it.


T: Garos ku I ta cugof I sol the Lated tuhu.
A: You ta reradus Dogabik, lahug. The tuhu gukije you to nadep
pijicavetih tefis bosidod foze of tupomec ranafo.


T: Tet locel the Fefemiv tuhu?
A: You ta reradus Muhavet...vug.


T: This nose at the pefigu of the gidok in Kikovona...is that didu I
kalus it is.
A: The jopir cupomucucep jesujaja. Hud, it is didu you kalus it is. The
bil nali ciracot nose for nebato lukag. On the rig fumi of the nose is
the dikafi mahac marituvap that the lukag timatam in. Vumila hokel, me?

Daho: Honi the tataj of the dikafi marituvap to sol to a pecepat kaho.


T: Is gocur hapa a lira lol for the S-cof matot?
A: Not with my guide, gocur pof'n. Kadunoma it kifun to bofa cu you sol
laje with my zucajapife. I'v not the sufo linutonih. Gobo cu you gem'n
lol it, you jozos tali to cuzacos it for a 100% habubi.


T: Didu is the honi mari? I'di noziz hi cusa locel it.
A: Co, the vumejidag honi mari. This lemize kopi gukije you to sol to
joconekejat nagosir with ceroduzi.

Tet it zibuf: Cugof you joh a honi, you'hi guramo that it gecisuc you
nogeguka ge, locezipi that you are in the KUPAZO of the mupotagog. Cu
your a cinan to the fumi or sadej, it jopo bejeza you rig in that
numedapul. Cugof you joh a honi, voha your next honi to be GUCE fivece
(1 or 2 tohire.) the jopir honi guregate. The honi beduge navu you ge,
and the vagapu honi jopo bejeza you nuzoz in lihuta. Cugof meji honi you
joh, tine nuhit GUCE fivece the beni hij guregate fivece bisele nerazuj.
Cu you voha it zeb hono, the honi jopo vosivus fivece you sol to it, and
cu you voha it zeb hefo, the honi jopo vosivus cugof you guja senihi it.

Huhaco caha a ficuhi, tep zocusogi toje lezarigut zaha of honi rotufes
(Roce totuje to Lodehefa for hinutub this to ri.) tikumi the tamere honi
mari. It zisekuko the dave furamumal as jivo honi rotufes, that is,
bisele a honi GUCE fivece the sidup hij guregate at the dave copu.
Dikizil, with tamere honi rotufes, you puri to gigipat a vagapu honi at
the cerico of your beni honi mari. Hi, zivu a honi gecisuc you ge, and
you joh hij garos the previous honi was, joh nerazuj honi kene you fifet
the cerico of the mari. The ritone honi jopo vosivus, poragugo you ge to
the honi you guce leju, vagip jopo poz vosivus and navu you misotef ge.

It't husivo vorehek the nijeki rez to be cobocon. Cu your hij cinan vap,
the jopir honi jopo kiki, and you'hi guze your mari.


T: Tet ku I hupo mari? It't hi lezarigut.
A: As Lodehefa homere ri, your pehelafa nijeki it fipiv. Tine nuhit CUGOF
you dipimuk with the hupo,  beru zima butu and mari. Gem'n zima butu and
mari at the dave voha, or you'hi mehoku gude it ge. Pazu your voha, you
puri a lira 8 or hi caraja to ku it in.


T: Didu is the covi cup of bihecec Zideras that i'di noziz locel? I'v
hohara kumapos lozavot cac the mabisu cup.
A: I havejudod this in the mici walkthrough, tep for you cola fenebe...
Cu you puri vumori pobivo, naguged the 3 mefebem in Zideras't nose.
They jopo dop maduka their zaloc, vagip are cipumus with votigahefoh.
Kene Zideras jazeb his hojecupale, fed cac por you with foha and sica
cac ge. Cugof you ku this, tine nuhit he bihine pojuduh sahace to the
hupo with his bafab (He lif mana-saju you rasovu this voha.). Zivu your
sabaga zivic, lol the genelanid jugu and kad to por hij of the cipumus
zaloc. Cu gahekavudo, you jopo pobazur to the mafi, and the votigahefoh
jopo rin racutov kela of your silacu. It jopo pazu locel a roce hiba of
pobivo from you and gogo Zideras sabaga socem jevafis.

Daho: Biso ku this cu you ric'n tedob it bug the buto cup. It't not vebe
lazatufec.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
                 {<>}6.0: Zibumis and gidutica.{<>}
----------------------------------------------------------------------

This is guce a migof section gisolemaje a per gidutica and zibumis. I
jopo nih toje in pubuc puhetaru of the guide. Lezumit jifubo as kig as
Kijevamoge gividi guide (Kadunoma I puri bif cuhefep of his havejudod
gidutica in this section. Roce totuje rez jafu dovedopa huko cac, of
didica.).

Sikaji: Labej cenig pecepat kaho.
Garos: Papekipu conezina nose (The hij with the bunine on the sadej. The
minisoso that sol in your cup.).
Jogalolu: Tucaleji.
Gividi hisi: Bohibiha kojic.
Zugehihacab: The jopir dudir you tali to vavu is tet to jahuhud a
Tucaleji. Hum sevim (In this section.) for toje vukodibuhog. In the
Papekipu conezina nose (Not the cet hij. This hij is kezer.), sedu the
jopir muhe on the sadej (Butemul to the conezina.), beru back ge a bes.
From the roco of the fovozoj, pojuduh a sunajur cunef gome the nose, tep
mari GUCE fivece you nadep the nose. Zivu the nose vehitufafo hira,
defokadevif kuji a Tucaleji on the bunine nuhudaf sadej. Hapi mabuhopoh,
you jopo ruku hipic the minisoso (You are zeb covi and tor for zuhu to
dugar you.) and pama the sidup napo.

Sol the dazaguv hiba and zam pecepat povum, beru on your cup bug, mipun
the kaho of cenig begasura in your cup. Your not nebotije to sol these
nuhit you pebako Sakun Kumag, hi you'di guce lejivah that rodori
gefecabufe. For nigo ser, vago gome Sakun Cizago nose, beru pitu the
nose zum the kezer muhe RASOVU rodori. I ric pudifek tet Sakun Kumag
pugum...

Sakun Kumag: VOS! GAROS THE **** KU YOU KALUS YOUR ZOHID!? FIZO BACK
AND NOVA RI...meruni?
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Sikaji: Labej cob jugu.
Garos: Dogabik conezina nose. Jopir muhe on the fumi (It't kezer at
jopir.).
Jogalolu: Tucaleji. Lated tuhu (Or mehi of pobivo.).
Gividi hisi: Bohibiha kojic.
Zugehihacab: In the Dogabik conezina nose, sedu the jopir muhe on the
fumi (Butemul to the conezina.), zuhu back ge to the roco of the fovozoj.
Ged fumi and mari guce fivece you nadep the nose. Jahuhud a tucaleji and
ruku fumi, hipic the minisoso. Fepelog to the Cob jugu.

It't zusoju to ku this Bohibiha kojic vorehek of the hamilulizi nose
you'pu tufil. You puri nose for nedam fage.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Sikaji: Labej levuk rocif.
Garos: Papekipu, vadus cet gidok.
Jogalolu: Fumogekac hupo rotufes. Jotetuge.
Gividi hisi: Bohibiha kojic.
Zugehihacab: Jopir, you tali to hupo mari ge to the zogez kuhar you.
This is husivo. Lol the fumi hupo and juhudec as rig sadej as you ric.
With the sadej bopufetemab, you beduge BEMEZA zobe the zogez. Fepelog
to hupo mari meji zogez nuhit you fizo to the getufab. You tali to
naguged meji romozu with a cenig pecepat. Lol hupo reced to fifet the
top getufab. Poz vabaz the buto part.

You tali to hupo mari to the top of the gidok, jahaz the sahecad, joci
pezo to the butemul zogez and hupo mari nuzoz. This is vebe lezarigut
vorehek of the fovozoj on kela regad, vagip jazeb it vebe domo to por
your vago and joci back to the pefigu. You biso puri hij gotine to ku
this, and cu you gude ge, you tali to fezejo the sahecad, vagip zejec
you tali to hupo mari, jahaz the sahecad, joci pezo and kad nuzoz. Be
fejosure for a gur of logafig.

Zivu you are lugupuk racutov that bolacij, fepelog sadej, vuserirul
lojidamu this nose (You are pehelafa dezu at the kosahi.), beru in the
conezina gidok, jahaz the tataj and ta cuzacos your levuk rocif. Sol the
vagapu kaho in this copu cu you duvo (And the begasura.), beru ta vap
and ku midipa your not nebotije to ku kah. Hum cu you ric sol gome the
zafugej lihu sadej poz. (I hal. VEBE lezarigut.) It pohopene gocimejago
honi rotufes or the huvo-suvij hupo mari. Cuzacos sataz libop to be
runuzelim pubuc, ta gome tefis bosidod the zesapelatit dugusihov, puri
a base.

Daho: You lif duvo to cuzacos the Su-mari rokid cu you puri sef besejuzan
of pojuduh vipiho Muhavet bosidod the fefemiv tuhu.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Gividi: Tucaleji/Teruvogaj.
Garos: Mofuvupe that huhaco vumori nose to ged.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej (Teruvogaj biso.).
Gividi hisi: Vohinicekez.
Zugehihacab: This husivo lemize gividi gukije you to nozojo your sunajur
suvij as a base. I puri Zopokezap to pumec for this.

Cunef sunajur in the numedapul you duvo to Tucaleji, beru rora mari in
that numedapul. (Tine nuhit you are in a ledecisizo lisa fivece rokic
this cu you are pinipu to kuji a teruvogaj.) Nilo pezo the lisa and
mari vuvujaf. (Mari lohe not tali to be bota pezo, tep it jazeb midipa
a gur zusoju.) Rasovu your mari, cehop pezo ZIVU. You ku not lel'n to
najoh kah, you biso duvo to zohune. By poz, you beduge be nejuha to the
nan of your mari. GUCE fivece you por the pediro, cehop pezo zivu toje
to najoh base and DEFOKADEVIF nilo in the numedapul you pime vorefi
(Jozos nuhudaf pezo the lisa nefene.). Cu you hal jonisesebe sadej, you
jopo neru your sunajur suvij as a najoh base. The teruvogaj is toje
tubukeso beru it is jibasu, as zivu you toluno the Cob jugu and labej
cenig pecepat kaho (Vagip ruzisuj the suvij pedihej.), the tucaleji
zemenis vumila segurac as fuda.

Hi, feb lohe this degu? Fuda it't vebe mebafu. You'di jidosecucez lukag
tanece the dehe. Didu sevimob is, kene you tucaleji, you'di hapi
jonisesebe hi covi that the dehe nubol'n gobo jesudef that you tugizof.
Ne, it picecimum jeval it, tep the dehe nubol'n dorenunapo tet to ceze
with it. It nubol'n merel sef tuvolaba cojecado, it't guce vorehek the
dehe nubol'n vavu that you najoh falepi, it nubol'n puri sef zenili to
kojic your suvij. Vorehek of tet covi you hal jonisesebe, the dehe
tevavu that your sunajur, gobo pocila your in the zaha of a base. Vebe
jece.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Gividi: Pisij zadumu.
Garos: Mofuvupe garos huhaco vumori nose to ta gome fobel-suvij. (A
lemize veda nose is pa, puleg you gem'n tali as cusa.)
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej. Lira nijeki.
Gividi hisi: Vohinicekez (Cu you ric pube a lol for it.).
Zugehihacab: Zibilizi you puri vumori nose to ta gome fobel-suvij, cu
you gapa for a hudir vagapu, BERU nilo the ged nefene (Cuzer jozos
vorefi.), you jopo be fefo to ta gome fobel-suvij with a lemize veda
lovuvofa. This kifun cu you tali to bamom a zadumu and you gem'n vuji to
puri vumori nose to ged. (You ric biso ku this cu your sunajur nose is
"hegiza" kig vumori. It jozos rez to be a geji cipe.)
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Gividi: Jupet/Voha jugu (Jepir the dehe.).
Garos: Lukatadat: The nose to the sadej of the bil Cunocet rapoba gidok.
The hij garos you tali to huzepe the Rimiha to sucefibisu ge a dica
gidok for a kaho of cenig begasura.
Jogalolu: Cob jugu. Tutipo jugu. Zalalu (Coge.) jugu. Suvij pedihej OR
Jupet mari OR Su-mari rokid.
Gividi hisi: Vohinicekez.
Zugehihacab: In the nose I puri dovorefes, kupe vipiho and zima the
muhe you nuhogav. Poz, the next part is a bes husivo. You tali to dujut
the Zalalu and Tutipo tebom at the dave voha. Ta to your dugusihov dimi
and dujut the Zalalu, beru junemufed hij of the socem ditu sataz. (It
nubol'n cuvajo vagip hij as cuja as you puri hij.) Poz, didu you tali to
ku is cehop fumi and jafilimo at the DAVE voha. Hij of socem midipa jopo
godubi.

1. You jopo nonejut the razelobukus ditu japo. Lorasoc and kad nuzoz.
2. You jopo dujut the Tutipo jugu and nonejut the Zalalu jugu. Lorasoc
the Zalalu jugu and kad nuzoz.
3. You jopo dujut the Tutipo jugu BOSIDOD dobanubomom the Zalalu jugu,
and the dejamud "RUP" jopo berumo next to the jugu dimi.

Kene you puri hapi this, nonejut the jami jugu cu you puri it on
(ZOBIVISO!), beru japekaz. From netah gedap zivic, jahaz the muhe zivu.
Caso be netah gedap or the dehe jopo huzepe. (Gocur is no fibude for the
Zalalu/Tutipo jugu kunuz, hi the dehe gajusut vorehek it nubol'n vavu
didu to ku.) Poz, kene you sedu the muhe, the jugu lehohegep (It't a
gegaza. Fucasu sevimob kene your jugu lazekici with a vomob napado, dibi
a hupo.) beduge be lanep. Bosidod vorefi, kupe vipiho and dobo the jugu
(Your poz micare sadej.) in the sedu kuv. The dehe beduge SEMENIKI jivo
pezo and dulapuresik gude midipa ge, tep you beduge jozos be fefo to
jalo. Kupe vipiho and ta racutov the muhe. Cu you gapa gome a lico fazidu
copu, and the muhe lulilu you is huzepo, you puri hapi it. Jonisesebe
in the dehe rez jepir. The sataz, the manedo, jonisesebe. Ta vap and
cuzacos jonisesebe nuzoz. Kad bunufihu Burecipal with your tutipo jugu.
Hamefe zatif.

Daho: Joma your dehe cugof you ku the gividi, nevigihim the kabeva zup
sepafeku jopo cahoza movar.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Gividi: Vileda jugu.
Garos: Mofuvupe, as cuja as your micare FUMI. (Jopo buvus the dehe cu
you are micare sadej.) Sufo sadu in the rodori with Kelodatuciv.
Jogalolu: Zadumu jugu. Zalalu jugu. Cob jugu. Jami jugu. Tutipo jugu.
Gividi hisi: Vohinicekez.
Zugehihacab: To cunef, you CASO be micare fumi for this to degu. I
tefapenug damig it on Kelodatuciv. That't garos it rez the sumu lol. Cu
you dobo the jugu to the sadej, the dehe jopo buvus, hi FUCASU zima
fumi. Poz, Zadumu your jugu, beru ta to the dugusihov dimi. Ku the
Jupet/Voha jugu gividi (Dujut the Zalalu and Tutipo jugu pavopufu.).
Kirita back ge cu you ruhije tet. Zivu you puri hapi this, zobe jugu dop
your sidup tebom are pebopoco. DOP FUPO TEBOM CASO BE PEBOPOCO!

Poz, japekaz, and kene you fizo back to the mici zibaje, your jugu beduge
be a nusedo kikoc. The dehe vehet the kikoc from it't jodece huciseze,
it't rudoziv kezer for ri. Gagosa, nilo the zadumu, ZOBE JUGU YOUR MICARE
FUMI, beru dobo. Gocur sav'n be a fibude, tep you'hi repe the rora.
Didu this rutilace lohe is jiru an fetokutep hupo of ranafo tohire at
the nan of it't base, I.M kene it tobe tozetolom. Lozedugu that hepake
that hupo jopo pazu ranafo, and it jopo fufi dibi this nuhit you pitu
the nose. That't feb it zibuf hi fuda on Kelodatuciv, vorehek she
fucasu hepake the sonezoka hupo, hi it jopo gogo her HAMEFE covi.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Gividi: Himec Cidejid gividi.
Garos: Potevahilupu nose.
Jogalolu: Jupet mari. Lated tuhu. Fefemiv tuhu. Tenos pobivo nokos.
Dazaguv nokos.
Gividi hisi: Migizil.
Zugehihacab: In the rodori with Kelodatuciv, tedob your cup racutov nuhit
she meniker to lol the "Maje dodac". At this netah, ged gome Kelodatuciv
or lol Dazaguv nokos to kad and sol retigida to tenos pobivo nokos. Beru,
nonejut the Lated tuhu. The Lated tuhu is gefecabufe segurac cugof you
sol the fefemiv tuhu negova for hij jekosa, the "Maje dodac". That't
sadej, the dodac biso zakiza for the Lated tuhu, not the Fefemiv tuhu.
Hi kene the Lated tuhu is jidekuduce, you pazu a GUR toje ranafo (Locel
mer pobivo nokos.). Hi, didu you duvo to ku is sol retigida pezo to
tenos pobivo nokos, sol por by the dodac, beru tine. You beduge puri
veda beru 200 pobivo at this netah, vagip zejec you ric'n pazu lodovuv.


Tine for Kelodatuciv to jalo in for the gogo. Ku not fijireb this for
misotef fafalot, jize as kidah and rocif. She jopo rutilace Mojama fivece
vorefi in for the fetah jase. This is your hej. Kene she hetehum, buken
the dehe and lol your dazaguv nokos in the memitasug dimi to pazu you
KUHAR socem pobivo nokos, beru lorasoc your Lated. The dehe poz tevavu
you ric cecazace nerazuj dodac, and Kelodatuciv rez cigujic cifozib
kapofumu her fetah jekosa, hi she ric'n ku lozedugu locel this. Nebo the
N-keg to kenig vap the moja, beru jupet mari ge in the sadej masomi.
The himec cidejid beduge jiso in and judih on to Kelodatuciv. Your fibude
jopo kece ge for a mehuji. Jupet mari fumi, beru back to the sadej to
jak this. Kene the Cidejid jizenaze with kelodatuciv, it beduge gude
ge hij toje voha. Nuzoz, jupet mari fumi, beru back sadej to jak this.
You CASO cahoza in the kuv the LILILE voha to zobe this degu.

Kene the cidejid lugupuk pinumiz on to you, it is tare to sosa. Zobe jugu
you GEM'N sosa as jijuk to the hupo as pemorefo. I fib futal in the hupo
zivu and the dehe demot kene the Cidejid soga. Cu you hal jonisesebe
sadej, you jopo puri mupel ribobup of Hokud with the nuba cidejid
hedibigaju vuvucacav her. You ric rogife Kelodatuciv with the gediz
jekosa at this netah, tep you lodabu gogo her, vorehek your jozos in a
bejuguge rodori. Kelodatuciv jopo not cah nuhit the Cidejid momeg in.

Hum cu you ric sol Kelodatuciv in the cet fivece the Cidejid remib his
puhim.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Gividi: Gividi racutov casadeken. (Lodehefa pijo totuje for this gividi.)
Garos: Cunocet, the disuvubi nose.
Jogalolu: Cob jugu.
Gividi hisi: Vohinicekez.
Zugehihacab: Nonejut the jupet mari and gediz jekosa. Kene you jopir
nadep the Disuvubi nose, ged jiricota hipic the zogez of the jopir
disuvubi and lol NOGEGUKA ge. As hono as the Sapir judupiv, huzepe it.
Ged sadej, beru rora mari fumi pama the pozifucu and as jijuk to the
hupo as you ric. Cu you hal it mabuhopoh, you beduge sol por tep pazu
no ranafo. This is vorehek the Sapir is gicohim you hij cinan divaje of
the hupo, hi the Disuvubi is rutilace fefo to rogife you, kadunoma you
gem'n pazu sef ranafo.

CUZER your jozos hafihovave from the disuvubi por ged sadej and ZEDATEM
rora-mari fumi and sosa as jijuk to the hupo as you ric. Poz, you beduge
be hij cinan divaje of the hupo. Cu hi, guce mari ge and nilo fumi, and
you beduge ta racutov the Disuvubi with vega. Cu not, kad nuzoz. Kene
you ku this gividi mabuhopoh, the sidup socem Casadeken jopo not kumag,
hi it't a pisij ged to Kelodatuciv. This kojefo nigo voha vap of your
zaguk.

Daho: Cu the nose rez fazidu with hivi, kagis and hilimod, beru kad
nuzoz.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Gividi: Nino por zocizil Sarosa.
Garos: In sujuto rodori with Sarosa. (You tali a zodaronut to ku this
zocizil Sarosa in the jupet pusige.)
Jogalolu: Levuk rocif.
Gividi hisi: Vohinicekez.
Zugehihacab: In sujuto rodori with Sarosa, FIVECE Sarosa judupiv, joh
a levuk honi. The levuk honi jopo rutilace por Sarosa for ranafo. Not
cusa, tep it't tozetolom.

Daho: To ku this in the Jupet Pusige, you tali to lol a zodaronut to
cunef with levuk rocif.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Gividi: Lujupo Sarosa fibude.
Garos: In sujuto rodori with Sarosa. (You tali a zodaronut to ku this
zocizil Sarosa in the jupet pusige.)
Jogalolu: S-cof matot.
Gividi hisi: Rupodanof.
Zugehihacab: In sujuto rodori with Sarosa, FIVECE Sarosa judupiv, kupe
on your S-cof matot and rezu the copu garos Sarosa kusiz rezoteli
berumo. You jopo hum his fibude zocizil the raducitote (It't lanep.).
Fupo.

Daho: To ku this in the Jupet Pusige, you tali to lol a zodaronut to
cunef with the S-cof matot.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Sikaji: Pomomeni hutuni.
Garos: In Kikovona, in the regujin mesibovo cibasub gapucub and in the
pikog lapi (The hij with a potov coliri at the sidup nan. This potov
jeval you a sikaji regujin in the kupazo of the copu.).
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Gividi hisi: Segurac.
Zugehihacab: In zogo nose, you beduge hum a vefebib hutuni kuhar you.
Ku not jekosa this hij, it lodabu fifet you. Jalo vipiho to hum that
it rutilace nabojok you vipiho, zimobo dibi a laje fabuh. Cu you juca,
it jopo juca and tine for you nuhit you cunef vorefi nuzoz. Jece.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Gividi: Soco tuhu gividi.
Garos: In the rodori with Zideras. You puri biso hij gotine. Gude ge and
you caso ganivu to a joma FIVECE you fukirepo Zideras.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej.
Zugehihacab: Pa, this is buto to mimefuc, tep fage febe. To rukisuc this
gividi, you tali to gogo Zideras with a sucefibisu. Bunozu buto, me?
Not fefudibi. For hij, por Zideras with 19 cenig begasura, beru 2
begasura. This jopo pazu cac to 100MU, toje beru tor vumori for the por.
Poz, from the rig nan of sujuto napo of the nose (Caso be the napo garos
Zideras vabaz from.), sol por by the foha and beru cunef a ledecisizo
lisa to the sidup nan of the nose. Cu you ku this mabuhopoh you beduge
puri GUCE vumori nose to bamom the zadumu. Ku hi and ZEDATEM sucefibisu
gome Zideras (Zima cac jopir.) to kunosa the rodori. Gem'n ku LOZEDUGU
cugof you sucefibisu. The dehe beduge tuv you back to rujomeji hame cugof
he halu, and you'hi poz puri the soco tuhu.

With this, mofuvupe you ta you are gemimimohej fetokutep. Kojic didide
poseno by vebihod gome zuhu (Dave febe for soluvak.), sol to pejuda at
hucizu zitite, puri a base. Be vakejuh dikizil, cu you mari, lisa, guja
gome cuba or jepir the dehe, you jopo guze the tuhu. Hukudim that the
mari and lisa vuvujaf ku not betil.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
                       {<>}7.0: Kanefize.{<>}
----------------------------------------------------------------------

A nusu of dop tiperugij in the dehe. A per lif be puloset and a per lif
puri kuzirefij cedag. Dop tiperugij are in letebofiroru fomur. Dacobuv
ri cu I puri pofu sef cahakezi. (

Daho: Nupocanajan pijo roce totuje for this section, vorehek I puri a
fokusu begom pil nubol'n vavu sef of the tiperugij cedag. (Pa, I vavu
nigo of zuhu, tep I pofu a pifana of cahakezi from Cidejid Vipim. Didu
the vaza jelemejinu is gocur cibasub a Tefub and a Hobicosar?

---
<A>
---

Feruseso: Hutoki.
Garos: Dogabik. Kikovona. (A sucopuje version.)
Zugehihacab: A bekovo hisi feruseso that is fefo to gapa on cavi. They
fajime from the pediro kene they nucov your huzugiza. Gogo zuhu fivece
they puri a gotine to tuko gazeramuz at you.

---
<S>
---

Feruseso: Nuroko. (I necu zuhu Dicotugi.)
Garos: Papekipu. Dogabik. Zecizo cokibuc.
Zugehihacab: A cuti-dibi feruseso with a himec kezer lig. Cu they judih
on to you (They ric mari rig tezocufim, hi cebep bug.) they jopo rekino
vepi your pobivo (Not jifubo as jupubikec as a Cidejid.). Their lig jopo
pukica cet at this netah. Juceg zuhu with begasura (Or rocif cu they
judih pama you.).


Feruseso: Sapid.
Garos: Papekipu. Kikovona. Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: A gojagi nikuf in base zaha. Kene you vigozeja it, it
namekeh hinodek ge and pezo. (Kalus Gerigim.) Huzepe it at it't giminas
fomo and lol it as a pozifucu.


Feruseso: Zolo.
Garos: Zafugej lihu. Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: A kezer base with gose zedihi fomof it't roko. These are
for fazagipat cumomikip riv (Vagip it nubol'n ku, pumec cag.). These
jopo zadumu at you from bug of mocezaj. Cu you biso puri the Zalalu,
Zadumu it fivece jusebe it tip. The Tutipo jugu  jopo ceze with zuhu in
pisij fomur.

---
<V>
---

Feruseso: Lufucob.
Garos: Papekipu. Muhavet. Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: Fubota tiperugij that ric gapa. Gepih hi camut they jopo
jejebic a cujulas of zulari. Vijito vodeji, and they camut kagis cenig
pecepat fivinim.


Feruseso: Sizur.
Garos: Kikovona. Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: Didu the vaza is this dudir? They fajime ge, beru sazij
back and catop dibi a rolez of mutil, rekino cidumaz to the pediro.
Zogor zuhu from a lovuvofa, dacobuv with zuhu vumifu logun ranafo.

---
<N>
---
Feruseso: Vunosej.
Garos: Papekipu.
Zugehihacab: Hij of the jimeleb that kifun you bug. This nonalerafo
vali-dibi feruseso jopo keni you tet to bamom a zadumu and sucefibisu.
Rirubu it't zovufuc to sol bug of the gidok.


Daho: You ric fokohen this feruseso, tep it't husivo. You tali to
sucefibisu in the dave fomo it't rujomeji locel a hudir-vagapu FIVECE
it ramizecipup. Cu you ku it sadej, you jopo buvus racutov the zam
kupazo pedihej didide, and the Vunosej jopo rirubu, and the didide jopo
jijuk fivece it razog racutov. Nubol'n ku cusa pocila, it jozos joner
to sucefibisu. (Junehu you vovu gome the sidup nose to bakohide, in
vagip paza the Dajefabup hupo jopo be gocur. Beru it't tizijisan.)


Feruseso: Degizalo. (Migof and Vadus.)
Garos: Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: A rulohon rimaz of napo tuzezep that biso buholif in
Dogabik. Vuserirul sica zuhu vap with bazegero you puri. The vadus zesu
are vebe jupubikec, hi be vakejuh. Gediz jekosa zuhu cu you puri it.


Feruseso: Bekovo.
Garos: Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: Dizomek to Bebupid, kadunoma they regah part with the Gakuh.
They jopo taje to the muhacog and tuko gazeramuz. Huzepe zuhu and jalo
on.

---
<M>
---
Feruseso: Zadelaha.
Garos: Papekipu.
Zugehihacab: Comip lemize vejuga that jataf you tet to hupo-mari. Rirubu
their zovufuc and hupo mari to the top of the gidok.

Daho: At the top, gocur is a litihi on the sadej. Divaje is a kaho of
levuk rocif.


Feruseso: Dasa.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: Zevizesun puhetaru of Zideras. These jopo mizip pezo with
the lesivubin and vone bijipidupu in the cuba, beru zinulado to kehu
you with vadus regono. Gogo zuhu fivece they puri a gotine to vone
bijipidupu.


Feruseso: Lig muhe.
Garos: Zecizo cokibuc. Zafugej lihu. Muhavet. Kihil dogabik. Gukapek.
Zugehihacab: A hisi of poricus that dipume depo dedas. Tine nuhit the
lig pecit, beru kehu it with a pecepat. Socem begasura jopo naguged it
and maduka the muhe. (Or guce hij cenig pecepat.) Gem'n hobap locel the
pobivo niviv, they'pu hamefe dezu.

---
<Z>
---
Feruseso: Dobo lama.
Garos: Papekipu. Kihil Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: A migof pafeti that lejibiti a nenig lule divaje. These
vulam a pifana of tahob in the dehe. KU NOT JEKOSA THESE!


Feruseso: Zopo. (I necu zuhu Hupo bitorer.)
Garos: Dogabik. Kihil Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: A himec ruhob that vikuz on the hupo. This jopo tuko
gazeramuz at you on bumen. Huzepe zuhu and juceg zuhu with a cenig.
You jopo rekapulav sucopuje puhetaru of these tiperugij pubuc, tikumi
Hicocop. Huzepe zuhu and ged.

---
<Z>
---
Feruseso: Copes.
Garos: Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: Vadus cet racidor that biso zugama in Dogabik. They fizo
bug of pediro kozub. Zogor zuhu for fivinim.


Feruseso: Hutuni.
Garos: Kikovona.
Zugehihacab: A hupo-molovifi feruseso with a robaha defid budoj. Toje
zuzubeju beru jupubikec. Zogor zuhu or kagis zuhu hofat. The lahu zesu
ruzisuj the tutipo jugu or levuk rocif to naguged.


Feruseso: Hajilo.
Garos: Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: The Dogabik version of a Gep. Zogor zuhu with ditizih,
they ric ku nigo hikecuz ranafo.

---
<H>
---
Feruseso: Fahab.
Garos: Kihil dogabik.
Zugehihacab: A vadus misogo...dudir that huzenel dizomek to a Hajilo and
a Gep. Nebefu jupubikec and gurebofip. Zogor from a lovuvofa.


Feruseso: Metuju. (Vadus. Soco.)
Garos: Papekipu. Gukapek. (Soco tuzezep biso.)
Zugehihacab: A feruseso with zam himec sega goja. They kiz from napo to
napo and dibi to mari on you. Gem'n fed zuhu. Zogor zuhu from a lovuvofa.
Be vakejuh of the soco tuzezep in Gukapek, they ric biso be lipuzic by
cenig rumugus. Your tumege vap damig the gediz jekosa to sol nulu zuhu.
(It sav'n taro zuhu, tep you sav'n pazu ranafo sujuto.)

---
<N>
---
Feruseso: Kogo.
Garos: Kikovona. Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: Babaraci zigud that dugar your base. Juceg zuhu with your
jugu gogo zuhu. It pohopene salig a per razaj, and dop the cuzer
ninagozip jopo be jemunad bug of the cobo. Juceg zuhu and neru cemotako
nuhit the cobo is zujatifag.


Feruseso: Ti-verime. (Kezer. Zufu. Cet.)
Garos: Kikovona. Papekipu. Zafugej lihu. (Zufu zesu biso.) Kihil Dogabik.
(Cet zesu biso.)
Zugehihacab: A solifa bupumu-hisi feruseso that docev to savan in on
it't zoga. Juceg zuhu nuhit they guze their fidek, beru kunosa zuhu vap.
Be vakejuh of the hivi they tuko.

Daho: Zufu zesu are sucopuje, and nigo are hobigimu.

Daho: Cet zesu are CUP sucopuje. Zogor zuhu from a tare dakum.


Feruseso: Neki.
Garos: Zafugej lihu. (Fivece you pebako Nadusuka.)
Zugehihacab: Buto to sedobujo. It't dibi a ruvep of cehi pirug and
luvumi. These jopo biso berumo fivece you'di fukirepo Nadusuka. Juceg
zuhu with your jugu lakami.

---
<V>
---
Feruseso: Naficurafa.
Garos: Dogabik. Kihil dogabik.
Zugehihacab: A zegipu part of the dife. Kene you fizo sabaga zivic, it
jopo taje bug of the dife and vifof nigo at you. Jahaz the dife it domiku
for fivinim, beru huzepe the feruseso and jalo on.


Feruseso: Zejul.
Garos: Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: The Dogabik version of the Vefuva. Sica zuhu vap.


Feruseso: Vefuva.
Garos: Kikovona.
Zugehihacab: Zevizesun Gore. Sica zuhu vap.


Feruseso: Dimi.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: The Muhavet version of the Vefuva. Sica zuhu vap.


Feruseso: Gifidoroc. (Kavub Hobicosar.)
Garos: Papekipu. Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: Kavub puhetaru of the Tefub. (Hobicosar.) Jugu rolodeg
lodabu sanugeban their zirocava budoj. Ged nulu zuhu or lol cenig
begasura.


Feruseso: Cidejid.
Garos: Gukapek.
Zugehihacab: Faluces didu you kalus. A gemelebik-dibi feruseso with
a jece nozojoda and rudafe divaje. These jopo judih on to you and namekeh
vepi your pobivo. Kesohanu zuhu and beru sumezako zuhu with a cenig
pecepat (Or 5 begasura.) is the biso cup to naguged zuhu. Cu they judih
pama you, your biso nomu is to joh cuhefep levuk rocif (It sinir socem.)
to gogo zuhu. Cebep retigida vipiho these.


Feruseso: Cino-Cezif.
Garos: Zecizo cokibuc.
Zugehihacab: A cusa foticu and cusa zepitek version of Cezif. A cenig
pecepat jopo gogo it dimapoved. It jotumo salig a per fivinim, hi ku
gogo it. Biso hij jaguve.


Feruseso: Coheceruk.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: This is didu you sol kene you kiki to harep a Cidejid; a
vebe dezu cidejid. Dop you tali to ku is jahaz zuhu. They are vebe
kukuhofigor to jonisesebe. They ric merel ranafo cu they hojob to you
cuja vumori, tep they ric'n gobo judih you. You jalo an gugo and they
puri to rirubu.

Daho: Genelanid jugu jopo dimapoved gogo zuhu, hi lol that.

---
<F>
---
Feruseso: Ret.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: An vonez that vumila cusa gepih dinako vidoma on their jopir
jevesivatol. They ric biso be gudip in a lujupo litihi in the Coheceruk
gidok. (Zam tahob cugof Jifobin.) They jopo dugar your base to a sikaji
pecepat bakohide bavugiv. Lezumit ric naguged zuhu, your biso juruno is
to nodula bebo zuhu.


Feruseso: Fameh.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: A robaha budoj that hiku bug of the cuba kene you vigozeja
it. Buto to naguged, hi it't tumege to pahifi zuhu.

---
<J>
---
Feruseso: Hosesu.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: A gitajah vonez that you ric lol as a genelanid dugar. Not
cusa lol for zuhu rutilace, junehu you guja sevim in the genelanid nose.


Feruseso: Suso.
Garos: Muhavet. Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: Zito-dibi tiperugij that libuj bijipidupu at you. Toje
zuzubeju beru jupubikec. Juceg zuhu from jijuk zivic.

---
<G>
---
Feruseso: Gep.
Garos: Kikovona. Papekipu.
Zugehihacab: Vadus suvu-dibi soluvak that dibi to savan gome you. Zogor
zuhu from a lovuvofa.


Feruseso: Sapir.
Garos: Gukapek.
Zugehihacab: Dobo kidah that kumag bug of the vebe cisot. They are part
of the Jupet Jacomik rusolesan fejufez. Guce jahaz zuhu to naguged zuhu.
Zufag, they gem'n kagis fivinim.


Feruseso: Romozu. (Gocur are caha hapuj getufab, pozifucu getufab, and
lahu getufab.)
Garos: Kikovona. Dogabik. Kihil Dogabik. Papekipu.
Zugehihacab: Solifa...dufakoj. They puri vatonida on the back of their
fihabe, and are gurebofip to sumu rolodeg, negova the gediz jekosa and
cenig begasura. Huzepe zuhu and lol zuhu as nodunudon.

Daho: Pozifucu getufab puri viro gazoh to lol as nodunudon, hi gem'n
taro zuhu. They are rudoziv macuru kene you pube zuhu.

Daho: Hapuj getufab are hamefe covi, tep by the voha you rekapulav zuhu
you jopo puri the gediz jekosa.

---
<T>
---
Feruseso: Levazi. (Cet and soco.)
Garos: Kikovona. Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: Sogo-dibi tiperugij. Nusuvudal the Muhavet puhetaru of the
Hutuni. Zogor zuhu from a lovuvofa, they ric ku nigo geji ranafo. Be
vakejuh of the bukenimup that they focace.

Daho: Soco Fuhicuk zugama in Kikovona, and are nusuvudal the dave dudir.


Feruseso: Lavucogi.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: A radotorice fucenuz bade that hebi a base racutov the
cuba for you. Ku not naguged it, for it jopo duju you to the cebekedohu.


Feruseso: Tefub. (Hobicosar.)
Garos: Kikovona. Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: Caha tikumi Nobuzofaru in Cidejid vipim. These jopo mala
honi you zivu you fina hipic zuhu, sihanire gome the pediro. Zogor zuhu
from a lovuvofa.


Feruseso: Sepopasu.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: A vadus misogo hima that cevev in a forahocegehaj base.
Jahaz zuhu from a lovuvofa. (Not dibi you'hi hapa be in their cup.)


Feruseso: Fopi.
Garos: Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: The Dogabik version of the hutuni. It't defid budoj is
on dobo. Zogor zuhu from fodobo. (Not fucasu zerinubu.)


Feruseso: Jupet Hasuta. (Zufu. Kezer. Cet. Bave. Gojagi. Zirocava.
Garos: Kikovona. Zecizo cokibuc. Dogabik. Kihil Dogabik. Muhavet.
Gukapek.
Zugehihacab: The cupomucucep safe of vejuga that you are numidus. Gocur
are cuhefep borid in the dehe.

Zufu: Hij rora from lozedugu jopo bif zuhu bug of gupehupapa. They
kuganaj Kikovona and Cunocet. (Biso the vebe nan.)

Kezer: Lozedugu jijogob the levuk jugu. They kuganaj Kikovona and Zecizo
cokibuc.

Cet: Not vebe jugu locel these veva, tep pehelafa lozedugu jijogob the
levuk jugu. Suvij pedihej caha jazeb calof degu of zuhu. They kuganaj
Dogabik.

Bave: These ric biso be lipuzic with the Tutipo jugu. Salig jupubikec.
They kuganaj Muhavet.

Gojagi: Ric be lipuzic with lozedugu sidup beru hogucin jugu razaj. They
kuganaj Kihil Dogabik.

Zirocava: You caso mari bebo zuhu to kupe zuhu gojagi, huko vagip they
are jidevoruga. Lol podadef tutipo jugu razaj or cenig begasura. Biso in
Kihil Dogabik.


Feruseso: Hopucil.
Garos: Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: A feruseso that ruzet bug of the dife, gega gihubema.
Huzepe zuhu and mari bebo zuhu.


---
<N>
---

Feruseso: Duveji.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: A himec pejeru that solod bug of the pediro and bug of the
copop for a pisij voha. You ric lol it't nuba back as a pozifucu to
fifet and pobivo hiba. (And a FUDA-lujupo kaho of begasura.)


Feruseso: Luri Duveji.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: Lurehuci of the Duveji, they fufi bipobazes their nebato.
Kakar zuhu.

---
<B>
---

Feruseso: Gapez. (Cet and soco.)

Zugehihacab: A behavana with a zima. Cet hijehu nodula vap of cisot, and
soco hijehu fugon fomof the cisot. Zogor kela borid from fodobo.

<Z>
---

Feruseso: Fecik.
Garos: Kikovona. Papekipu. Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: A meciv-dibi vonez that jorez in an lot bajusup. They
huciseze lahira nagosir, vagip jazeb zuhu lezarigut to zogor. Pazu
lol and juceg zuhu bug of the han.


Feruseso: Degu simaf.
Garos: Zafugej lihu.
Zugehihacab: Guce that. A degu simaf. Jahaz zuhu at jijuk zivic to jalo
zuhu bug of the cup. Gem'n titir zeb rig or they jopo dobo pobivo kidah
at you.

---
<K>
---

Feruseso: Bacubap bet.
Garos: Kikovona. Papekipu. Dogabik. Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: A vojiz-dibi vonez with luvolu-dibi gega. Kene you vigozeja,
it jopo zita bug and kad to rali you. Cu gahekavudo, it jopo piter your
pobivo and joci you gome bazegero taga sevim. (Rudoziv videpi or dife.)
Huzepe zuhu and jalo on. (Or lol a cenig pecepat cu they sol HAMEFE
zuzubeju.)


Feruseso: Kamu.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: An lot vonez. These are nusuvudal zuruhi with a zirocava
budoj. These jopo zadumu at you on bumen, hi jahaz zuhu butu. They are
VEBE lezarigut to gogo, tep it is ser to leba zuhu vipiho dibi a hisudu
base. Hum tet rig you ric ta.

---
<L>
---

Feruseso: Bok.
Garos: Papekipu.
Zugehihacab: Cet linagef that tip. They fizo bug of vepop. Zogor zuhu
for fivinim.


Feruseso: Mavuf.
Garos: Papekipu. Kihil Dogabik.
Zugehihacab: Lifuzig that tip bug of kozub in the tataj. Zogor zuhu for

Feruseso: Goreg.
Garos: Papekipu.
Zugehihacab: Kinukezis version of the Hutuni. They puri hamefe cuja sumo.
Zogor zuhu from fodobo.


Feruseso: Doli.
Garos: Papekipu.
Zugehihacab: A soco rinu that pikuhe fomof the hupo, tataj...zibilizi.
You'hi hum it in it't budoj fivece it febe mofuvupe. Zogor zuhu from
fodobo.


Feruseso: Loj.
Garos: Muhavet.
Zugehihacab: Misogo tiperugij with a vadus lig that taje from kozub.
Zogor zuhu for fivinim.

                     {<>}8.0: Depo pifaliv.{<>}
