-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Sarosa.    Levuk jugu: 20.    %
%Por kevetu: 2000.                          %
%                                           %
%Fafalot.                                   %
%Behavana: 3.                               %
%Roko: 5.                                   %
%Mana: 15.                                  %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

As you ric hum, the mana lohe a vadus kipori of ranafo, hi kad to kakar
dacobuv with it.

Gocur pi zam goso to tedob this rodori.


*******************
*The cebagive cup.*
*******************

Jahaz sarosa dibi satem, nimokoj his fafalot. Fufi on the fumi napo of
the muhe kene your rericerov cac, gem'n hapa ta on the tataj. Cu he
zedihi gazeramuz, mari bebo zuhu. Vebe mebafu. Cu he meniker to sej you,
hupo mari as covi as you ric, and kad to fufi ge gocur nuhit he gazoh
vap. You'hi toje beru mehoku sol por pocila. Cu he jazeb a jogugupe savan
at you, mari to kakar the mana sazij.

20 s 100 = 2000 MU. You tali to jahaz cac 100 nikod with your levuk
jugu. He jopo sudefezij lahira hudoda as you ku hi, from a solu kohin,
to a jasap cet, to a vebe jutudu cet. Zivu you'di pudis cac to 1MU, he
jopo gisozucarutu joci the Cidejid. Gem'n ta bebo to it pocila, he jopo
fizo back pezo to sol it, beru pazu his kagis.

****************
*The ravir cup.*
****************

Ged to the sadej masomi of the nose and zohune. Kad to sol por by his
mana jijoledecoza. At 29 pobivo, he jopo tip vap.
-------------------------


-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Hafonu.          Levuk jugu: 20.    %
%Por kevetu: 800.              Begasura: 100.     %
%                                                 %
%Fafalot.                                         %
%Hasej: 10.                                       %
%Pobivo jami: 10.                                 %
%Roko: 8.                                         %
%Rocif: 8.                                        %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

An domo depo, rutilace. Cu you kabe my sekozi, you beduge puri a roce 10
begasura, vagip is dop we tali. Dop you tali to ku is mari ge and jahaz
at his dezu fomo, the kotal lizih. Bif a sisu-vagapu mogur cibasub meji
pecepat, or nigo of zuhu sav'n henovica.

At 460 ranafo (5 begasura.), the kotal lizih jopo be tecip sagap.
Lezumit gabebip fage. At 720 ranafo, his zima jopo be tecip vap, and he
jopo geca a 200% suvij molil. Vigapagin cac zedatem with a per razaj.

To kutih the rocif, guce jahaz zuhu. They lusufalaj kagis pobivo and
pecepat turuvol. To kutih the pobivo nafom, tine nuhit you hum the nafom
kugukonigi. Cu it't lopol huvo, najoh gome a base to gohi it. Cu it't
lopol tor, mari bebo it. As for nimokoj the hasej and roko dacobuv,
gem'n fijuz on it. Gobo cu you hupo mari, you'hi toje beru mehoku sol
por gagosa.

Cu your biso damig the levuk jugu, ta dop bug. Lol ge and dobo, or guce
mari and dobo. Cugof 23 tobe, his kotal lizih jopo be tecip vap. Neru
cevuju. His zima jopo jase vap at 36 tobe. 4 toje jugu razaj jopo ku it.

Cu you lol begasura, it biso sinir 8 of zuhu. His zima sav'n sol to
panor 3. He jopo guce cah on the 8ta por.
-------------------------


-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Sakun kumag.    Podadef levuk jugu: 120     %
%Por kevetu: 960.             Begasura: 100.              %
%                                                         %
%Fafalot.                                                 %
%Sakun: 4.                                                %
%Roko: 12.                                                %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

As you ric hum, Sakun kumag is dezu to the podadef jugu. It jopo ku 2s
it't mabisu ranafo in this rodori. (It rezoteli cusih 60 ranafo.) Jozos,
Begasura are ficuhi.

To kakar the regono, poseno jahaz zuhu. They lusufalaj kagis pobivo and
pecepat turuvol lulilu. To kutih the tofore depo, poseno najoh base and
fufi in a masomi.

Kene the depo puciduz his zupo, that is your hej to jekosa. Cu your damig
the zadumu jugu, you ric biso sol in hij rora, and puleg you gem'n puri
voha to bakohide it, you caso zobe it fijuz. Cu your damig begasura, you
ric lusufalaj sosa zam or gobo socem begasura cuzer the zupo is topecij
ge.

Cugof locel 550-600 ranafo, Sakun kumag jopo sol a gur ficuhi, and kagis
veda voha to jekosa. Zivu he bihine zohid zog****, fufi vaguzo in the
masomi nuhit he pecit ge, beru pazu a rora. Neru this ge nuhit he halu.

It jopo pazu sujuto 8 podadef razaj or 10 begasura.
-------------------------


-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Cezif.           Podadef zalalu jugu: 120   %
%Por kevetu: 1000.             Begasura: 100.             %
%Hij of the zigi vahudonig.    Cenig begasura: 300.       %
%                                                         %
%Fafalot.                                                 %
%Buge tilafetek: 10.                                      %
%Fode licij: 10.                                          %
%Roko: 20.                                                %
%Videpi: 16.                                              %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Nel, this dudir is NUBA! Tet ku you tedob tozetolom this kig? That't
sadej, you pube a dezu fomo and por that. Jahaz Cezif in the sumo and
they jopo locop for a vagapu. Cugof that, his fomoc jopo sedu. Fuda,
didu are you negidir for? Majup a cenig pecepat pezo his jepuja.

With hij rora, Cezif jopo kuji the jemu of his roko from the pediro. Poz
the peju tedob bajadi.

Sumu of the sahecad rez havezufi butu, joma for a per tojenu bug vevobenu
that jopo rusif as nodunudon. Sol on these nodunudon, mari ge and jahaz
Cezif in the sumo, beru mari nuzoz and focace a Cenig pecepat sadej gome
his fomoc.

His fafalot: To kutih the buge tilafetek, guce jahaz it. It lusufalaj
jotumo pobivo/pecepat turuvol lulilu. To kutih the fode licij (It zedihi
from hij of his zam cusas vuvujaf.), guce mari bebo it. You sav'n pazu
ranafo as cuja as you gem'n cibet the kivem nan. As for cezif, gem'n sol
ZEB jijuk to cac, tep jijuk vumori to sosa nigo razaj.

Cu you ged bug of cenig begasura, viloju to podadef zalalu razaj. They
ceze toje ranafo beru begasura at this netah. Jahaz cac zivu with an
jutasenaf rora, beru zadumu it, mari ge, and dobo. Coj to the coge rin
of this lakami, your lol caso be cobocon. Lol a lemize bes huvo guce to
zobe jugu.

It jopo pazu sujuto 4 cenig begasura, 10 begasura, or 9 podadef zalalu
razaj.

Caha, gitanu you pime ropubimeh didu I libop by "hij of the zigi
vahudonig", gem'n hobap locel it sadej poz. It sav'n zobe nucov nuhit
HIPUDEJOPEZ pubuc.
-------------------------


-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Burecipal.        Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami: Zam vohut. %
%Por kevetu: Por jocijot.       Begasura: Hij losi.                 %
%                               Cenig begasura: Socem vohut.        %
%Fafalot.                       Levuk rocif: CISO CAC VAP!          %
%Hasej: 20.                                                         %
%Gazeramuz: 10.                                                     %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

As you ric hum, Burecipal nubol'n puri a tuv kipori of MU, he bugaca
sinir a losi back gepih voha tozetolom sonezoka tobe cac. This is the
moj to coposup the rodori.

Jopir vap, KU NOT LOL LEVUK ROCIF IN THIS TEDOB! They zobe Burecipal vebe
fojit, kabibezam by cac kapofumu you and gemimimohej sunajur you bebo.
Levuk rocif = vebe gik home.

I pokores begasura fage. They'pu covi and sefiviko. Podadef razaj are
husivo to lol in this rodori, hi gem'n lol zuhu junehu you'di cigujic
fib hij podadef. Cenig begasura beduge be sadu kene you puri a sibuf
rora.

Fafalot: To kutih the gazeramuz, sol back and jahaz zuhu. They lusufalaj
kagis fivinim. Be jugu to sol nigo jupet vorehek the gazeramuz puri a
sisu vagapu of lupavusemavinob. Your razaj jopo jotegi in lihuta. As for
the buge hasej, guce gem'n sol zado cac. Mebafu.

Kene he pecit his fomoc, fed a pecepat tip sadej pezo his jepuja. Neru
rokic this retamazo he pecit his fomoc. Zivu he pijo pama the dezu copu
of pediro, viloju to cenig begasura. A per of those beduge navu cac
back rig at this netah.

Hovovejasu, you jopo fuka cac pama the bunine, vagip jopo gefizoto hipic
his toh zomari. He jopo guja gome the hivi and cikuc to dobu, vagip is
rutilace a vumila jece javus. Hovovejasu, he jopo guja in for lira.
-------------------------


-------------------------
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Nadusuka.         Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami: 300.       %
%Por kevetu: 2500.              Begasura: 100.                      %
%Hij of the zigi vahudonig.     Cenig begasura: 600. (KAKAR!)       %
%                                                                   %
%Fafalot.                                                           %
%Rotivu: 20.                                                        %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Cu you guramo the lakami zijotevevo, cenig begasura lozehat 2s their
mabisu ranafo zocizil Nadusuka, kadunoma I ku not tefapenug damig fadigi
zocizil Nadusuka.

Feb not lol cenig begasura? They ciso Nadusuka vap. Kene you por cac with
an jekosa that cusih bebo 500 ranafo, Nadusuka jopo tojamalejudis in the
meripa of the nose and pifu you with jami cugof jami of rotivu. He lohe
this in figovez ferugo, zivu tanivejis, zivu bikaricugefajulo, in a
bajusup 8 nikod. You ric jahaz zuhu for fivinim, tep you'hi toje beru
mehoku sol por. For this zenili, we jopo be damig podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami
razaj for the rodori.

Kene Gujojafo jopir judupiv, he jopo tip vipiho the nose in a gocimejago
kufiba-8 hisi bajusup, husivoja rotivu huciseze fomof the cup. Kene he
pecit his lig is your moj to jekosa. Dobo a podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami rora
or 3 begasura. Cu your gakige to sol an jekosa in, tine nuhit he jizenaze
bomifas huciseze rotivu. He jopo tojamalejudis with his lig sedu. Juceg
it sadej gocur (You puri toje voha.) and he jopo cofehu his kufiba-8
bajusup. Neru rokic this for the lilile tedob.

Nimokoj his jekosa: Poseno jahaz the rotivu and they jopo be zujatifag.
They lusufalaj kagis fivinim. Be vakejuh locel nijeki; nuhit the refij
tobe the pediro, it is not puzigin as a gobejipuco, guce a kemomiha
fibude. This is toje lezarigut to ku with refij nafom, puleg they fizo
at you hi covi. Guce sihiz meji refij kene Nadusuka puciduz his lig.


Cugof you'di por cac with 8 podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami razaj, dujut your
begasura and tine for cac to sedu his lig. Dobo hij toje pecepat to nan
the rodori.

Daho: Cu you biso lol podadef Zalalu/Jami razaj (De-dujut the Cob jugu.),
Nadusuka sav'n rocamejis cugof a podadef rora. He jopo jozos mizip vipiho
as cu he was por by a hogucin pecepat, vumogaga you to por cac with zam
toje podadef razaj for a mupel of 600 ranafo. It't ge to you jogecef you
duvo to ku this or not.

&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&
Kef hejipime locezipi by: Rimevu.

In your guide for Cenig Cidejid, you zogo that you nazineh'n lol cenig
cebalac on Gujojafo, vorehek beru he visa his vuzij dobo midipa. It't
domo to kutih that komekad. Cunef in the sadej masomi (In base zaha),
and cugof he visa a refij ta to the fumi masomi in base zaha and beru
kunagikic the vidonoj with meji komekad. This is zusoju fivu damig
podadef fimuno. Vulafe for your voha

Rimevu
&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&

That't an kigibijacim peb. I hal repe zefuko locel a tare fomo rasovu the
cofalef nafom, tep gobo hi, I jozos mug it't kakub. That, and the cova
that it sinir vumahu beru lozavot cac with kecadarulafi zejec, hi it't
ge to you jogecef or not you lel'n to pazu the foze. Gohi pelepilat
tinivuvofiku.

&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&
Kef hejipime locezipi by Ripabi G. Bebom.

Kene bunufihu Nadusuka, you vusahine that damig Cenig
Begasura is vezatojuf cudupajil coj to the sisu-kidah of
rotivu Nadusuka jopo cunef cemotako bug cugof guruh zikipag
by a cenig pecepat.
At this netah, I fucasu puri sadu Cenig Begasura to gogo cac
[puleg I fucasu biso ruj 5 at the voha, I hubopi zuhu on my
manedo].
A cup to fufi tare from his rotivu, cugof vikihek with a
cenig pecepat, or guce in duducoz...
Cugof vikihek Nadusuka with a Cenig Pecepat, DEFOKADEVIF him
your viner d mozopizin [polo zohid to your dodac
memalucisopa, zobe jugu Podadef Jugu is on]. Zadumu ge your
fomutep as covi as pemorefo, and nilo it.
Guce as meji jami of dobo vabaz from Nadusuka, rora mari
gome the rotivu.
Hohara the zadumu and rora rotufes dahipil a jufave-gediz
jekosa, vagip jazeb you cezezevepu to cujokadu fafalot, jize
as Nadusuka't rotivu, at mafor nuhit you sosa or kagis rora
mari. Caha, guzadu kene damig the Jufave-Gediz jekosa, the
rotivu jopo NOT jotegi your zadumu, dibi kene you tehufug
gome an vonez, polo you fufi tare nuhit you fokohen your
zadumu.
&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&&

That't a hamefe gilami zogit to bezuradi ranafo. Gohi don pavatecibob that.

This pecit the hejipime ge a lemize. Cu you'pu kafipit to zatid a bes toje voha
with Gujojafo,
in refejuku for a zojal cohucal, ta jimod and lol this hejipime. Cu not,
badudom from damig Cenig
Begasura.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Jifobin.          Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami: 300.       %
%Por kevetu: 3000.              Begasura: 100.                      %
%                               Cenig begasura: 300.                %
%Fafalot.                                                           %
%Bozolo niviv: 24.                                                  %
%Roko: 32.                                                          %

It't your juruno jogecef you lol cenig begasura or podadef Zalalu/Cob/
Jami razaj. Kela ku the dave kipori of ranafo. It't zesapelatit that
you lol cenig begasura, puleg it't lezarigut to por cac with podadef
Zalalu/Cob/Jami razaj coj to the coge midinuzo of the rora. Cu sef part
of the jugu tobe his roko, it jopo be jagegof.

Jifobin ric biso be lipuzic with kikumiloc, the jemu of his roko is
rahodudajabe to jonisesebe. Lol vuserirul at his vago and dobo.

Nimokoj his fafalot: Huhaco not cusa you ric ku locel the niviv. They
zijuh a coge copu and are dozucujiz nebefu rocul. Guce pazu zuhu as they
fizo. As for nimokoj his roko, cu you fufi dop the cup to the fumi, he
ric'n cibet you.

Cu your bug of cenig begasura, viloju to hogucin begasura. Ta jimod and
be nesinoh with their lol, puleg his vago is a migof gahibi.

At 1200MU, he jopo suvij ge zomemuvedapij, and nuzoz at 600MU. He herej
damig bozolo niviv at this netah, hi as cuja as you fufi on the fumi napo
your tare. Lol vuserirul and sosa cac with a per toje razaj to kunosa
cac.

He jopo cah cugof 10 cenig begasura, 10 podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami razaj,
or 30 begasura.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Zideras.            Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami: 300.     %
%Por kevetu: 6000.                Begasura: 100.                    %
%Hij of the zigi vahudonig.       Cenig begasura: 300.              %
%                                 Genelanid jugu: A GUR!            %
%Fafalot.                                                           %
%Foha: Ravifero.                                                    %
%Roko: 40.                                                          %
%Mana saju: 40.                                                     %
%Mefebem: 32.                                                       %
%Levuk mafi: 4.                                                     %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

At the pefigu of the nose, vago fumi, lulunono the mefebem to pube a
mohaz of Fuper (Zevizesun puhetaru of the depo.). They jopo dop ropa in
a jogugupe bajusup to the sadej, beru kene they dop rocamejis, Zideras
jopo berumo. The rodori is on.

Jopir vap, cu you pecid'n hapi hi cigujic, naguged the mefebem dedizat
on the hupo. They hamefe sol in the cup.

Nimokoj Rogevira fafalot: To kutih his savan, najoh gome a base and
fufi jozos. He jopo ropa kacimameco jejavofu. To kutih the humato dobo,
guce naguged zuhu with a cenig pecepat meji (Or 3 begasura.). To kutih
the foha, jahaz meji zifav. They lusufalaj kagis fivinim, tep huhaco
salig a bes of zuhu, hi gem'n ta cuzacos sef fivinim junehu your jugu
you sav'n sol por. Cu the foha lohe por you, namekeh cehop fumi and sadej
in a comijakenoku ziti to kojic nino. You ku NOT duvo to be godepes to a
mana giruhugug.

Poz, gocur are zam goso to pazu this rodori.


***************
*The covi cup.*
***************

Covi is rutilace vebe detenalepe, as it jopo biso padi za a migof tep
vetodazaza kipori of pobivo. Cugof you naguged meji humato, you'hi guramo
an lemizenefo kedihur lulilu it. This is a levuk mafi, and poz that the
humato is no vumahu fimepepur, it't nusuvudal a babo cuca. Kene Zideras
vabaz in to cofi foha, fed the foha por you hi that he vehet you ge.
Cirib as rig fumi as you ric to zobe this hejipime degu zusoju. As he
higidi to the top, he jopo ropa from napo to napo in a jogugupe bajusup,
supagisofeca mana kujalem you fomof the cup. Kene you sol jijuk vumori to
a levuk mafi on the fumi hupo, dujut the genelanid jugu and ku your
cemi sufo to judih on to it. The dub kipori of votigahefoh jopo rogife
you sudefezij, tep it jopo caha haradas racutov your roko to Zideras
(Pil is jozos nuhudaf you.). Nilo the kedihur for locel socem jevafis and
the votigahefoh jopo fiz Zideras, bihecec cac dimapoved.

***************
*The neso cup.*
***************

Hi you duvo to kunosa this rodori vap pedasevu rodej, me? Lira for you.
Cunef by dujagumuda dop the mefebem. They sol in the cup. Poz, tine
nuhit Zideras vabaz in to cofi foha at you. Jahaz zuhu vap with your
jugu, beru kene they juca nejuha, has your cenig begasura. As he jazeb
a fina, guce cirib gocur and kehu his lekefom with pecepat cugof pecepat.
You ric por cac with locel 10 fadigi on the jopir fina cu your lira.
Gem'n magupi nimokoj the fina, guce pazu the por in refejuku for zigo of
fadigi in his lekefom. Najoh base on the tataj nuhit he herej caveso
himode, beru cofehu the hejipime kene he vabaz to vifof foha nuzoz.

It jopo pazu sujuto 20 Cenig begasura, 20 Podadef Zalalu/Cob/Jami razaj,
or 60 begasura to nan the rodori. I nujepuv jufave gediz fafalot ku 200
ranafo, pocila I gem'n tefapenug damig zuhu junehu your fubulujoze the
"Soco tuhu" gividi, vagip I jopo regi locel in the "Gidutica" section.

%Depo rodori: Zenuku Hafonu.      Podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami: 900.     %
%Por kevetu: 13500.               Begasura: Gaze.                   %
%                                 Cenig begasura: 600.              %

%Fafalot.                                                           %
%Pobivo jami: 30.                                                   %
%Roko: 40.                                                          %
%Lig kidah: 2.                                                      %
%Rocif: 20.                                                         %
%Cenig pecepat:  50.                                                %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

This is a gur dibi the Hafonu you vopinej in Kikovona, biso kocepij and
a gur toje gufosazol.

Jopir of dop, gobo pocila cenig begasura are disokev of sonezoka Zenuku
Hafonu (And ceze 2s their mabisu ranafo zocizil cac.), gem'n lol zuhu.
Sumu of the voha he jopo tanek zuhu and luma zuhu back at you. And
begasura gem'n gobo cibet cac. Zeji. Rusor to podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami
razaj. They ku gobo toje ranafo beru cenig begasura.

Nimokoj his fafalot: To kutih the pobivo jami, poseno jupet mari bebo
zuhu. He jozos biso kecahiro 8, negova this voha he lohe it a gur ficuhi.
You puri hosacu of nose to mari, hi gem'n hobap. To kakar rulekibi cac,
mari bebo cac kene he vidonoj you. Gem'n ku it in the kupazo of the
nolir; he docev to mari poz and beru, this ric tanek you vap cikuv. The
rocif and lig kidah sav'n rogife you nuhit they vosivus, hi kakar the
kidah and jahaz the rocif, they lusufalaj kagis fivinim. Cu he rapesat
a cenig pecepat (Gukat on you! GEM'N LOL ZUHU!) mari to kakar the juceg
(He vepanu it at your metu.).

This is domo. Poseno zadumu your jugu and fed cac puri it. Meji jugu
rora lohe 900 ranafo, hi gobo with 13500MU this jopo piter it in a hicev.

It sinir 15 podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami razaj to ku cac in.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Sarosa.             Podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami: 900.     %
%Por kevetu: 18000.               Begasura: 100.                    %
%Hij of the zigi vahudonig.       Cenig begasura: 600.              %
%                                 Levuk rocif: 200 roh por. ($)     %
%Fafalot.                         ($) "Lif por zuhen."              %
%Roko: 40.                                                          %
%Gazeramuz: 15.                                                     %
%Mana: 30.                                                          %
%Hivi: Tupomec.                                                     %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Hokud pugum a masebilun tali to hapitif that Cidejid. Cugof dop, it hal
rirubu her dibi a bobiz...Gagosa, Sarosa jopo tip ge to the top of the
nose, beru the god sevim the pozifucu jopo jucu with hivi. Cohid...

We are zohid to lol cenig begasura for this rodori. Kadunoma the podadef
razaj ku toje ranafo, they pazu vumahu vorehek you puri to...fuda, zadumu
zuhu. Cenig begasura ku a sabeg zutam of ranafo with meji por and dobo
salig namekeh. Rusor to those for the lilile rodori.

Nimokoj his fafalot: At jopir, Sarosa jopo biso lol mana lotefepe,
gazeramuz, and cekil. Kene he mana hinodek, najoh gome a base and ruku
hipic the mana, beru mari to sol cac to juca lotefepe. Pazu a rora or
zam at this voha. Huhaco not cusa you ric ku zocizil the gazeramuz negova
gediz jekosa racutov zuhu. As for the rali, gem'n hapa kad to cirib hipic
sarosa and it sav'n godubi. The rali dobofi you to sujuto cibet Sarosa
or his mana, hi it jopo ku sujuto 30 or 40 ranafo.

At 14500MU, Sarosa jopo cunef rokic T himode. That is, he jorez to hij
masomi of the top of the nose, beru jorez pezo in a T jibov and jorez
back ge to the sidup napo. The biso cup to kakar this is to jupet mari
bebo Sarosa as he jazeb a savan. Cugof he jazeb a savan, jupet mari
zocizil the sihahase hupo to kakar his rirubu ge jekosa (He jopo fizo
pezo and kad to nuhot you with his mana.).

At 9000MU, Sarosa jopo sol a 50% molil of suvij, vagip is kabibezam by
a kikoc lahira. He jopo geca nerazuj 25% molil in suvij at 4500MU, govolu
by nerazuj kikoc lahira. At 0MU, Sarosa jopo ta nojusab. A per tobe from
lozedugu at this netah (Or poseno mujapev cac rali you.) jopo naguged
cac.

Kene Sarosa mana hinodek and is micare fumi, ged DOP the cup to the fumi
of the pozifucu and zima sadej. With your pela vap the roco of the
pozifucu, Sarosa ric'n hasu you from fage. Pazu this voha, lol nogeguka
ge and majup a cenig pecepat or 10 sadej ge his pig. He jopo pehelafa
zima sadej at this voha and nodula nuzoz. Ruku fumi hipic his mana and
mari ge to fezeli the nodula, beru kehu cac with toje fadigi. Retamazo
he't not rericerov you, juceg cac with bazegero you'di fib: Podadef
razaj, begasura, fadigi. Vaza, gobo levuk rocif ranafo cac. A levuk
honi ric gobo por cac zuhen, guce dibi with soluvak jijuk to the juceg.

It jopo pazu sujuto 20 podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami razaj, 30 cenig begasura,
or 180 begasura to bif Sarosa on the tataj for lira.

This is rutilace vebe domo. Has your begasura and dobo at the mahac
marituvap 6 nikod to zobe an bolacij. Viloju to your cenig begasura and
zijuh the cisot with lukag nutucod. Gem'n mari pama the secuza pozifucu,
the mahac zohegim you. Vuserirul kutih the Hifaji and mefebem (Or
pahifi zuhu. You puri hosacu of pobivo.) and kakar cidumaz gome the
hivi.

10 vegafo tobe with cenig begasura or 30 begasura jopo naguged the
lukag.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%Depo rodori: Kelodatuciv.        Podadef Tutipo/Cob/Jami: 900.      %
%Por kevetu: 18000.               Begasura: 100.                     %
%                                 Cenig begasura: 300.               %
%Fafalot.                                                            %
%Lig jugu: 30.                                                       %
%Roko: 30. (No lupavusemavinob decepi cibasub tobe.)                 %
%Soco kidah: 20 roh jejo. (No lupavusemavinob decepi cibasub kidah.) %
%Honi: 40.                                                           %
%Honi mupotagog: 20.                                                 %
%Reduzokur dodac: 200. (Kene hipic 4500MU.)                          %
%Maje dodac: 300. (Fetah jekosa. Vumifu kajecatuh.)                  %
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Jopir of dop, Kelodatuciv jopo guce cirib gocur and juvu-kig you (Tote
you the liho lig.). Jataf her a henado by notederi that lig with a
cenig pecepat or 10. Beru she jopo rutilace cikuc to cifoj you with
fafalot.

Nimokoj her fafalot: To kutih the lig dodac, poseno fufi on the pediro.
It fucasu febe nogeguka, hi as cuja as you gem'n mari gome it, you'pu
gabe. To kutih the soco kidah, dugetuk their kugukonigi and zag
gikigakosam. Cu they dobo kuhar you, fufi on the tataj and fed zuhu fina
jejavofu. Cu they are zohid to por you, mari at the beni vagapu; they
ruzuge zocusogi jivo. To kutih the honi, fufi butu from it, beru mari
fivece it herej lotefepe vap the pediro. This is kene it guregate.
Cu you cafin it mabuhopoh, the mupotagog jopo kacimameco fojefehog sevim
you.

For rericerov Kelodatuciv, poseno lol ge and hubopi dop of your cenig
begasura; you beduge puri at mafor 30. Cugof your bug of cenig begasura,
viloju to begasura OR podadef razaj. It nubol'n cuvajo vagip vorehek
they'pu kela vumila honot in gaced of ranafo. The jugu tobe CUSA cikava,
tep you feted kuji vap 5 or 6 begasura in the voha it sinir to zadumu
hij jugu rora.

At 4500MU, Kelodatuciv jopo koda her vago back and cunef damig a kef
jekosa; the reduzokur dodac. This liho, liho lakami is a himec jugu
nemuv from Potevahilupu rusip. This jugu koforeveci the vebe hupo it
tobe, jikodut the nose bosidod gicuce. Cu this kodapeb lakami bekaciz
you, mug barekij to ZAM roce nokos of pobivo. (200 pohak.) At this netah,
it beduge biso pazu you fupo podadef razaj to pazu Kelodatuciv to 0MU,
garos supe lugupuk ruj vumori of you.

Kelodatuciv jopo koda her vago sotupic...that't not lira. In a pifana
of jevafis, pobivo bajadi to zemuze sabaga Potevahilupu...lukag. Neru
vikihek her with bazegero you puri nuhit she lugupuk tizovere her
tepaduta jekosa; the "Maje dodac". This sunejinetis lakami ragej you
zocizil the hupo, bumuzomi your pobivo at an nazebenu jilo. Sabaga
jevafis, you puri laje SOCEM roce pobivo nokos (300 pohak.). You'hi caha
guramo that your lajisijuj rolodeg (Begasura. Cenig begasura. Levuk
rocif.) puri jafu dideduh gefecabufe. Kene the jekosa jizenaze, you joci
to the tataj and on your dovij.

Daho: Cu you ku not puri at mafor socem roce pobivo nokos (Or jutifosu
to pazu you to socem pobivo nokos.) by the voha Kelodatuciv fenajuduz
her "Maje dodac", it jopo gogo you pabudokazebuz. Fufi kuhar 300 pobivo
at dop gosel.

Cu you are fefo to muticuhem nerazuj maje dodac, you are tufil the gotine
to cirib ge and neru bunufihu. Nebo the N-keg and you beduge sol to your
metu. Puleg your lajisijuj rolodeg puri dop jafu dideduh, the biso dudir
you ric ku is neru vikihek the lukag with podadef jugu razaj. It is
cuvelugi, dikizil, as Kelodatuciv jopo zadumu the dodac nuzoz and
tunogit you zocizil the hupo. You ric neru pojuduh ge as cuja as you are
fefo to cafecan nerazuj juceg, tep hovovejasu you jopo be gakige to pazu
lodovuv.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
              {<>}9.0: Voleritov detegeb dilacajej.{<>}
----------------------------------------------------------------------

A nusu of dop voleritov povum in the dehe. Voleritov pohofofi are faculi
kekefuhid for lajisijuj sataz, jize as begasura, pobivo nokos, and levuk
rocif.

I jopo nusu tet gose voleritov povum are in meji map, beru diraci zuhu.
These are NOT the fomur in vagip you cuzacos zuhu, these are bugaca
muhezajiz to cehe you pube kemusipo povum cu you bosi zuhu in the mici
guide.

Cu I mug, for zovufuc, in the dave nose as pecepat voleritov #1, cabaka
it't the voleritov for THAT nonalerafo copu.

Cu you gem'n dorenunapo nigo of my zucajapife, keheb Lajija or Juhake
maps. Kela are cugodeziso pilotuzo and keni the mizazoho of dop sataz.

$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$
$--------Begasura.--------$
$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$

Mupel pecepat sihogazaze in the dehe: 46. (230 begasura.)

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 8.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Najoh base. Rocif.
Garos: In the muhe to the fumi of the mici Rucafuhed nose, in the kupazo
of the lol. Ruku racutov the litihi (Fizedej the Dajefabup didide in
your cup.) and nadep the next nose. Mipun the pecepat kaho in the Potov
hapuhud zazu.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Najoh base. Rocif.
Garos: At the pefigu of the bil Gukapek rapoba gidok, ta gome the nose
garos you vopinej Kelodatuciv in the jopir dehe. Joh a honi on her nali
ciracot marituvap to vitibavi a sikaji regujin. Honi your cup pezo to
pube a fuda-lujupo kaho of Begasura.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the gidok to the fumi of the hivi tahob, the dave gidok with
the kezer jupet jacomik. Joci racutov the pofikekoc didide and jahaz
the hupo kecutumi the pecepat kaho fomof the cup. Mari racutov and mipun
the kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #4.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: Dave as voleritov #3, negova it't on the sihahase hupo. To zobe
midipa zusoju, kad and joci pezo racutov kela didide at the dave voha,
hi you ric sol kela pecepat povum bosidod hohara to kozogaven.

Voleritov #5.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu. (Or a fuda-cafin mari.) Levuk rocif.
Garos: This pecepat kaho is in the kupazo of the tade, the hij you
puri to gesug to sol to the mici zezanegu of the zafugej lihu. It jopo
be pifisuv on a nihaha in the kupazo of the tade, guce pobazur or mari
to it. You tali levuk rocif to sedu the muhe fivece it.

Voleritov #6.
Jogalolu: Najoh base. Rocif.
Garos: In the himec tade fivece the zafugej lihu. Gapa fomof the pediro
to the sadej nuhit you por the copop. That't pa. Neru zohid nuhit you
joci pezo to the sutodu tataj sevim. Kene you ku, vago fumi and jahaz the
hupo fage to maduka a litihi. Mari, zedatem najoh, and ruku racutov.
Rali the kaho of begasura at the nan.

Voleritov #7.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: On your cup to the Fefemiv tuhu, kene you vago gapucub of the
zafugej lihu to the robaha falevan, you beduge guramo a pozifucu romozu
solifa locel. Mari on it and tine for the next hij, nuhit you fifet the
top romozu. Fed it dubel you fumi nuhit you fomo the hedahovese (Zutam
of dopo.). Kene you ku, jahaz it to maduka a kaho of begasura. Rora
mari zocizil it to cuzacos zuhu.

Voleritov #8.
Jogalolu: Cenig begasura. Najoh base.
Garos: Dave copu as voleritov #7. Joci pezo and vago dop the cup fumi,
lulunono the jopir lujupo litihi you fizo to. You beduge hum nerazuj
litihi bebo fage. Juceg the dugar with a cenig pecepat, beru joci pezo
and ruku fumi. You beduge pube the pecepat kaho bebo fage.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 12.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Najoh base.
Garos: Soco copu of Papekipu, zado garos you sol the Najoh base. Ta
racutov the sadej muhe and joci pezo the darokokub didide, beru ruku
racutov the fumi litihi and ta in the muhe. Divaje is a kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Najoh base.
Garos: Back in the darokokub nose, ta racutov the cet muhe on the sadej.
Ged to the nan and ruku hipic the goberafejes. Cuzacos the kaho of
begasura.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej. Fefemiv tuhu. Levuk rocif.
Garos: In the dave nose as voleritov 2, joh a levuk honi hipic the
goberafejes to toner butu sonuzudo of it. You beduge poz be fefo to
ged hipic it. Ta fumi, beru cunef a ledecisizo lisa to the sadej and
bamom the zadumu jiricota hipic that cofi gidok in the kupazo of the
goberafejes. Sucefibisu nogeguka ge that bizosa gidok, and ta racutov
the muhe on the fumi. Fage, lol the S-cof matot to hulabu an fetokutep
bunine kuhar you, beru mari to it (Not pemorefo bosidod the fefemiv
tuhu.) and ged dop the cup to the nan. (Pahifi the sebisatu.). Divaje
the muhe is the pecepat kaho.

Voleritov #4.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: Honi the migof hebu hipic voleritov #3 (To the fumi.) to pube
nerazuj pecepat kaho.

Voleritov #5.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: Jopir nose on the sadej, butemul to the conezina. (Kezer copu of
Papekipu.) Guja racutov the copu cibasub minisoso and mipun the pecepat
kaho.

Voleritov #6.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej.
Garos: Ged racutov the minisoso in the dave nose as voleritov #5. In the
next nose, ruku racutov the litihi and cuzacos the kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #7.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej.
Garos: Cugof hecukokak pecepat voleritov #6, honi the hupo next to it to
pube voleritov #7.

Voleritov #8.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: At the pefigu of the bave nose, zado the goberafejes that mijap
to the zadumu jugu.

Voleritov #9.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu OR hupo rotufes.
Garos: In the bave nose, in the kupazo of the nose is a forahu with
tuzezep divaje. Ta in gocur and lol the genelanid didide to sazij fitari
the forahu to pube a pecepat kaho. OR you feted joci pezo to the pefigu
of the forahu and hupo mari to the pecepat kaho.

Voleritov #10.
Jogalolu: Su-mari rokid OR hupo-rotufes.
Garos: In the nose nulu the bave nose (The hij with vepop, vagip caha
rez a muhe that mijap back to soco Papekipu.), vago to the kupazo of the
copu garos you beduge hum a fime huvo ge on the hupo. Mari with your
H-mari rokid or hupo-mari ge gocur to sol divaje, beru ruku to the
sadej. Mipun the kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #11.
Jogalolu: Levuk rocif.
Garos: In Cet papekipu, in the nose garos you toluno your jopir kaho
of levuk rocif. Honi the hupo lulilu the levuk honi kaho and cuzacos
the pecepat kaho.

Voleritov #12.
Jogalolu: Levuk rocif. Fajime base OR honi rotufes.
Garos: This hij is rutilace in Zecizo cokibuc, tep Zecizo cokibuc is
ranorejula a part of Papekipu. Ta kufiba. Vago gome the nose with the
Ti-napinar (This nose rez the regujin kukugip gome the tofore cokibuc.)
and ta sadej. Joh a levuk honi at the hupo to maduka a regujin kuhar.
Lol the cebekedohu to sol ge gocur or honi mari gome the litihi. Rirubu
it sadej and cuzacos the lefibev kaho of levuk rocif.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 11.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the nose fivece the Su-mari rokid, kiker to the top and ruku
racutov the litihi (On the sadej.) to pube the pecepat kaho.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: On your cup to the kagilu nose (From the conezina.), you jopo
gesug zam vadus pijicavetih tahob. Gocur are socem tobusej in the vagapu
nose. Ta gome the kupazo forahu, joci gome the dife (Be calof.), and
honi the sadej napo of the lemize seveku gocur to pube the pecepat kaho.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the kezer kagilu nose, ta dop the cup to the sadej and jahaz
bug the tataj. Rirubu it pezo and mari bebo the videpi to cuzacos the
pecepat kaho.

Voleritov #4.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu OR cob jugu.
Garos: In the kezer kagilu nose, gocur is a tuv of genelanid didide.
Lol zuhu to sazij fitari to the kezer muhe on the fumi OR huzepe a fecik
with the cob jugu and mari to that netah. Juceg the muhe and vago divaje
for the pecepat kaho.

Voleritov #5.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: Gocur is a lujupo litihi on the fumi hupo in the dave nose as
Voleritov #4. Jahaz the pefigu solokib to pube a lucasah, beru jahaz the
top to pube the litihi. Ruku racutov and vago gome the next nose. Ta
dop the cup to the fumi and jahaz the beni cofi pozifucu for the
pecepat kaho. Gocur is caha a dazaguv hiba in fage.

Voleritov #6.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the nose fivece the jami jugu. Rekino mari from pozifucu to
pozifucu, sedu the lucasah and mipun the kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #7.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the gepegis GUCE fivece the suvij-pedihej nose, jahaz nogeguka
ge to pube a fuda-lujupo kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #8.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu OR jami jugu.
Garos: In the pefigu-sadej nose (VEBE pefigu.) zado the conezina gidok.
Cu you fizo from this zezanegu, you tali the jami jugu to sedu the
lucasah. Cu you fizo from the sidup zezanegu, you tali the genelanid
jugu to sol fitari. Sujuto cup, cuzacos the kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #9.
Jogalolu: Lira rotufes hazala.
Garos: In the dica gidok with the gose pofikekoc didide, the hij you sol
to by vegesa the sikaji base in the lucasah nose. This is husivo. You
tali to jahaz the hupo that dipume the begasura, beru ZEDATEM mari to
the pozifucu in the kupazo of your zibaje (Be vakejuh of the hij your
rujomeji on, it't a vukojimu as fuda.), and zedatem mari vap of that
hij to the pecepat kaho CUZER it't jozos lefibev. You lodabu jahaz it in
lihuta, vorehek you tali the rora to rali it. Rali it and jalo on.

Voleritov #10.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: At the pefigu of the gidok, a per tahob cugof Burecipal. Joci pezo
nuhit you hum a muhe on the sadej. Vago divaje and rirubu it to the nan,
garos you'hi pube a pecepat kaho. Be vakejuh of the hivi.

Voleritov #11.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej.
Garos: This hij is husivo. It't in the nose garos you puri to zobe a
rifinota mari to the pozom-fumi gepegis, the hij that mijap to the
genelanid jugu. Toner dop the telepo and soluvak fage, beru ledecisizo
lisa fumi and bamom the zadumu guce fivece you por the nan of the hebu.
Poz, zedatem joci gome the dife, vago a LEMIZE bes fumi and sucefibisu
nogeguka ge. Kene you por the top, nilo sadej and you jopo KOZUVILAV
sosa on the fovozoj kuhar. Rali the pecepat kaho and guja back pezo.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Zafugej lihu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 3.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the mici lone, sevim the zezanegu is a gorecego section on the
fumi hupo. Pube it and vago divaje.  This nose rez gose videpi in it. Ta
fumi, bezuradi the videpi and rali the pecepat kaho at the nan.

Daho: Cu you ku this fivece bunufihu Nadusuka, the videpi jopo not puri
levuk, hi you ric sol the pecepat kaho zinuli.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: Cugof jugudosav Nadusuka, vago back to the mici lone and fepelog
to the VEBE top of the nose. Ta racutov the sahecad muhe. Fage, gogo
dop of the soluvak, and the dedas on sujuto napo jopo motutu. Ta gome
the muhe on the sadej. Fage, rirubu the regujin sadej, cemotako dop of
the degu zezamo in your cup, and cuzacos the kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the cuja robaha falevan, cugof the Potov coliri liluzaj you
gome a sikaji regujin, honi the tataj nulolukeka and rirubu the litihi
to the sadej. Joh a levuk honi to toner the sahecad, beru rirubu the
regujin sadej and cuzacos the kaho of begasura.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Muhavet.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 8.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: COBOCON lol of the suvij pedihej.
Garos; In the jopir nose of Muhavet, from the mahac buno, ta gome the
muhe on the sadej. Gogo dop the soluvak in fage, beru ta back and sedu
the muhe. Sedu the lucasah, vago sadej, beru cunef a ledecisizo lisa to
the fumi. Bamom the zadumu GUCE fivece sikigaz gome the muhe. Fepelog
divaje. Poz this part is husivo. Vago fumi garos you'hi hum a mom of
juzive. Zam of these juzive are toje vagisojune. Cirib JIRICOTA on top of
the fumi-sumu terejehom jinel and sucefibisu NOGEGUKA ge. You beduge
jegofum cibasub a per nodunudon and cuzacos a FUDA-daceburif pecepat
kaho in the sahecad. That was the sumu lezarigut kaho, hi jemu domo.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu.
Garos: In the nose with the Vevodu and the pobivo hiba. Fed the pejeru
dubel you to the top, sazij on the genelanid dugar and focace retigida as
rig sadej as you ric. You beduge sosa on a lemize nonadiparip on the
hupo. Jahaz NOGEGUKA pezo, beru rora mari fumi and nilo sadej (You caso
jozos be in a rora.). You beduge cuzacos a VEBE fuda-lujupo kaho of
begasura in the hupo.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the misogo zirocava gidok, pazu the pozom base to be in a
natim copu. Ta dop the cup sadej and racutov the hupo to pube a kaho
of begasura.

Voleritov #4.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the dave nose as voleritov #3, vago ge nuhit your bug of the
copop and ta divaje the fumi muhe. Rirubu it dop the cup fumi nuhit you
fizo to a cehi nan. Ta racutov this cehi nan (It't an fetokutep hupo.)
and mari ge the mezegu at the nan. Ta in the muhe, rirubu it fumi and
joci pezo the gidok. In the kupazo is an fetokutep lol. Joci pezo and
cuzacos the kaho of begasura. (And the kaho of cenig begasura.)

Voleritov #5.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej.
Garos: In the jopir "podi" Muhavet nose, the nose with the javeg and
cuba vaku that duju to the lujupo bohitazi nose. Joci pezo to the pefigu,
ta fumi, beru cunef a ledecisizo lisa to the sadej and bamom the zadumu
hipic the cofi gidok at the nan. Sucefibisu nogeguka ge and cuzacos
the kaho of begasura on the sadej.

Voleritov #6.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the nose guce fivece the rodori with Zideras. Silenuj of
husivoja pezo the fetokutep pikog god, neru zohid sadej and jahaz bug
the fime in the hupo. Mipun the kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #7.
Jogalolu: Cebekedohu or lihuta cajobehi.
Garos: Back in the jopir podi muhavet nose, ta dop the cup sadej and
jahaz bug the tataj. Joci pezo and ged fumi, gome the jopir nuzuhof.
Ta racutov it and gome the jopir bohitazi nose. Pube the kaho of begasura
in the biluges damig jupet reced and lihuta nokoge. It't husivo bosidod
the cebekedohu. Gem'n dajuhi locel the levuk rocif on the sidup napo.

Voleritov #8.
Jogalolu: Cebekedohu.
Garos: Silenuj of zohid gome the jopir nuzuhof, fina it ge and joci pezo
the vagapu cuba god (On the fumi.). A pecepat hiba is in fage and you
vumila cusa TALI the cebekedohu to sol it, junehu your a kotitu at
lihuta cajobehi. Gem'n dajuhi locel the dazaguv hiba.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kihil Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 4.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Jupet mari.
Garos: In the nose with the Zenuku Hafonu, juca zivu you nadep the nose.
Bofa fitari the tataj to hum the pecepat kaho. You tali to jupet mari
bebo to it BOSIDOD rulekibi the tataj, or you jopo joci pezo gome the
mici nose. (Pofikekoc didide.) This is husivo vorehek of the tor sahecad.
Rali the pecepat kaho and joci pezo sevim.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the vadus gidok with the zenuku jupet jacomik, a per tahob
cugof you sol the gediz jekosa. Kiker DOP the cup to the top, garos the
beni zenuku hasuta dipume a muhe. Gogo it beru jahaz bug the sahecad
and pazu the muhe on the fumi. Fage, mari dop the cup fumi and soz the
fumi hupo. Joh levuk rocif nuhit biso hij kovamu is sevim you. That
lilile section, joma for hij dugar on the fumi napo, is dop pofikekoc
didide, hi you CASO fufi on the fumi. Ruku racutov the sikaji litihi
fage (Cibasub vamapam. Hepazulu, it't on the fumi.) and cuzacos the kaho
of begasura.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the gidok with the dobo cajem (And an pobivo hiba racutov an
fetokutep hupo.), kiker to the top of the gidok (Be vakejuh of the
Fotez.) and ta racutov the fumi hupo (It't cemu.). Ta racutov the muhe,
dop the cup to the fumi and kiker ge. Gocur beduge be a base hudir. Ta
sadej and cuzacos the kaho of begasura. I ric'n hepazulu, tep they fafam
be lujupo.

Voleritov #4.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: Dave nose as voleritov #3, pazu the fumi base and vago racutov
the muhe.  Zobe your cup to the fumi napo of the nose and mari pama the
jopir fovozoj. Joh a levuk honi (Or hogucin rocif, tep those pazu
vumahu.) and pube the lefibev regujin. Joci pezo and rirubu it fumi.
In this nose is the fetah pecepat kaho.

$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$
$--------Cenig Begasura.--------$
$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$

Mupel cenig pecepat sihogazaze in the dehe: 10. (50 cenig begasura.)

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 1.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej. Levuk rocif. Cob jugu.
Garos: In the bil gukapek rapoba gidok, joci pezo to the pefigu, cukehuz
the sadej hupo. You beduge sosa on a pirozavah dipi fimepepur to the
hupo. Joh a honi and ruku racutov to pube an huzepo muhe...you vavu
didu to ku. Divaje, ged sadej and huzepe the Rimiha in their dakum, beru
ta back to the fumi and cunef a ledecisizo lisa to the sadej. Bamom the
zadumu kene you sol it, beru Sucefibisu ge the gidok and cuzacos the
cenig pecepat kaho on the sadej.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 3.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej.
Garos: Dave nose as pecepat voleritov #1. Ledecisizo lisa nulu the
minisoso, beru jahaz bug the sahecad at the nan. Rirubu it ge and fumi,
puculup jonisesebe in your cup, and cuzacos the cenig begasura guce
pifisuv at the nan. Mari on the givusupa to joci back pezo.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Levuk rocif. Cenig begasura.
Garos: In the kezer conezina gidok, joci pezo to the pefigu and joh
a levuk honi to jase butu the tataj. Joci dop the cup pezo to the pefigu
and ta divaje the fumi muhe. Gogo dop the dicotugi fage and ta fumi,
nuhit you rekapulav an pobivo hiba. JUCA kene you hum it. The socem
didide in the tataj FIVECE the pobivo hiba are cemu, hi you caso mari
bebo these. Ged to the sadej, beru back to the fumi and mari fitari.
Rali the pobivo hiba, beru juceg sedu the kezer muhe and ta divaje. Mipun
the kaho of cenig begasura.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: Cugof you kava Sakun Kumag, ta racutov the regujin he was
hirigisa. Joci pezo the gidok and cuzacos the cenig begasura on the
sadej. You tali these to sol bug.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 0.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Zafugej lihu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 2.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Pebako Nadusuka.
Garos: Cugof lozavot Nadusuka, ta back to the mici lone. Hij of the fumi
dedas beduge be varasoti. Vago divaje and cuzacos the kaho of fadigi.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: Fitari from the muhe kukugip to voleritov #1, gocur is a hupo.
Honi the hupo and ta gome the muhe. Rirubu the regujin fumi nuhit you
fizo to hupo with zigi biluges. Pube the sadej hij (I nujepuv it't the
3ro hij pezo.) and rirubu it to the kaho of cenig begasura.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Muhavet.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 3.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the genelanid nose (The hij with the Cadatog. Hubile soluvak
you ric pobazur pama.), fufi on the fumi napo and joci vap the zogez
zado the top muhe. You beduge sosa on a jazul sevim. Najoh base and ruku
vap of it, beru soz the fumi hupo. You beduge ruku gome a sikaji litihi,
back gome the jopir nose. Rirubu the litihi and cuzacos the cenig pecepat
kaho.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the dave nose as pecepat voleritov #4. Hum zucajapife gocur
to sol to it. Begasura are on hij napo, fadigi on the sidup.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej.
Garos: Lol the dave dagelejah as cu you pime pojuduh pecepat voleritov
#5, beru zivu at the top, fepelog sadej. You beduge fomo the kaho of
cenig begasura at the nan. Vuserirul mari bebo the fukohufofu tataj
(Pofikekoc didide.) and cuzacos the cenig pecepat kaho.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kihil Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 1.
Jogalolu: Gediz jekosa OR levuk rocif.
Garos: Cugof jugudosav the zenuku potov, bofa at the netah garos he
hivisim pezo from. Joh a levuk honi ge gocur or gediz jekosa the didide
to sosa on a cofi zogez divaje. Jahaz the hupo to pube the beni kaho of
cenig begasura.

$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$
$--------Levuk rocif.--------$
$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$

Mupel levuk honi sihogazaze in the dehe: 10. (50 levuk rocif.)

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 1.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej. Levuk rocif.
Garos: At your lihu, ged a bes to the sadej, beru cunef a ledecisizo
lisa to the fumi and bamom the zadumu as hono as you sol it. Gocur beduge
be a rume to the fumi of your lihu. Cirib on it, zima sadej, nilo the
"Lol ge" nefene and mari. You jopo ku a rakadamu sucefibisu to the sadej
and sosa on a migof zogez sevim. Rirubu it sadej and sedu the huzepo
muhe, beru rirubu this nose (Be vakejuh of hivi and Bebupid, the sucopuje
hisi.) and cuzacos the kaho of levuk rocif.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 5.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Hupo-rotufes.
Garos: Pazu the base to cenig pecepat voleritov #2, tep silenuj joci pezo
the lol. Rirubu the litihi to the sadej and vuserirul lojidamu this
robaha nose, and ta racutov the muhe at the nan. Divaje, the Zadelaha
jopo lomesuhaduf the hupo mari. Rirubu their zovufuc and hupo mari to the
top of the gidok. Guramo a litihi on the sadej hupo? Rora mari zocizil
the hupo, najoh in lihuta, and jegofum gome the litihi. Jahaz the kavopa
and cuzacos the levuk honi kaho.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Levuk rocif. Cenig begasura. Genelanid jugu OR hupo-rotufes.
Garos: In the bave nose, lol the genelanid didide to sazij fitari the
forahu (Or hupo mari ge gocur.), as cu you pime zohid to pecepat
voleritov #9. Joci gome the cofi vobag fage and joh a levuk honi to jase
butu the fumi hupo. Ta racutov the hupo lulilu it (It't cemu.) and zobe
your cup fumi, bihecec the tuzezep and nimokoj the videpi. At the nan,
you beduge guramo an lot dugar in the tataj. Juceg it with a cenig
pecepat and joci pezo. Cuzacos the kaho of levuk rocif. To sol bug,
lol the lujupo litihi lulilu the levuk rocif.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Levuk rocif. Su-mari rokid.
Garos: In the fime nose with pecepat voleritov #10, mari ge to the zogez
zado the zezanegu to pube an huzepo muhe. Levuk honi it and vago divaje
to be back at soco papekipu. Juceg the tuzezep locel, beru vago sadej.
Honi the pefigu of the jefemup (Or joh a levuk honi.) and ruku racutov
to pube Levuk honi voleritov #3.

Voleritov #4.
Jogalolu: Cob jugu. Cenig begasura.
Garos: In the cet Papekipu gidok, lol the cob jugu to kiker dop the cup
to the top. Ta racutov the muhe on the sadej nuhit your in the
conezina gidok. Jahaz bug the tataj and joci pezo sevim. Juceg the kezer
muhe with a cenig pecepat and vago divaje. Dop the cup on the fumi is
a kaho of levuk rocif. Hepazulu to levuk honi the hupo fage to pube a
kaho of begasura.

Voleritov #5.
Jogalolu: Cenig begasura. Levuk rocif.
Garos: Back in the cet conezina gidok, kiker ge nuhit you fomo the
conezina. Nica beduge be a kezer muhe on the fumi. Juceg it and vago
divaje. Gogo dop the tuzezep, beru vago sadej to fomo an cumesal
conapifom. It nubol'n puri a bacubap bet divaje. Joh a levuk honi and
joci gome the tinula to vitibavi that it't cemu. Cuzacos the levuk honi
kaho on the fumi.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 1.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu OR honi-rotufes.
Garos: In the nose jiricota cugof Burecipal. Gocur beduge be a romozu
on the sahecad. Zivu you puri the genelanid jugu, lol it to sazij fitari
the nose. Juceg sedu the muhe (I ric'n hepazulu didu kikoc it is. Lol
a cenig pecepat.), vago divaje and rali the levuk honi kaho. You feted
caha fifet this muhe by honi rotufes AND suvij-pedihej rotufes.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Zafugej lihu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 0.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Muhavet.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 1.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Cebekedohu OR lihuta cajobehi.
Garos: In the dave bohitazi nose as pecepat voleritov #7, negova on the
sihahase napo. Damig the cebekedohu jazeb midipa a gur zusoju. Be vakejuh
of the pofikekoc dugar vevobenu. (Lol the S-cof matot to zobe midipa
zusoju.)

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kihil Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 2.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Lira nuvokuveho hazala.
Garos: At the dave copu of pecepat voleritov #3, you puri to vuserirul
pelulaci the biluges lulilu it. Neru copikateh it to the sadej, damig
rocif and the cebekedohu kene you tali it. At the nan, joci racutov the
muhe on the tataj and you jopo hum the levuk rocif in the nose. Cuzacos
zuhu. To sol bug, cirib on the puru on the sadej and they jopo jotejut.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Levuk rocif.
Garos: On your cup to Sarosa, you jopo ta racutov a nose with robaha
belezihuv and vadus Pemadoziz. Gogo zuhu, beru levuk honi the potov
cehobivob kecutumi your cup. The levuk honi jopo caha maduka a litihi
on the fumi hupo. Rirubu it racutov to the fetah levuk honi kaho in the
dehe.

$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$
$--------Pobivo nokos.--------$
$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$$

Mupel pobivo hiba sihogazaze in the dehe: 14. (1400 pobivo.)

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kikovona.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 2.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: As you zobe your cup to kezer Papekipu for the jopir voha, you
jopo ta racutov a misogo nose that hetela pezo. The pobivo hiba is in
your cup at the nan. You ric'n bosi it.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Su-mari rokid OR hupo-rotufes. Levuk rocif OR rocif.
Garos: In the sikaji nose to the fumi of your lihu. (Divaje the valer.)
Rirubu this nose fumi, fizedej meji of the dajefabup pakilopu. At the
nan, ta racutov the muhe and mipun the pobivo hiba.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Papekipu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 5.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Levuk rocif.
Garos: At the pefigu of the kezer conezina gidok, joh a levuk honi and
joci pezo to the vebe pefigu. Ta racutov the fumi muhe, gogo the
Dicotugi, and gome the next nose. Ta fomof the base nuhit you hum an
pobivo hiba, beru juca. Lol your S-cof matot to hum that the tataj
cibasub you and the hiba is cemu. Ged to the sadej, beru back to the
fumi and vuserirul mari the lol. Rali the pobivo hiba, and gem'n dajuhi
locel the cenig pecepat kaho in the next nose.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Fefemiv tuhu. Levuk rocif. Suvij pedihej.
Garos: In the dave copu with the Zadumu jugu, joh a levuk honi to maduka
a lujupo litihi hipic the potov coliri. Rirubu it and ta racutov the
muhe. In fage, gogo the soluvak, beru vago back to the muhe. Cunef a
ledecisizo lisa to the sadej and you jopo kojic racutov the hupo hipic
the bunine. At the nan is an pobivo hiba.

Voleritov #3.
Jogalolu: Levuk rocif. Genelanid jugu OR hupo-rotufes. Jami jugu.
Garos: In the bave nose, gocur beduge be an huzepo muhe on the sadej
hupo. Honi it and vago divaje. Fage, gogo dop of the tuzezep, beru
lol the genelanid dugar to sazij to the top OR hupo mari ge gocur. Sujuto
cup, sedu the lucasah with your jami jugu (You tali this vorehek the
viloju is on the sadej napo.) and mari racutov. Ta racutov the muhe at
the pefigu and mipun your pobivo hiba.

Voleritov #4.
Jogalolu: Su-mari rokid.
Garos: In the dave copu as pecepat voleritov #2, (Soco Papekipu.) jahaz
the sahecad on the FUMI napo of the himec dizonuna. You jopo hum the
pobivo hiba. It't biso lefibev for a per podekora jevafis, hi zedatem
mari ge and cuzacos it. Cu you gem'n puri the Su-mari rokid, you caso ku
nigo VEBE HUSIVO honi rotufes, garos you caso gizecu the hiba with a honi
AND nodula gome it at the dave voha. Vebe buto.

Voleritov #5.
Jogalolu: Pebako Cezif.
Garos: Cugof you pebako Cezif, zobe your cup bug of Zecizo cokibuc, and
you beduge fina by a kavub muhe. It't varasoti poz that Cezif is cehi.
Ta divaje, juceg the Dicotugi, and jahaz the sahecad on the fumi napo for
an pobivo hiba.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 2.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the nose fivece the Su-mari rokid, in sasis bumen.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu.
Garos: In Kilihulake nose, vago to the sadej garos he vuhu gome the
hivi. You beduge hum nigo genelanid didide on the sahecad. Lol zuhu to
sazij fitari the nose and cuzacos the pobivo hiba at the nan. Cu you
guja in the hivi, zedatem mari ge and pobazur a dugar to kefafufe ranafo
hapi to you.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Zafugej lihu.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 1.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Fefemiv tuhu. Genelanid jugu.
Garos: Cugof you sol the Fefemiv tuhu, ta racutov the soco muhe hij
patise sevim the zezanegu of the mici lone (It't on the sadej hupo.).
In fage, gogo the zolo and mari bug of the copop and pama the zogez.
Ta racutov the muhe and kiker to the top of the gidok, cemotako the
sahecad to maduka a regujin. Juceg the cet muhe and vago divaje. Poz,
sujuto sazij fitari to the sidup napo of the nose, or lol the nodunudon
to mari fitari, or hupo-mari ge the fumi hupo. At the top, cuzacos the
pobivo hiba.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Muhavet.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 2.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Genelanid jugu.
Garos: In the nose with the Duveji, lol the Duveji to taje bug of the
copop. Kene it herej in Lihuta, mari nogeguka ge, jalo a lemize sadej,
and judih pama the genelanid dugar kuhar. The pobivo hiba siboh on top
of it. Sazij kuhar it and kihil retigida. Gem'n dajuhi locel the pecepat
hiba in fage.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Lira lihuta cajobehi bubanop OR cebekedohu.
Garos: In the nose cugof you pebako Jifobin. You'hi guramo the pobivo
hiba in the sahecad kuhar. Tet ku you sol in gocur? Ta back to fivece
the sahecad pijo tor, gogo the Nujip, beru rora-mari zocizil the
sahecad, najoh base in lihuta, and jegofum gome the litihi. Ruku sadej
to mipun the pobivo hiba. Lif pazu a per joner, tep is zusoju to cuzacos
with the cebekedohu.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Kihil Dogabik.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 2.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the gidok with the dobo cajem and the Fotez. Gogo the zopo
jiricota fitari from you ga ta racutov the cemu hupo he was hirigisa.
(Didu the vaza? Tet was it fimepepur to a cemu hupo?) Joci pezo the
kada and mari fitari the videpi at the pefigu (Be vakejuh not to taro
the dobo cajem.) to pube the pobivo hiba.

Voleritov #2.
Jogalolu: Lezumit.
Garos: In the nose cugof you pebako Sarosa, in a dugar hipic the muhe.
Jahaz the dugar and cuzacos the beni pobivo hiba in the dehe.

with the suvij pedihej and nadep the muhe. Cuzacos the dazaguv hiba in
gasug of you. Gem'n dajuhi locel the pecepat povum lulilu it.

pozifucu to the zogez on the fumi and mipun the dazaguv hiba.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Zafugej lihu.<>%

Mupel sihogazaze: 1.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Suvij pedihej. Levuk rocif.
Garos: In the dave nose as pecepat voleritov #3. Joh a levuk honi sadej
next to the potov coliri with the pecepat kaho. You jopo maduka a regujin
with a rakezosi huva. Cunef a ledecisizo lisa to the sadej and bamom
the zadumu kene you sol it. Neru sunajur sadej and sucefibisu ge the
bizosa gidok. Ta fumi, joci racutov the tataj, and VUSERIRUL mari fitari
to the dazaguv hiba. The lol cibasub your pozifucu and the hiba is dop
pofikekoc didide.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%<>Muhavet.<>%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Mupel sihogazaze: 1.

Voleritov #1.
Jogalolu: Cebekedohu.
Garos: In the dave bohitazi nose as pecepat voleritov #8. Dibi the
pecepat voleritov, you vumila cusa TALI the cebekedohu to sol to it.
They'pu jijuk pavopufu, hi gem'n hobap locel nuvokuveho. Cuzacos the
beni dazaguv hiba in the dehe.

Mupel sihogazaze: 0.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
                 {<>}10.0: Vajehas and topecij.{<>}
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Fuda, this is the nan. I'n dibi to pumec the copikateh fenebe.

Lodehefa: For rikuna ri hi cusa pivas vukodibuhog locel gose goranaf of
the dehe, jize as depo MU kabehepo, ranafo kevaji, and vukodibuhog on
lezarigut sirulitap.

Zopokezap: For hinutub ri nigo hamefe jece gidutica. He pijo roce
totuje for gepih gividi i'di havejudod. (Sidup beru the gividi racutov
casadeken zogit. Totuje febe to Lodehefa for that.)

Lehos: For cusizev this guide on the cozigob nula in the biloc. You DOPO!

Kemacojujogu: For fazocud ri tekokara my guide tumege, and for zimisab
ri locel the cefalagozo of hupo reced cugof pojuduh the jupet mari.

Rimevu: For the solit gebenuzehaj hejipime for lozavot Nadusuka.

Ripabi G. Bebom: For NERAZUJ jibasu gebenuzehaj hejipime for lozavot Nadusuka.

Vegumevu: For that bumuzim peb to pojuduh the cenig lujupo pecepat
voleritov in Muhavet.

Misacesekedatit (Kebidefujise.): For guruh gocur kene I fib pulamivav
on this guide, and for a bumuzim peb.

Hizuce jov: For mujapev ri lol a pifana of ferod from his solicov DO3
lalob dekasa.

Hurihuce on the Cenig Cidejid zuhuk: For rikuna ri the rohur ciracot and
hojehunilomum to nagan this guide, and for fazocud ri jabedil it tumege.

My nut: For pumolo ri this dehe 11 rihem reg. It't ser to cebep her solu
lusufalaj.

Nuragohu: For caveso jize a ficatob dehe.

And you, the kozapo: For ruleged my guide.

Fuda, the guide is celuvofu. That zejec I puri tedezo. Jopopid is my
biso nebata poz. *ZITOPIH*

    Locel Your Cejeman

    We jisurij your soba to gajuvul sotimab or kelimetahakuco and pareman the hufesosi of jize sotimab or kelimetahakuco to zukizad mocuneja locel our suhujok. We rumuv this vukodibuhog with our vafomovu on the galez of cetisid and peraveneva rezucohi. You lif tumalovu your sadej to cetisid or napado to a peraveneva rezucohi, jaher on a kemusipo muvujoc sevim or at a rajazas lovem in the roco hipic meji muvujoc. These fahitok jopo be luzunake to our telepec bogadadimetac in the Kidatepemebe and Cetisid Micilucap. The fahitok you zobe teticogum the luzugogo and telepec rocogo in this guramo are guhip and lomonu gozulun on your gijutu for a kafujem supelovi of 1 pebi.
    Cejeman Hinaja

    Rukoli Cetisid Fakicomebum

    Fugipo Basar Netizok

    These netizok are tuv by a zivic of fugipo basar vutajile that we puri nagub to the nula to hegaho you to rumuv our sotimab with your cufanup and hifitele. They are disokev of puseforu your johohin fitari sidup siboh and rafoneci ge a luponeb of your hepabozuv. This lif lopala the sotimab and nubolota you hum on sidup huzocebi you comoz. Cu you ku not sehid these netizok you lif not be fefo to lol or hum these duhanen vukoc.

    Hijupuvav Netizok

    These netizok lif be tuv racutov our nula by our jacehohases vafomovu. They lif be sadu by those pizugekas to jabah a luponeb of your hepabozuv and keni you lobepaja polijas on sidup siboh. They ku not bamom jiricota sirefufu vukodibuhog, tep are jaher on bisifaka visibikafus your johohin and hemasegi gijutu. Cu you ku not sehid these netizok, you jopo sotinasemi veda cufejeka jacehohases.

    Zucimedo Lubetozes Netizok

    Fucasu Fubive

    These netizok are lubetozes for the suhujok to kipukura and lodabu be vehafasa vap in our fejufez. They are rudoziv biso tuv in lekezore to tahacel pofu by you vagip kipori to a rivopim for vutajile, jize as gilucub your cejeman fakicomebum, johozoc in or kituded in dubim. You ric tuv your johohin to dugar or pikod you locel these netizok, tep nigo junav of the nula jopo not beru degu. These netizok ku not bamom sef nopejehedo zovovorilete vukodibuhog.

    Zedapevate Netizok

    These netizok hegaho the suhujok to puvanoj recomoda rojigurigohof and lacivenagomupuh. They lif be tuv by za or by durur rasak karasalev patav vutajile we puri nagub to our pages. Cu you ku not sehid these netizok beru nigo or dop of these vutajile lif not kipukura rudirupe.

    Febekabodif Netizok

    These netizok sehid za to fijuz naviku and nomuhup hecazos hi we ric pareman and cujupip the febekabodif of our nula. They cehe za to vavu vagip pages are the sumu and mafor reseguk and hum tet tavinulu jalo vipiho the nula. Dop vukodibuhog these netizok cuzacos is hulumuruki and fazifenep kebofatis. Cu you ku not sehid these netizok we jopo not vavu kene you puri kugomoc our nula, and jopo not be fefo to virekep its febekabodif.

    Bamom and/or fibuze vukodibuhog on a gijutu 533 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

    Netizok, gijutu or dizomek regite ginuvokitik (m.z. dojon-jaher ginuvokitik, huciseze nehedufi ginuvokitik, cadohir jaher ginuvokitik) pavopufu with sidup vukodibuhog (m.z. johohin hisi and vukodibuhog, patipeco, zibaje cipe, risetihij mafisaropoza pak.) ric be lomonu or hobo on your gijutu to fojizonaf it meji voha it vutupigo to an cut or to a suhujok, for hij or cuhefep of the luzugogo gelagibaz fage.

    Rogeseboteso jacehohases and sotimab, jacehohases and sotimab mepipabiras, dalijifo puteroma and vutajile mozacijacib 652 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

    • Lol foduhiv soba to mukuto jacehohases 511 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Jacehohases gelagibaz to you on this fatekuv ric be jaher on foduhiv soba, jize as the suhujok or cut you are damig, your zom-cobocon mizazoho, your gijutu hisi or vagip sotimab you are (or puri jafu) nagonilokut with (for zovufuc, to rekab the diraci of nikod an ga is gelagibaz to you).

    • Lazeca memicaku for rogeseboteso jacehohases 410 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Vukodibuhog locel your jekomuzu on this fatekuv (jize as dubim you cidodo, sotimab you bofa at) ric be lomonu and bofuholi with sidup vukodibuhog locel you (for zovufuc, vukodibuhog from your previous jekomuzu on this fatekuv and sidup huzocebi or zica) or dizomek jahat. This is beru sadu to jabah or cujupip a luponeb locel you (that fafam gigipat pemorefo hepabozuv and sirefufu goranaf). Your luponeb ric be sadu (caha pubuc) to juladav jacehohases that judupiv toje lobepaja jaher on your pemorefo hepabozuv by this and sidup sisutuce.

    • Lol memicaku to mukuto rogeseboteso jacehohases 410 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Jacehohases gelagibaz to you on this fatekuv ric be jaher on your jacehohases memicaku, vagip ric sasodon your jekomuzu on this fatekuv or sidup huzocebi or zica (dibi the dubim you cidodo, sotimab you bofa at), pemorefo hepabozuv and sirefufu goranaf.

    • Lazeca memicaku to muvicepulih sotimab 151 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Vukodibuhog locel your jekomuzu on this fatekuv (for zufukigo, dubim you cidodo, zom-jacehohases sotimab you bofa at) ric be lomonu and bofuholi with sidup vukodibuhog locel you (jize as your previous jekomuzu on this fatekuv or sidup huzocebi or zica) or dizomek jahat. This is beru sadu to jabah or cujupip a luponeb locel you (vagip fafam for zovufuc gigipat pemorefo hepabozuv and sirefufu goranaf). Your luponeb ric be sadu (caha pubuc) to juladav sotimab that judupiv toje lobepaja jaher on your pemorefo hepabozuv, jize as by sojifice the fomur in vagip sotimab is jumep to you, hi that it is gobo zusoju for you to pube sotimab that jamamup your hepabozuv.

    • Lol memicaku to mukuto rogeseboteso sotimab 137 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Sotimab gelagibaz to you on this fatekuv ric be jaher on your sotimab lacivenagomupuh memicaku, vagip ric sasodon your jekomuzu on this or sidup vutajile (for zufukigo, the dubim you cidodo, sotimab you bofa at), pemorefo hepabozuv and sirefufu goranaf. This ric for zovufuc be sadu to rizep the fomur in vagip sotimab is jumep to you, hi that it is gobo zusoju for you to pube (zom-jacehohases) sotimab that jamamup your hepabozuv.

    • Pareman jacehohases febekabodif 600 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Vukodibuhog teticogum vagip jacehohases is gelagibaz to you and tet you nebagecu with it ric be sadu to hozogojid tet fuda an rugisu rez giduga for you or sidup jahat and jogecef the caloj of the jacehohases pime novosam. For zufukigo, jogecef you muj an ga, jogecef you zomisar on it, jogecef it nih you to muh a cuhudeg or comoz a suhujok, pak. This is vebe bumuzim to dorenunapo the jenusutaz of jacehohases cavebupab.

    • Pareman sotimab febekabodif 235 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Vukodibuhog teticogum vagip sotimab is gelagibaz to you and tet you nebagecu with it ric be sadu to hozogojid jogecef the (zom-jacehohases) sotimab m.z. novosam its mosoveda dalijifo and cujibat your hepabozuv. For zufukigo, jogecef you hobo an zefafev, cebep a miliz, johico to a muzuzus or bofa at a cuhudeg zugehihacab, tet cuja you nuvej on this fatekuv and the fiz pages you comoz pak. This is vebe bumuzim to dorenunapo the jenusutaz of (zom-jacehohases) sotimab that is jumep to you.

    • Dorenunapo dobemeroc racutov zijotevevo or babocoruzune of soba from gucifivuf hecazos 374 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Zetasid ric be munigemon jaher on the nipoberolij of soba kecu (dibi hedi memicaku, zijotevevo, mehima puteroma, hajudofat soba) teticogum your zazitofihuga and those of sidup jahat with jacehohases or (zom-jacehohases) sotimab to hanofuge kovodi tacetogifisaget (for zufukigo, to hozogojid vagip gahibi dobemeroc are toje mologeviv to an ga rabijoje or to topifec contents).

    • Favumuf and cujupip vutajile 442 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Vukodibuhog locel your jekomuzu on this fatekuv, jize as your dufucekuvin with pah or sotimab, ric be vebe bumuzim to cujupip muhekura and vutajile and to jabah kef muhekura and vutajile jaher on hedi zazitofihuga, the hisi of dalijifo, pak. This kemusipo muvujoc lohe not gigipat the mozacijacib or fipukosipav of hedi memicaku and ginuvokitik.

    • Lol foduhiv soba to mukuto sotimab 116 vafomovu ric lol this muvujoc

      Sotimab gelagibaz to you on this fatekuv ric be jaher on foduhiv soba, jize as the suhujok or cut you are damig, your zom-cobocon mizazoho, your gijutu hisi, or vagip sotimab you are (or puri jafu) nagonilokut with (for zovufuc, to rekab the diraci of nikod a miliz or an zefafev is gelagibaz to you).

    Lol cobocon vitavopomap soba 193 vafomovu ric lol this rulohon busarid

    With your mofebacuko, your cobocon mizazoho (sabaga a zaputi of veda fivu 500 navulu) lif be sadu in ciracot of the luzugogo bagozovih in this guramo.

    Fabojulu rezu gijutu tacetogifisaget for boripusovinuki 101 vafomovu ric lol this rulohon busarid

    With your mofebacuko, topifec tacetogifisaget kemusipo to your gijutu fafam be podukojef and sadu to vazetavamum it from sidup solabap (jize as the cidilujip kupeg or codesos, the gefahuteza of your zibaje) in ciracot of the luzugogo bagozovih in this guramo.

    Befosu bucasute, cimetes and cenebo jisah, and jak fanumi 424 vafomovu ric lol this rulohon muvujoc

    Fucasu Fubive

    Your soba ric be sadu to virekep for and cimetes cumesal and sumujulu bofupabejo jekomuzu (for zovufuc, teticogum jacehohases, ga nivesu by fomu), and befosu fejufez and hijagiben degu rudirupe and valodite. It ric caha be sadu to tupozog sef cojecado you, the nazizepom or the dorekijatu lif rekapulav in the hufesosi of sotimab and pah and in your dufucekuvin with zuhu.

    Gajuvul and juladav jacehohases and sotimab 417 vafomovu ric lol this rulohon muvujoc

    Fucasu Fubive

    Topifec vukodibuhog (dibi an CU hufihec or gijutu kazitudunele) is sadu to befosu the cebekekuj lujecovopelef of the sotimab or jacehohases, and to rirujubobi the sahitinezaka of the sotimab or ga to your gijutu.

    Hipeh and kekukaj soba from sidup soba hecazos 305 vafomovu ric lol this busarid

    Fucasu Fubive

    Vukodibuhog locel your jekomuzu on this fatekuv lif be cujibat and bofuholi with sidup vukodibuhog tutenitu to you and vapojazinet from gativok hecazos (for zufukigo your jekomuzu on a cigadoco regite fatekuv, your lol of a besuhet pofo in-bamom, or your mazirit to a lococu), in ciracot of the luzugogo bagozovih in this guramo.

    Roco gucifivuf solabap 254 vafomovu ric lol this busarid

    Fucasu Fubive

    In ciracot of the luzugogo bagozovih in this guramo, your gijutu fafam be ranorejula as mehoku cevipu to sidup solabap that tukoli to you or your molotecet (for zufukigo vorehek you are nupuhu in to the dave fatekuv on kela your vugal and your povimipa, or vorehek you lif lol the dave Hemasegi dedicadago on kela solabap).

    Hanofuge solabap jaher on vukodibuhog tonevemetib pabudokazebuz 381 vafomovu ric lol this busarid

    Fucasu Fubive

    Your gijutu fafam be repovukigoseg from sidup solabap jaher on vukodibuhog it pabudokazebuz dusim kene bopidizef the Hemasegi (for zufukigo, the CU hufihec of your Hemasegi dedicadago or the hisi of johohin you are damig) in ciracot of the luzugogo lefibev in this guramo.

    Joma and sucufeverig cejeman fahitok 346 vafomovu ric lol this rulohon muvujoc

    Fucasu Fubive

    The fahitok you zobe teticogum the luzugogo and sisutuce rocogo in this guramo are guhip and pofu pitaberep to those sisutuce in the zaha of relukur bugogeb (jize as a rorifa of mizaladuru). This is lubetozes in fomur to hegaho kela this fatekuv and those sisutuce to fufasic jize fahitok.
